# Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot

ברטנורא על משנה בכורות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Kodashim

## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:1)


**הַלּוֹקֵחַ עֻבַּר חֲמוֹרוֹ שֶׁל נָכְרִי.** כְּשֶׁהוּא בִמְעֵי אִמּוֹ וּבְכוֹר הוּא. וּלְהָכִי נָקַט עֻבַּר חֲמוֹרוֹ, שֶׁאֵין לְךָ בְהֵמָה טְמֵאָה קְדוֹשָׁה בִּבְכוֹרָה אֶלָּא פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר בִּלְבַד, וְאַיְדֵי דְזוּטְרָן מִלֵּיהּ דְּפֶטֶר חֲמוֹר, פָּסֵיק וְשָׁרֵי לֵיהּ. וְכָל הָנָךְ פִּרְקֵי אַחֲרִינֵי מַיְרֵי בִּבְכוֹר בְּהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה:


הלוקח עובר חמורו של נכרי – when it is in the womb of its mother and it is a firstling (i.e., male or female). And for this reason, it (i.e., the Mishnah) took [the words], “the fetus of his donkey,” for there is no holy unclean animal with regard to its first-born other than the firstborn of a donkey alone, but since the words are few of the firstborn of the donkey, he decided and permitted it. But all of these other chapters speak of a first born of a pure animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי.** לִמְכֹּר לְנָכְרִי בְּהֵמָה גַסָּה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית בָּהּ מְלָאכָה בְּשַׁבָּת:


אף על פי שאינו רשאי – to sell to a heathen a large animal, because of work on Shabbat that would be performed by it (see Tractate Avodah Zarah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 6 – where the Halakha is established that it is forbidden to sell a heathen a large animal – originally owned by an Israelite).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:3)


**וְהַמִּשְׁתַּתֵּף לוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ חֵלֶק הַנָּכְרִי מְסֻיָּם וְיָדוּעַ, כְּגוֹן יָדוֹ אוֹ רַגְלוֹ שֶׁל בְּכוֹר, אוֹ שֶׁל אִמּוֹ. כָּל זְמַן שֶׁאִם יַחְתֹּךְ מִמֶּנּוּ אוֹתוֹ אֵבָר תִּשָּׁאֵר הַבְּהֵמָה בַּעֲלַת מוּם הֲרֵי זֶה שֻׁתְּפוּת נָכְרִי וּפָטוּר:


והמשתתף לו – even if the portion of the heathen is substantial and known, as for example, the [yet unborn] firstborn’s arm or leg, or that of its mother. Any time that if they would sever from it that limb, the animal remains with a blemish – this is the partnership of the heathen and it is exempt [from redemption].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:4)


**וְהַמְקַבֵּל מִמֶּנּוּ.** שֶׁהַחֲמוֹרָה שֶׁל נָכְרִי וְיִשְׂרָאֵל מְקַבְּלָהּ וּמְטַפֵּל בָּהּ כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהוּ חוֹלְקִין בַּוְּלָדוֹת, אֲבָל גּוּף הַבְּהֵמָה שֶׁל נָכְרִי:


והמקבל ממנו – that the female donkey belongs to the heathen that an Israelite receives and takes care of it in order that they will divide its offspring, but the body of the animal belongs to the heathen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:5)


**פָּטוּר מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה.** אוֹתוֹ עֻבָּר שֶׁקָּנָה מִן הַנָּכְרִי אוֹ שֶׁמָּכַר הוּא לַנָּכְרִי אֵינוֹ קָדוֹשׁ, הוֹאִיל וְיֵשׁ לַנָּכְרִי חֵלֶק בּוֹ אוֹ בְאִמּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר ג) הִקְדַּשְׁתִּי לִי כָל בְּכוֹר בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, וְלֹא בְנָכְרִי:


פטור מן הבכורה – that fetus that he (i.e., the Israelite) purchased from the heathen or that he sold to the heathen is not holy, for since the heathen has a portion in it or in its mother, as it states (Numbers 3:13): “I consecrated every first-born in Israel, [man and beast, to Myself, to be Mine],” but not of the heathen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:1:6)


**אִם פָּטְרוּ אֶת שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּמִּדְבָּר.** הַלְוִיִּם לֹא פָּטְרוּ בְּהֶמְתָּן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּמִּדְבָּר, דְּפֶטֶר בְּהֵמָה, בְּהֵמָה פָּטְרָה, כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) וְאֶת בֶּהֱמַת הַלְוִיִּם תַּחַת בְּהֶמְתָּם. אֶלָּא הָכִי קָאָמַר, הַלְוִיִּם הִפְקִיעוּ קְדֻשַּׁת בְּכוֹרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּמִּדְבָּר, כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) קַח אֶת הַלְוִיִּם תַּחַת כָּל בְּכוֹר כוּ', וְאִם הִפְקִיעָה קְדֻשַּׁת הַלְוִיִּם אֶת קְדֻשָּׁתָן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, דִּין הוּא שֶׁתַּפְקִיעַ אֶת שֶׁל עַצְמָן, וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁלָּמַדְנוּ שֶׁהֻפְקְעָה קְדֻשַּׁת בְּכוֹרָה מֵעַצְמָן שֶׁל לְוִיִּם, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁפִּטְרֵי חֲמוֹרֵיהֶן פְּטוּרִין מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה, דִּכְתִיב (שם יח) אַךְ פָּדֹה תִפְדֶּה אֵת בְּכוֹר הָאָדָם וְאֵת בְּכוֹר הַבְּהֵמָה הַטְּמֵאָה תִּפְדֶּה, כָּל שֶׁיֶּשְׁנוֹ בִּבְכוֹר אָדָם יֶשְׁנוֹ בִּבְכוֹר בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה, וְכָל שֶׁאֵינוֹ בִּבְכוֹר אָדָם אֵינוֹ בִּבְכוֹר בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה. אֲבָל מִבְּכוֹר בְּהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה לֹא נִפְטְרוּ כֹהֲנִים וּלְוִיִּם, כְּדִלְקַמָּן:


אם פטרו את של ישראל במדבר – the Levites did not exempt the animals of the Israel in the wilderness, that the first-born of the cattle, [the] cattle [of the Levites] replaced [and redeemed] it, as it is written (Numbers 3: 45): “and the cattle of the Levites in place of their cattle,” but rather, this is how it should be read: The Levities released/served as ransom the holiness of the first born of the Israelites in the wilderness, as it is written (Numbers 3:45): “Take the Levites in place of all the first-born among the Israelite people, [and the cattle of the Levites in place of their cattle; and the Levites shall be Mine, the LORD’s],” but if the sanctity of the Levites released/ransomed the sanctity of the Israelites, it is a law that they would release/ransom themselves. But after we learned that the sanctity of the first-born was released/ransomed from the livestock of the Levities, we learned that the released/ransomed of their donkeys are exempt from [the rules governing] the first-born, as it is written (Numbers 18:15): “but you shall have the first-born of man redeemed, and you shall also have the firstling of impure animals redeemed,” all that the first-born of man has, the first-born of impure animals have, and all that the first-born of man doesn’t have, is not found in the first-born of impure animals. But from the first born of pure animals, the Priests and Levites were not exempt, as we learn ahead.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:1)


**פָּרָה שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְמִין חֲמוֹר וַחֲמוֹר שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְמִין סוּס פָּטוּר.** אִי תָנָא פָּרָה שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְּמִין חֲמוֹר וְלֹא תָנָא חֲמוֹר שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְּמִין סוּס, הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא פָּרָה שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְּמִין חֲמוֹר הוּא דְּפָטוּר מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא דָמוּ אַהֲדָדֵי כְלָל, דְּהָא יֵשׁ לָהּ קַרְנַיִם וְהָא אֵין לָהּ קַרְנַיִם, הָא פַּרְסוֹתֶיהָ סְדוּקוֹת וְהָא פַּרְסוֹתֶיהָ קְלוּטוֹת. אֲבָל חֲמוֹר שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְמִין סוּס, אֵימָא חֲמוֹר אָדֹם הוּא וְלִחַיַּב. וְאִי תָנָא סֵיפָא וְלֹא תָנָא רֵישָׁא, הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא חֲמוֹר שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְמִין סוּס הוּא דְּפָטוּר מִשּׁוּם דְּסוּס לֹא קָדוֹשׁ בַּבְּכוֹרָה, אֲבָל פָּרָה שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְּמִין חֲמוֹר דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ קַדְּשֵׁי בַּבְּכוֹרָה אֵימָא לִיחַיַּב, צְרִיכֵי:


פרה שילדה כמין חמור וחמור שילדה כמין סוס פטור (a cow that gave birth to a donkey of so) – if [the Mishnah] taught [only] “a cow that gave birth to a donkey of sorts” but did not teach “a donkey that gave birth to a horse of sorts,” I might say that the cow that gave birth to a donkey of sorts is that which is exempt from [their offspring being counted] as a firstborn, because they are not at all similar to each other, for this one (i.e., the cow) has horns and that one (i.e., the donkey) does not have horns, for this one (i.e., the cow), its hoofs are cloven but that one (i.e., the donkey), its hoofs are closed, but a donkey which gave birth to a horse of sorts, I might say that that it (i.e., the horse of sorts) is a red donkey and it is obligated [for its offspring to be counted as a firstborn]. But if it (i.e., the Mishnah) had only taught the concluding clause and did not teach the opening clause, I would say that the donkey that gave birth to a horse of sorts is exempt from [its offspring being counted as a firstborn] because a horse is not sanctified with a firstborn, but a cow that gave birth to a donkey of sorts where both of them are sanctified through the firstborn, I might say that it (i.e., the donkey of sorts) is obligated [for its offspring to be counted as a firstborn. Therefore, both phrases are necessary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:2)


**פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר שְׁנֵי פְעָמִים.** חַד בְּקַדֶּשׁ לִּי כָל בְּכוֹר, וְחַד בִּרְאֵה אַתָּה אוֹמֵר אֵלַי:


פטר חמור פטר חמור שני פעמים – one in “Sanctify to me all firstborns” (Exodus 13:12) and the other in (the beginning of the Torah reading for Shabbat Hol HaMoed Passover and Sukkot – beginning with Exodus 33:12 and extending to Exodus 34:26) “See you say to me,” (Exodus 34:20).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:3)


**מַהוּ בַאֲכִילָה.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּהֵמָה שֶׁאֵין הַיּוֹלֶדֶת דּוֹמָה לַנּוֹלָד, מַהוּ בַאֲכִילָה:


מהו באכילה – that is to say, an animal where the birth mother is not similar to the animal born- what is the Halakha with regard to their consumption/eating?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:4)


**שֶׁהַיּוֹצֵא מֵהַטָּמֵא טָמֵא.** וּדְבַשׁ דְּבוֹרִים וּצְרָעִין מֻתָּר, וְלֹא מִקְּרֵי יוֹצֵא מִן הַטָּמֵא, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין מְמַצּוֹת אוֹתוֹ מִבְּשָׂרָן אֶלָּא מַכְנִיסוֹת אוֹתוֹ לְגוּפָן, שֶׁאוֹכְלוֹת מִפִּרְחֵי הָאִילָנוֹת וּמֵהֶן נַעֲשֶׂה הַדְּבַשׁ. וַחֲלֵב הָאָדָם מֻתָּר כְּשֶׁפֵּרַשׁ, אֲבָל לִינֹק מִשְּׁדֵי הָאִשָּׁה אָסוּר. וְכָל שְׁאָר חָלָב שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה וְחַיָּה טְמֵאָה, הָוֵי יוֹצֵא מִן הַטָּמֵא וְאָסוּר:


שהיוצא מן הטמא טמא – and the honey of bees and hornets. It is permitted and is not called that which comes out from the impure, because it does not squeeze/wring it out from their flesh but rather, they bring it into their bodies, since they consume from the flours of the trees and from them the honey is made. But the milk of a human is permitted as I explained, but to suck from the breasts of a woman is prohibited. And all the rest of the milk of impure animals and wildlife, they come from that which is impure and is forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:2:5)


**דָּג טָמֵא שֶׁבָּלַע דָּג טָהוֹר.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא חָזִינַן לֵיהּ שֶׁבָּלַע, כֵּיוָן דְּרֹב דָּגִים מַשְׁרִיצִים בְּמִינָן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁבָּלַע בְּפָנֵינוּ דָּמֵי:


דג טמא שבלע דג טהור – even though we did not see it that it swallowed it, for since most fish breed with their own kind, it is like it swallowed in our presence.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:1)


**נוֹתֵן טָלֶה אֶחָד לַכֹּהֵן.** בְּפִדְיוֹן. דְּמִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ חַד מִנַּיְהוּ בְּכוֹר:


נותן טלה אחד לכהן – in redemption. For whichever way you look at it, one of them is a firstborn.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:2)


**זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה.** וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אִם יָצָא זָכָר תְּחִלָּה וְהוּא בְכוֹר, אוֹ נְקֵבָה יָצְאָה תְחִלָּה וְאֵין כָּאן דִּין בְּכוֹרָה:


זכר ונקבה - and it is not known if the male came out first and he is the firstborn, or the female came out first, and there isn’t here the law of the firstborn.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:3)


**מַפְרִישׁ טָלֶה.** מִשּׁוּם סָפֵק, וּמַפְקִיעַ עָלָיו קְדֻשַּׁת הַפֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר:


מפריש טלה – because of doubt, and he releases from him the holiness of the redemption of the donkey.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:4)


**וְהוּא לְעַצְמוֹ.** שֶׁהוּא עַצְמוֹ יֹאכַל הַטָּלֶה וְלֹא יִתְּנֶנּוּ לַכֹּהֵן, דְּכֹהֵן הָוֵי מוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ, וְעָלָיו הָרְאָיָה לְהָבִיא עֵדִים שֶׁהַזָּכָר יָצָא תְחִלָּה. וְאִסּוּרָא לֵיכָּא, דַּאֲפִלּוּ פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר גָּמוּר שֶׁפְּדָאוֹ בְשֶׂה שׁוּב אֵין קְדֻשָּׁה לֹא בוֹ וְלֹא בְּפִדְיוֹנוֹ, אֶלָּא גָזֵל הוּא דְּאִיכָּא אִם אֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן טָלֶה לַכֹּהֵן, דְּמָמוֹנוֹ הוּא, וְהָכָא לֵיכָּא גָּזֵל דְּסָפֵק הוּא:


והוא לעצמו- that he himself will consume the lamb and he will not give it to the Kohen, for the Kohen is like someone who removes something from his fellow, and the proof is upon him to bring witnesses that the male came out first. But there is no prohibition, for even the firstling of a complete donkey that was redeemed with a young sheep, furthermore there is no sanctity neither with him or that which he redeems it with. But rather where there is theft, if he does not give a lamb to the Kohen, that is his (i.e., the Kohen’s) money, but here there is no theft for there is a doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:5)


**שְׁתֵּי חֲמוֹרוֹת וְיָלְדוּ וְכוּ'.** זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה, נוֹתֵן טָלֶה אֶחָד לַכֹּהֵן בִּשְׁבִיל הַזָּכָר. אוֹ שְׁנֵי זְכָרִים וּנְקֵבָה, דַּחֲדָא יָלְדָה זָכָר וַחֲדָא יָלְדָה זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה אוֹ אִי נַמִּי חֲדָא יָלְדָה שְׁנֵי זְכָרִים וְאַחַת יָלְדָה נְקֵבָה, נוֹתְנִים טָלֶה אֶחָד לַכֹּהֵן, דְּהָא חַד זָכָר אִיכָּא וַדַּאי בְּכוֹר, וְאַאִידָךְ דַּהֲוֵי סָפֵק דְּשֶׁמָּא הַנְּקֵבָה יָצְאָה תְחִלָּה מַפְרִישׁ טָלֶה וְהוּא לְעַצְמוֹ:


שתי חמורות וילדו כו' – a male and female, he gives one lamb to a Kohen because of the male [firstborn]. Or, two males and one female, that one gave birth to a male and one gave birth to a male and female, or alternatively, one gave birth to two males and one gave birth to a female, we give one lamb to the Kohen for certainly there is one male that is definitely a firstborn, but regarding the other where there is a doubt lest the female was born first, we set aside a lamb and it is for himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:3:6)


**שְׁנֵי זְכָרִים וּשְׁתֵּי נְקֵבוֹת.** סָפֵק הֵן, דְּשֶׁמָּא כָּל חֲדָא יָלְדָה זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה, וְשֶׁמָּא הַנְּקֵבוֹת יָצְאוּ תְחִלָּה, הִלְכָּךְ אֵין לַכֹּהֵן כָּאן כְּלוּם, אֶלָּא מַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶם שְׁנֵי טְלָאִים לְאַפְקוֹעֵי לְאִסּוּרַיְהוּ וְהֵן לְעַצְמָן, כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתַי. אֲבָל רַמְבַּ"ם סָבַר דְּאֵין כָּאן לַכֹּהֵן כְּלוּם וַאֲפִלּוּ טָלֶה לְעַצְמוֹ אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהַפְרִישׁ, מִשּׁוּם דְּאִיכָּא בְכָל חַד מֵהָנָךְ סְפֵקֵי טוּבָא כְּשֶׁיָּלְדוּ שְׁתֵּי נְקֵבוֹת וְזָכָר, שֶׁמָּא אַחַת מִשְּׁתֵּיהֶן יָלְדָה שְׁתֵּי נְקֵבוֹת וְהָאַחֶרֶת זָכָר, אוֹ שֶׁמָּא זוֹ יָלְדָה נְקֵבָה וְהָאַחֶרֶת זָכָר וְאַחֲרָיו נְקֵבָה, אוֹ נְקֵבָה וְאַחֲרֶיהָ זָכָר. וְכֵן סְפֵקוֹת הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בִּשְׁנֵי זְכָרִים וּשְׁתֵּי נְקֵבוֹת, הִלְכָּךְ אֲפִלּוּ טָלֶה לְעַצְמוֹ אֵינוֹ מַפְרִישׁ. וּפֵרוּשׁ רַבּוֹתַי עִקָּר:


שני זכרים ושני נקבות – they are of doubt, for perhaps each one of them gave birth to a male and a female, and perhaps the females came out first, therefore, the Kohen does not have anything here, but he designates for them two lambs to release them from their sin and they are for themselves (i.e., the owners), such My teachers explained. But Maimonides holds that there is nothing for the Kohen her and even a lamb for himself he does not have to separate/dedicate, because there is in each one [of these donkeys] a great down when they gave birth to two females and one male, for perhaps one of the two of them gave birth to two females and the other gave birth to a male. Or perhaps, this one gave birth to a female, and the other [gave birth to] a male and afterwards a female, or a female and afterwards a male. mal And similarly there are many doubts with [offspring of] two males and two females, therefore even one lamb for himself he does not designate/separate, and the explanation of my Rabbis is the essential principle.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:1)


**זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה מַפְרִישׁ טָלֶה אֶחָד לְעַצְמוֹ.** דְּשֶׁמָּא אוֹתָהּ שֶׁלֹּא בִכְּרָה יָלְדָה אֶת הַנְּקֵבָה:


זכר ונקבה מפריש טלה אחד לעצמו – for perhaps that one (i.e., donkey) that did not give birth gave birth to a female.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וּפֶטֶר חֲמֹר תִּפְדֶּה בְשֶׂה.** כָּל שֶׂה בְּמַשְׁמַע, שֵׂה כְבָשִׂים אוֹ שֵׂה עִזִּים, שֶׂה זָכָר אוֹ שֶׂה נְקֵבָה וְכוּ':


שנאמר ופטר חמור תפדה בשה – every young lamb by implication, young lamb of lambs or young goats, a young male or a young female lamb, etc.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:3)


**וּפוֹדֶה בוֹ פְעָמִים הַרְבֵּה.** אִם חָזַר כֹּהֵן וּנְתָנוֹ לוֹ:


ופודה בו פעמים רבות - if the Kohen returned and they gave him [the redemption animal].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:4)


**נִכְנָס לַדִּיר לְהִתְעַשֵּׂר.** כְּגוֹן יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ עֲשָׂרָה סְפֵק פִּטְרֵי חֲמוֹרִים וְהִפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶן עֲשָׂרָה שֵׂיִין וְהֵן שֶׁלּוֹ, הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ נִכְנָסִים לַדִּיר לַמָּקוֹם שֶׁמַּכְנִיסִים שָׁם הַבְּהֵמוֹת לְהִתְעַשֵּׂר, וּמְעַשְּׂרָן כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁמְּעַשְּׂרִים שְׁאָר בְּהֵמוֹת. וְהוּא הַדִּין שֶׁאִם הָיָה לוֹ סְפֵק פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר אֶחָד אוֹ שְׁנַיִם [וְהִפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶם שֶׂה אֶחָד אוֹ שְׁנַיִם], שֶׁנִּכְנָס לַדִּיר עִם שְׁאָר כְּבָשָׂיו לְהִתְעַשֵּׂר:


נכנס לדיר להתעשר – as for example, an Israelite who had ten donkeys who were doubtful first issues of the womb and he separated/dedicated ten lambs and they are his, behold they enter the pen to a place where they bring in animals there to be tithed, and we tithe them in the manner that we tithe the rest of the animals, and the same law applies that if he had a doubt regarding one first-born donkey or two [and he separated/dedicated for them one or two young sheep] that it would enter the pen with the rest of his sheep to be tithed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:4:5)


**וְאִם מֵת נֶהֱנִים בּוֹ.** שֶׂה שֶׁהִפְרִישׁוֹ לְפִדְיוֹן פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר, אִם מֵת בְּיַד בְּעָלִים קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּבֹא לְיַד כֹּהֵן, נֶהֱנֶה בוֹ הַכֹּהֵן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ לְיָדוֹ מֵחַיִּים, דְּמֵעִדָּנָא דְּאַפְרְשֵׁיהּ בִּרְשׁוּתָא דְכֹהֵן קָאֵי:


ואם מת נהנים בו – what he separated/dedicated for redemption of the first-born donkey, if it died at the hand of the owners prior to coming into the hand of the Kohen, the Kohen derives benefit from even though it didn’t reach his hand while living, for from the time that he (i.e., the owner) separated it, it was in the domain of the Kohen.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:1)


**וְלֹא בְחַיָּה.** צְבִי וְאַיָּל:


ולא בחיה – a deer or a ram.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:2)


**וְלֹא בִשְׁחוּטָה.** בְּשֶׂה שָׁחוּט:


ולא בשחוטה – a slaughtered young sheep/goat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:3)


**וְלֹא בִטְרֵפָה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנֶחְתְּכוּ רַגְלֶיהָ מִן הָאַרְכּוּבָה וּלְמַעְלָה:


ולא בטריפה – as for example, that its feet were cut off from the knee and above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:4)


**כִּלְאַיִם.** תַּיִשׁ הַבָּא עַל רָחֵל:


כלאים – a he-goat that comes upon a ewe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:5)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא שֶׂה.** דְּבֵין רָחֵל בֵּין עֵז קְרוּיִין שֶׂה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


מפני שהוא שה – whether a ewe or whether a goat, they are called a young goat/young sheep. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:6)


**כּוֹי.** בְּרִיָּה בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ הִיא, סָפֵק חַיָּה סָפֵק בְּהֵמָה:


כוי – it is a creature of its own, doubtfully wildlife and doubtfully an animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:7)


**נְתָנוֹ לַכֹּהֵן.** הַפֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר עַצְמוֹ:


נתנו לכהן- the actual first-born donkey.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:5:8)


**עַד שֶׁיַּפְרִישׁ שֶׂה תַחְתָּיו.** וְהוּא לְעַצְמוֹ. וְאַחַר כָּךְ יִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בַּפֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר. וּלְפִי שֶׁהַכֹּהֲנִים חֲשׁוּדִים לְעִנְיָן זֶה שֶׁלֹּא לְהַפְרִישׁ שֶׂה תַּחְתָּיו, הִלְכָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל הַנּוֹתֵן פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר לַכֹּהֵן אֵינוֹ זָז מִשָּׁם עַד שֶׁיִּפְדֶּנּוּ הַכֹּהֵן בְּפָנָיו:


עד שיפריש שה תחתיו- and this for himself. But afterwards he can use the first-born donkey. But because the Kohanim were suspected in this matter of not separating a young sheep in its place, therefore, an Israelite who gives a first-born donkey to a Kohen does not move from there until the Kohen redeems it in his presence.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:1)


**כְּחָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים שֶׁל פִּדְיוֹן הַבֵּן.** דְּחַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ:


בחמש סלעים של פדיון הבן – for they are liable for it (i.e., the five Selas for the redemption price of the first-born male).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:2)


**כְּפִדְיוֹן מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי.** דְּאִם אָבַד אֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ. דְּהַהִיא כֶּסֶף חַיְּבֵיהּ רַחֲמָנָא לֶאֱכֹל בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם וְהָא אֲזַל לֵיהּ. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ שֶׁהִקִּישׁ הַכָּתוּב פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר לִבְכוֹר אָדָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וּפֶטֶר חֲמוֹר תִּפְדֶּה בְשֶׂה וְגוֹ'‏ וְכָל בְּכוֹר בָּנֶיךָ תִּפְדֶּה. וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אַךְ פָּדֹה תִפְדֶּה אֶת בְּכוֹר הָאָדָם וְאֶת בְּכוֹר הַבְּהֵמָה הַטְּמֵאָה תִּפְדֶּה, לִפְדִיָּה הִקַּשְׁתִּיו וְלֹא לְדָבָר אַחֵר:


כפדיון מעשר שני – that if he lost it, he is not liable for it (i.e., to replace the redemption of the Second Tithe). For that money, the All-Merciful made him liable to eat in Jerusalem and it left him. And the reason of Rabbi Eliezer is that we found that Scripture made an analogy between the first-born of the donkey and the first born of a human, as it states (Exodus 34:20): “But the firstling of an ass you shall redeem with a sheep; [if you do not redeem it, you must break its neck.] And you must redeem every first-born among your sons.” But the Sages state (Numbers 18:15): “But you shall have the fist-born of man redeemed, and you shall also have the firstling of unclean animals redeemed.” For redemption, the analogy was made but not for another thing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:3)


**שֶׁאֵין כָּאן לַכֹּהֵן כְּלוּם.** שֶׁאֵין חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּתוֹ, כְּרַבָּנָן. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


ואין כאן לכהן כלום – and he (i.e., the Kohen) is not liable for it, according to the Rabbis. And such is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:4)


**מֵת פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר.** לְאַחַר שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ הַפִּדְיוֹן, וַעֲדַיִן לֹא נְתָנוֹ לַכֹּהֵן:


מת פטר חמור – after he (i.e., the owner) set aside the redemption, but he had not as yet given it to the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יִקָּבֵר.** דְּהוֹאִיל וְהוּא חַיָּב בְּאַחֲרָיוּת הַטָּלֶה, כְּמַאן דְּלֹא אַפְרְשֵׁיהּ דָּמֵי:


רבי אליעזר אומר יקבר – for since he is liable for the lamb, it is like someone who had not separated it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:6)


**וּמֻתָּר.** יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּהֲנָאָתוֹ שֶׁל טָלֶה כְּמִי שֶׁלֹּא הִפְרִישׁוֹ:


ומותר – an Israelite [is permitted] in deriving benefit from the lamb, for it is like someone who did not separate it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:6:7)


**אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהִקָּבֵר.** דְּמֵעִדָּנָא דְּאַפְרְשֵׁיהּ לַטָּלֶה קָם לֵיהּ בִּרְשׁוּת כֹּהֵן וְהֻפְקְעָה קְדֻשָּׁתוֹ שֶׁל פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


אין צריך להקבר – that from the time that I will separate the lamb, it becomes the property of the Kohen and the sanctity of the firstborn ass is released.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:1)


**קוֹפִיץ.** סַכִּין גָּדוֹל כְּמִין גַּרְזֶן קָטָן שֶׁהַקַּצָּבִים מְחַתְּכִים בּוֹ בָּשָׂר:


קופיץ (hatchet) – a large knife like a small ax that the butchers cut with it the meat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:2)


**וְעוֹרְפוֹ.** הַיְנוּ שֶׁחוֹתֵךְ רֹאשׁוֹ מִמוּל עָרְפּוֹ:


ועורפו – that is that he cuts off its head from the border of the neck.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:3)


**וְקוֹבְרוֹ.** לְפִי שֶׁאָסוּר בַּהֲנָאָה לְאַחַר עֲרִיפָה:


וקוברו – because it is prohibited to derive benefit after breaking the neck [of the heifer].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:4)


**מִצְוַת יְעִידָה.** בָּאָמָה הָעִבְרִיָּה:


מצות יעידה – regarding a Hebrew maidservant.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:5)


**בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁהָיוּ מִתְכַּוְּנִים לְשֵׁם מִצְוָה.** וְהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים הַמְיַבֵּם לְשֵׁם נוֹי לְשֵׁם מָמוֹן הָוֵי פּוֹגֵעַ בָּעֶרְוָה שֶׁלֹּא בִמְקוֹם מִצְוָה. וְאֵין כֵּן הֲלָכָה, אֶלָּא הוֹאִיל וּפָקַע אִסּוּר אֵשֶׁת אָח מִנַּהּ כְּשֶׁמֵּת אָחִיו בְּלֹא בָנִים הֲרֵי הֻתְּרָה לוֹ לְגַמְרֵי וַאֲפִלּוּ יִבְּמָהּ לְשֵׁם נוֹי וּלְשֵׁם מָמוֹן:


בראשונה שהיו מתכוונים לשם מצוה – and they (i.e., the Sages) would say the dead husband’s brother (brother-in-law who marries his wife) , for the sake of beauty, for the sake of money, they would come in contact with illicit sexual behavior that was not in the place [of the fulfillment] of the commandment [of levirate marriage]. But this is not the Halakha, but rather since the prohibition of marrying the wife of one’s brother has no effect from her, when his brother died without children, behold, she is completely permitted to him, and even a sister-in-law (i.e., widow of a brother who died without issue) for the sake of beauty or for the sake of money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 1:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/1:7:6)


**מִצְוַת גְּאֻלָּה בָּאָדוֹן.** הַמַּתְפִּיס בְּהֵמָה לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת, מִצְוַת פְּדִיָּתָהּ בָּאָדוֹן קֹדֶם לְכָל אָדָם, שֶׁהוּא מוֹסִיף אֶת הַחֹמֶשׁ:


מצות גאולה באדון – who decides by lottery the donation of an animal to the Temple treasury, the commandment of its redemption is by the owner prior to all individuals, because he adds the one-fifth.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:1:1)


**הַלּוֹקֵחַ עֻבַּר פָּרָתוֹ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי. לִמְכֹּר לוֹ בְּהֵמָה גַסָּה:


הלוקח עובר פרתו. אע"פ שאינו רשאין – to sell him a large animal (see Tractate Bekhorot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 and Tractate Avodah Zarah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 6).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:2:1)


**כָּל הַקֳּדָשִׁים שֶׁקָּדַם מוּם קָבוּעַ לְהֶקְדֵּשָׁן כוּ'.** וְכֹל שֶׁקָּדַם הֶקְדֵּשָׁן אֶת מוּמָן. הָנָךְ תַּרְתֵּי בָּבֵי פֵּרַשְׁנוּ לְעֵיל בְּמַסֶּכֶת חֻלִּין:


כל הקדשים שקדם מום קבוע להקדשן כו'. וכל שקדם הקדשן את מומן – these two clauses/sections, we explained above in Tractate Hullin [Chapter 10, Mishnah 2 and also Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 9, Mishnah 3].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:1)


**הַמְקַבֵּל צֹאן בַּרְזֶל מִן הַנָּכְרִי.** שֶׁשָּׁם לוֹ הַנָּכְרִי בְּהֵמוֹתָיו בְּדָמִים קְצוּבִים וְלָתֵת לוֹ אוֹתָן דָּמִים עַד עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים בֵּין מֵתוּ בֵּין הוּזְלוּ, וְאוֹתָן וְלָדוֹת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לָהֶן עַד אוֹתוֹ זְמַן יְהוּ בֵינֵיהֶן:


המקבל צאן ברזל מן הנכרי (he receives a guaranteed investment from the heathen – which is secure from any loss) – that the heathen placed his cattle to him (i.e., an Israelite) for fixed compensation and to give him those monies for up to ten years, whether they (i.e., the animals) died or lost value, and those offspring that they will have until that time will be [divided] between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:2)


**וְלָדוֹת פְּטוּרִין.** מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה. וַלְדוֹתֵיהֶן שֶׁל אוֹתָן צֹאן כְּשֶׁיִּגְדְּלוּ וִיבַכְּרוּ יִפָּטְרוּ מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה. דְּאִי לֹא יָהֵיב זוּזֵי לַנָּכְרִי תָּפֵיס בְּהֵמָה, וְאִי לֹא מַשְׁכַּח בְּהֵמָה תָּפֵיס וְלָדוֹת, אִשְׁתַּכַּח דְּשָׁיְכָא יַד נָכְרִי בַּוְּלָדוֹת. הִלְכָּךְ וְלָדוֹת בְּכוֹרוֹת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לְאוֹתָן וְלָדוֹת פְּטוּרוֹת מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה, דְּכָל דְּשָׁיְכָא יַד נָכְרִי בְּאִמּוֹ הוּא פָּטוּר מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה. אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁיְּבַכְּרוּ וַלְדֵי וְלָדוֹת, דְּהַיְנוּ רְבִיעִי לְצֹאן בַּרְזֶל, יִתְּנוּ לַכֹּהֵן, דְּכֻלֵּי הַאי לֹא שָׁיְכָא יַד נָכְרִי לְמִתְפַּס וַלְדֵי וְלָדוֹת:


וולדות פטורין – from being firstling. Their offspring of those sheep when they grow and bear animals for the first time will be exempt from the law of firstlings. For if he does not give money to the heathen, he will seize the animal, and if he does not find the animal, he will seize the offspring, it is found that the heathen has a connection with the offspring. Therefore, the firstborn of these offspring is exempt from the law of firstlings, for all that the heathen has a hand in/connection to its mother is exempt from the [law of] firstlings. But when the offspring of the offspring bear animals for the first time , that is the fourth [generation] of the guaranteed investment, they shall be given to the Kohen, for all that much the heathen does not have a hand in/connection to seize the offspring of the offspring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:3)


הָכִי גָרְסִינַן הֶעֱמִיד וְלָדוֹת תַּחַת אִמּוֹתֵיהֶן וַלְדֵי וְלָדוֹת פְּטוּרִין וַלְדֵי וַלְדֵי וְלָדוֹת חַיָּבִים. הֶעֱמִיד וְלָדוֹת תַּחַת אִמּוֹתֵיהֶן, שֶׁפָּתַח לוֹ פֶּתַח לַנָּכְרִי לִהְיוֹת לוֹ כֹּחַ בַּוְּלָדוֹת וְאָמַר לוֹ אִם יָמוּתוּ הַבְּהֵמוֹת תִּטֹּל וְלָדוֹת הַמַּגִּיעוֹת לְחֶלְקִי, הַשְׁתָּא אַלִּימָא יַד נָכְרִי טְפֵי וְשָׁיְכָא בְּדוֹר אֶחָד טְפֵי מִשֶּׁל הָרִאשׁוֹן. הִלְכָּךְ וַלְדֵי וְלָדוֹת שֶׁל בְּהֵמוֹת צֹאן בַּרְזֶל כְּשֶׁבִּכְּרוּ פְּטוּרוֹת מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה, הוֹאִיל וְשָׁיְכָא יַד הַנָּכְרִי בְּדִידְהוּ. וּוַלְדֵי וְלָדוֹת שֶׁל וְלָדוֹת כְּשֶׁיְּבַכְּרוּ, דְּהַיְנוּ חֲמִישִׁי לְצֹאן בַּרְזֶל, יִנָּתְנוּ לַכֹּהֵן:


העמיד ולדות תחת אמותיהן ולדי ולדות פטורין ולדי ולדי ולדות חייבים – if [the Israelite] stipulated that the offspring were in place of their mothers, when he (i.e., the Israelite) made an opening for the heathen for him to have power over the offspring and stated to him: “If the animals will die, you will take the offspring that reach my portion. Now, the power of the hand of the heathen is stronger and is connected to one generation more than of the first [alone]. Therefore, the offspring of the offspring of animals is a guaranteed investment, that when the animals gave birth for the first time, they are exempt from the [law relating] to firstborn animals, since the hand of the heathen belongs to/is attached to them. The offspring of the offspring of the offspring, when they first give birth, that is the fifth [generation] of guaranteed investment, should be given to the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:4:4)


**שֶׁאַחֲרָיוּתָן לַנָּכְרִי.** דִּלְעוֹלָם כָּל מַה דְּמַשְׁכַּח נָכְרִי תָּפֵיס. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


שאחריותן לנכרי -for always, everything that is found the Gentile grabs hold of. But the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:5:1)


**רָחֵל שֶׁיָּלְדָה כְמִין עֵז.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ מִינִים חַיָּבִים בַּבְּכוֹרָה, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אִם אֵינוֹ דּוֹמֶה לְאִמּוֹ פָּטוּר מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה. דִּכְתִיב אַךְ בְּכוֹר שׁוֹר, עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא הוּא [שׁוֹר] וּבְכוֹרוֹ שׁוֹר:


רחל שילדה כמין עז – even though that both of them are species they are liable for [the law] of the firstborn, nevertheless, if it is not similar to its mother, it is exempt from [the law] of the firstborn. As is it written (Numbers 18:17): “But the firstlings of cattle, [sheep or goats may not be redeemed; they are consecrated],” until it [the cattle/ox] and its first-born is a cattle/ox.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:5:2)


**וְאִם יֶשׁ בּוֹ מִקְצָת סִימָנִים.** שֶׁדּוֹמֶה לְאִמּוֹ עַד שֶׁהוּא נִכָּר בָּהֶן שֶׁהוּא מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין, חַיָּב:


ואם יש בו מקצת סימנים – that it is similar to its mother, until that it is recognized in them that it is from the same species, it is liable.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:1)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר הַזְּכָרִים לַה'.** הַזְּכָרִים מַשְׁמַע שְׁנַיִם:


שנאמר הזכרים לה' – [the word] "זכרים"/males – implies two.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:2)


**אִי אֶפְשָׁר.** לְצַמְצֵם שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ שְׁנֵי רָאשֵׁיהֶן כְּאֶחָד, אֶלָּא הָאֶחָד יָצָא תְחִלָּה וְלֹא יָדְעִינַן הֵי מִנַּיְהוּ:


אי אפשר – to ascertain exactly their two heads came out as one (see Tractate Bekhorot 17a), but rather, the one came out first and we don’t know which of them it was.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:3)


**בּוֹרֵר לוֹ אֶת הַיָּפֶה.** דְּמִסְּתָמָא הַיָּפֶה וְהַבָּרִיא נָפַק בְּרֵישָׁא:


בורר לו את היפה – that in general, the better and [more] healthy one goes out at the beginning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:4)


**מְשַׁמְּנִים בֵּינֵיהֶן.** הַבְּעָלִים וְהַכֹּהֵן אֵין לָהֶם יִתְרוֹן זֶה עַל זֶה בַּחֲלֻקָּתָן אֶלָּא הַשֻּׁמָּן, שֶׁהַיִּשְׂרָאֵל נוֹטֵל הַשָּׁמֵן וּמֵנִיחַ הַכָּחוּשׁ לַכֹּהֵן, שֶׁהַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


משמנים ביניהם – the owners and the Kohen do not have an advantage one on the other in their division other than the [permitted] fat, for the Israelite takes the fat and the leaves the lean one to the Kohen, for he who wants to exact [compensation] from his fellow bears the burden of proof (see Tractate Bava Kamma, Chapter 3, Mishnah 11). And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva (see Talmud Bekhorot 18a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:5)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי.** שֶׁנִּשְׁאַר לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, יִרְעֶה עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ מוּם וְאַחַר כָּךְ יֵאָכֵל, דִּסְפֵק בְּכוֹר הוּא, הִלְכָּךְ אֵינוֹ נִשְׁחָט תָּמִים. וּבְאוֹתוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן לֹא צָרִיךְ לְמֵימַר, דְּוַדַּאי אֵינוֹ שׁוֹחֲטוֹ עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בוֹ מוּם, דְּבַזְּמַן הַזֶּה קָמַיְרֵי:


והשני – that remains to the Israelite, it should pasture until a blemish befalls it and afterwards it may be consumed, for it is a doubtful firstling , therefore, it is not slaughtered as pure. But that which belongs to the Kohen, it is not necessary to state that certainly he does not slaughter it until there should be a blemish, but we are speaking about at this time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:6)


**וְחַיָּב בַּמַּתָּנוֹת.** הַיִּשְׂרָאֵל כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁחָטֶנּוּ יִתֵּן לַכֹּהֵן הַזְּרוֹעַ וְהַלְּחָיַיִם וְהַקֵּבָה. מִמַּה נַּפְשָׁךְ, אִי בְכוֹר הוּא כֻלּוֹ לַכֹּהֵן, אִי לָאו בְּכוֹר הוּא חַיָּב בַּמַּתָּנוֹת:


וחייב במתנות – the Israelite, when he slaughters it, he would give to the Kohen the foreleg, jaw and maw. For whichever way you turn, if it is firstling, all of it goes to the Kohen, but if it is not a firstling, he (i.e., the owner) is liable to the priestly gifts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:7)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי פּוֹטֵר.** דַּהֲוֵי כְאִלּוּ זָכָה בוֹ כֹהֵן וְהִגִּיעַ לְיָדוֹ וּכְשֶׁנָּפַל בּוֹ מוּם נְתָנוֹ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁהוּא פָּטוּר מִן הַמַּתָּנוֹת, הוֹאִיל וְחָשְׁבִינַן לֵיהּ כְּאִלּוּ בָא מִיַּד כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ורבי יוסי פוטר – for this is as if the Kohen was worthy of it and it reached his hand but if a blemish befell it, it is given to an Israelite. And it is found that it is exempt from the priestly gifts, for since we consider it as if it came from the hand of a Kohen to an Israelite. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:8)


**רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן אוֹמֵר יַחֲלֹקוּ.** דְּהָדַר בֵּיהּ רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן מִמַּאי דְּאָמַר לְעֵיל דְּכֹהֵן בּוֹרֵר לוֹ אֶת הַיָּפֶה, וְסָבַר דְּמֵחַיִּים נַמִּי שָׁמִין אֶת שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּדָמִים וְחוֹלְקִים אֶת הַדָּמִים. דְּלִשְׁנֵיהֶן יֵשׁ חֵלֶק בִּשְׁנֵיהֶם, הִלְכָּךְ מֵת אֶחָד מֵהֶם יַחְלְקוּ אֶת הַחַי:


רבי טרפון אומר יחלוקו – for Rabbi Tarfon retracted from what he said above [in this Mishnah] that the Kohen selects for himself the better part, for he holds that while alive also we compromise with both of them (i.e., the Kohen and the Israelite) and divide the proceeds. For both of them have a part in both of them (i.e., the animals – the two males). Therefore, if one of them died, they should divide the living one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:9)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר הַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


רבי עקיבא אומר המוציא מחבירו עליו הראיה – and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:6:10)


**זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה אֵין לַכֹּהֵן כְּלוּם.** דְּשֶׁמָּא נְקֵבָה יָצָאתָה תְחִלָּה וְהַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה. וְהָכָא אֲפִלּוּ רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן מוֹדֶה, דְּהָתָם הוּא דְּקָא פָלֵיג מִשּׁוּם דְּוַדַּאי הָוֵי חַד לַכֹּהֵן הִלְכָּךְ יָפֶה כֹּחוֹ לַחֲלֹק בְּשָׁוֶה, אֲבָל הָכָא הֻרַע כֹּחוֹ דְּשֶׁמָּא לֹא שָׁיְכָא בְּכוֹרָה כְלָל. וּמִיהוּ יִרְעֶה עַד שֶׁיִּסְתָּאֵב וְאַחַר כָּךְ יֹאכְלֶנּוּ, דְּסָפֵק הוּא:


זכר ונקבה אין לכהן כלום – for perhaps a female [animal] came out first and he who wants to exact [compensation] from his fellow bears the burden of proof. But here, even Rabbi Tarfon admits, that there, when he disagrees because certainly that one [animal] goes to the Kohen, therefore it strengthens one’s right to divide it equally, but here, his power is lessened for perhaps the issue of first-born status is not connected at all. But however, it should go to pasture until a blemish befalls it and afterwards, he can consume it, for it’s a doubt.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:9:1)


**יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן.** שֶׁנִּקְרְעָה אִמּוֹ וְהוֹצִיאוּ הָעֻבָּר דֶּרֶךְ דָּפְנוֹתֶיהָ:


יוצא דפן (a fetus extracted by means of the cesarean section) – its mother was operated upon by a section and they removed the fetus through its walls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:9:2)


**וְהַבָּא אַחֲרָיו.** הַנּוֹלָד אַחֲרָיו דֶּרֶךְ הָרֶחֶם:


והבא אחריו – that which is born after it through the path of the womb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/2:9:3)


**יִרְעוּ עַד שֶׁיִּסְתָּאֲבוּ.** דִּמְסַפְּקָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי טַרְפוֹן אִי בְכוֹר לַוְּלָדוֹת קַדִּישׁ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ בְכוֹר לָרְחָמִים, כְּגוֹן יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן שֶׁהוּא הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁנּוֹלַד אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא פָּטַר הוּא אֶת הָרֶחֶם, אִי בְכוֹר לָרְחָמִים קַדִּישׁ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ בְכוֹר לַוְּלָדוֹת, כְּגוֹן הַבָּא אַחַר יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן שֶׁהוּא הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁפָּטַר אֶת הָרֶחֶם אֲבָל אֵינוֹ בְכוֹר לַוְּלָדוֹת שֶׁהֲרֵי יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן קְדָמָהוּ. וּמִסְּפֵקָא שְׁנֵיהֶם יִרְעוּ עַד שֶׁיִּסְתָּאֲבוּ וְיֵאָכְלוּ בְּמוּמָן לַבְּעָלִים, דְּשֶׁמָּא תַרְתֵּי בָעִינַן, בְּכוֹר לַוְּלָדוֹת וְלָרְחָמִים, וְאֵין שׁוּם אֶחָד מֵהֶן קָדוֹשׁ, וְהַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה. וְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא פְּשִׁיטָא לֵיהּ דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ בָעִינַן וְאֵין שׁוּם אֶחָד מֵהֶן קָדוֹשׁ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ירעו עד שיסתאבו – for it is doubtful to Rabbi Tarfon if the firstling of the offspring is holy even though it is not a firstling to the womb, such as a fetus extracted by means of cesarean section which is the first that is born even though it did not open up the womb. If it is a firstling to the womb, it is holy even though it is not the firstling to the offspring, as for example, that which comes after one born by means of cesarean section which is the first that opened the womb bur is not the firstling of the offspring since the one born by means of cesarian section preceded it. But from doubt, both are sent to pasture until they develop blemishes and their owners will consume them with their blemishes for perhaps, we require both [that it is the first to open the womb and the first one born] of them to be firstlings of offspring and to [the opening of] their wombs, but neither one of them is holy. But he who wants to exact [compensation] from his fellow bears the burden of proof. But for Rabbi Akiva, it is obvious to him that we require both [of these prerequisites] require, and not a single one of them is holy. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:1)


**הַלּוֹקֵחַ.** עֵז בַּת שְׁנָתָהּ. שֶׁיָּלְדָה בְתוֹךְ שְׁנָתָהּ:


הלוקח. עז בת שנתה – [a goat] a year old that produced an offspring within its first year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:2)


**וַדַּאי.** אוֹתוֹ הַוָּלָד לַכֹּהֵן, דְּקֹדֶם לָכֵן לֹא יָלְדָה:


ודאי- that offspring belongs to the Kohen, for prior to this it had not produced offspring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:3)


**סָפֵק.** יִרְעֶה עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ מוּם וְיֵאָכֵל בְּמוּמוֹ לַבְּעָלִים. וְאִם סְפֵק פֶּטֶר חֲמוֹר הוּא, מַפְרִישׁ טָלֶה וְהוּא שֶׁלּוֹ:


ספק - it would be sent to pasture until a blemish befalls it and the owners would consume it in its blemishes (see Tractate Bekhorot, Chapter 2, Mishnayot 6-7). But if it is a doubtful firstling donkey, he would separate a lamb and it would be his.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:4)


**טִנּוּף.** אֲבַעְבּוּעוֹת שֶׁל דָּם שֶׁפּוֹלֶטֶת הַבְּהֵמָה. וּמַרְאִים אוֹתָן לְרוֹעֶה חָכָם וּמַכִּיר בָּהֶן אִם הֵן מֵחֲמַת הֵרָיוֹן שֶׁהִפִּילָה וְשֶׁמָּא טִנְּפָה בְתוֹךְ שְׁנָתָהּ דַּהֲוֵי סְפֵק וָלָד וְנִפְטְרָה מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה, הִלְכָּךְ אֲפִלּוּ עֵז בַּת שְׁנָתָהּ סָפֵק הוּא:


טנוף (discharge from the womb indicating abortion) – bubbles/pustules of blood that the animal discharges. And they show them to a wise shepherd and recognizes them if they are because of the pregnancy that it miscarried and perhaps it discharged within the first year which is a doubtful offspring and it is exempt from the laws of firstlings, therefore, even a goat that is one year old is doubtful.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:5)


**שִׁלְיָא.** כְּמִין כִּסּוּי עַל הָעֻבָּר שֶׁהָעֻבָּר מֻנָּח בָּהּ:


שליא – a kind of covering over the fetus that the fetus is lying in it (i.e., placenta).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:6)


**שָׁפִיר.** לְמַעְלָה מִן הַשִּׁלְיָא קְרוּם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ דָּם נִקְרָשׁ וְהוּא קָרוּי שָׁפִיר. וְרַבּוֹתַי פֵּרְשׁוּ, חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל בָּשָׂר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ צוּרַת אָדָם. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים לְפִיכָךְ נִקְרֵאת שָׁפִיר, לְפִי שֶׁעֲשׂוּיָה כִשְׁפוֹפֶרֶת:


שפיר – above from the placenta is a membrane/skin that has in it congealed blood and it is called the sac of a fetus. And my teachers/Rabbis explained, it is a piece of flesh that has on it the form of a human. But there are those who say that is called a sac of a fetus, because it is made like a tube.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:7)


**שֶׁשָּׁפְעָה חֲרָרַת דָּם.** שֶׁהִפִּילָה חֲתִיכָה דָם:


ששפעה חרדת דם (that discharged a clod of blood) – that discharged a portion of blood (see also Tractate Hullin, Chapter 4, Mishnah 7 regarding to the placenta).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:1:8)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ תִקָּבֵר.** לְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁנִּפְטְרָה מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה. שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה שֶׁקּוֹבְרִים אוֹתָהּ יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהַבָּא אַחֲרָיו אֵינוֹ קָדוֹשׁ בַּבְּכוֹרָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וּכְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב:


הרי זו תקבר – to inform that it was exempted from the [law of] the firstling, for a person who sees that they bury it knows that what comes after it (i.e., born afterwards) is not holy for the [law of] the firstling. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva and [according to] Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:2:1)


**אֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין שֶׁמָּא בְנָהּ שֶׁל אַחֶרֶת הָיָה.** שֶׁיְּהֵא הַבָּא אַחֲרָיו בְּכוֹר סָפֵק דְּנֵימָא הַךְ בְּהֵמָה לֹא יָלְדָה מֵעוֹלָם אֶלָּא שֶׁאָהֲבָה אֶת זֶה בֶּן חֲבֶרְתָּהּ, וְאִי מִשּׁוּם דְּאִית לָהּ חָלָב, הָא אִיכָּא מִעוּטָא דְּחוֹלְבוֹת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן יוֹלְדוֹת, הָא וַדַּאי לֹא אָמְרִינַן, אֶלָּא בְנָהּ הוּא וּפְטוּרָה מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה:


אין חוששין שמא בנה של אחרת היה - that what comes (i.e., the offspring) after it is a doubtful firstling, that we would say: “this animal never gave birth, but that she loved this one, the male offspring of her neighbor,” but if because that she has milk, there is a minority of those giving milk even though they have not given birth, but certainly we don’t say, that it is her offspring and that she is exempt from the laws of firstlings (see Talmud Bekhorot 20b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:2:2)


**מַבְכִּירוֹת.** בַּחוּרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יָלְדוּ עַד עַכְשָׁיו:


מבכירות – female animals that did not give birth until now.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:2:3)


**אֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין שֶׁמָּא בְנָהּ שֶׁל זוֹ בָא לוֹ אֵצֶל זוֹ.** דְּלֵחוּשׁ לְכֻלְּהוּ בִּסְפֵק בְּכוֹרוֹת, אֶלָּא וַדַּאי אוֹתָן הַכְּרוּכִין אַחַר הַמַּבְכִּירוֹת הָווּ בְּכוֹרוֹת וַדָּאִין, וְהַכְּרוּכִין אַחַר שֶׁאֵין מַבְכִּירוֹת הָווּ פְּשׁוּטִין וַדָּאִין. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


אין חוששין שמא בנה של זו בא לו אצל זו – for we should be troubled for all of them of a doubtful firstling status, but certainly, those that are running after/clinging to those animals that have given birth for the first time were definite firstlings, but those running after/clinging to those animals that were not giving birth for the first time were definitely plain (i.e., not firstlings). And the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:3:1)


**עוֹשֶׂה מָקוֹם בְּקוֹפִיץ.** גָּרְסִינַן. וּבַגְּמָרָא [דַּף כה] מְפָרֵשׁ, תָּנֵי לְקוֹפִיץ, כְּלוֹמַר לְצֹרֶךְ מְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל קוֹפִיץ תּוֹלֵשׁ שֵׂעָר שֶׁל צַוָּאר. וְאֵין כָּאן מִשּׁוּם לֹא תָגֹז בְּכוֹר צֹאנֶךָ, דִּתְלִישָׁה בַּיָּד לָאו גְּזִיזָה הִיא. אֲבָל בִּכְלִי וַדַּאי אָסוּר:


עושה מקום בקופיץ - This is our reading. But in the Gemara (Talmud Bekhorot 25a), it explains that we teach לקופיץ (instead of בקופיץ )/to a hatchel, that is to say, for the need of the place of the hatchel, we remove the hair of the neck, and there isn’t here [a violation of] (Deuteronomy 15:19): “you must not…sheer your firstling sheep,” for plucking with the hand is not sheering , but with a utensil, it is definitely forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:3:2)


**וְכֵן מֻתָּר לִתְלֹשׁ אֶת הַשֵּׂעָר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לְכַתְּחִלָּה, לְהַרְאוֹת בּוֹ לְחָכָם מְקוֹם הַמּוּם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי בֶּן מְשֻׁלָּם:


וכן מותר לתלוש את השער- and even ab initio, to show to the Sage the place of the blemish. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi ben Meshulam.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:1)


**עֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל מַתִּיר.** הַצֶּמֶר בַּהֲנָאָה לַכֹּהֵן:


עקביה בן מהללאל מתיר [Rabbi Akavya ben Mehallalel permits] the wool for the Kohen to derive benefit from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים.** דְּאִי שָׁרֵית לֵיהּ צֶמֶר הַנּוֹשֶׁרֶת מֵחַיִּים אָתֵי לְאַשְׁהוֹיֵי לַבְּכוֹר כְּדֵי שֶׁתַּשִּׁיר צִמְרוֹ כָּל שָׁעָה, וְאָתֵי בָהּ לִידֵי תַקָּלָה שֶׁיִּגְזֹז וְיַעֲבֹד בּוֹ, וּפְסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁין אֲסִירֵי בְגִזָּה וַעֲבוֹדָה דִּכְתִיב תִּזְבַּח וְאָכַלְתָּ בָּשָׂר, תִּזְבַּח וְלֹא גִזָּה:


וחכמים אוסרים – for if he permitted him the wool that drops off/falls out while living, he will come to wait for a firstling in order that his wool will drop off/fall out every hour, and it will come to be a snare/stumbling block that he will shear it and work on it, and those consecrated animals that are disqualified are forbidden for shearing and for work, as it is written )Deuteronomy 12:15): “[But whenever you desire,] you may slaughter and eat meat [in any of your settlements according to the blessing that the LORD your God has granted you],” you may slaughter but not shearing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:3)


**לֹא בָזֶה הִתִּיר עֲקַבְיָא.** כְּלוֹמַר לֹא בָזֶה עֲקַבְיָא מַתִּיר וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים, דְּבִשְׁחָטוֹ דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל שָׁרֵי, דְּמִגּוֹ דִמְהַנְּיָא שְׁחִיטָה לַצֶּמֶר הַמְחֻבָּר בּוֹ לְהַתִּירוֹ לְאַחַר שְׁחִיטָה מְהַנְּיָא נַמִּי לְתָלוּשׁ וּמֻנָּח בַּחַלּוֹן. וְלֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא בְמֵת, דְּאוֹתוֹ צֶמֶר הַמְחֻבָּר בּוֹ טָעוּן קְבוּרָה, עֲקַבְיָא מַתִּיר לַצֶּמֶר שֶׁנָּשַׁר מִמֶּנּוּ כְּשֶׁהוּא בַּחַיִּים, וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַשְׁהֶנּוּ כְּדֵי לֵהָנוֹת בַּצֶּמֶר שֶׁנּוֹשֵׁר מִמֶּנּוּ כָּל שָׁעָה וְאָתֵי לְמֶעֱבַד בֵּיהּ גִּזָּה וַעֲבוֹדָה. וּפְסַק הַהֲלָכָה, דַּאֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שְׁחִיטָה צֶמֶר שֶׁנָּשַׁר מִמֶּנּוּ בַּחַיִּים אָסוּר:


לא בזה התיר עקביה – meaning to say, not in this [case- of the hair of a blemished firstling which fell out, and which one put in a wall niche/window, and which -firstling – one slaughtered afterwards] did [Rabbi] Akavyah [ben Mehallalel] permit and the Sages prohibited, for everyone agrees that when he slaughters it, it is permitted, slaughtering provides the benefit of the wool that is attached to it to permit it after ritual slaughtering, he also benefits from that which is plucked/detached and placed in a wall niche/window. But they did not disagree other than regarding a dead animal, for that wool that is attached to it requires burial, [Rabbi] Akavya [ben Mehallalel] permits the wool that fell out from it while it was living but the Sages prohibit it as a decree lest he will delay in order to derive benefit from the wool that falls out from it every hour, and one would come to perform acts of shearing and work. But the legal decision is even after the ritual slaughter, the wool that fell out from it while living is forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:4)


**צֶמֶר הַמְדֻבְלָל.** שֶׁלֹּא נִתְלַשׁ לְגַמְרֵי אֲבָל מְחֻבָּר הוּא עִם הַצֶּמֶר וְאֵינוֹ נוֹפֵל:


צמר המדובלל (wool which dangles/clumps in the wool) – that was not detached entirely but is attached with the wool and it doesn’t fall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:5)


**אֶת שֶׁנִּרְאֶה עִם הַגִּזָּה.** כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁחָטֶנּוּ וְגוֹזְזוֹ לְאַחַר שְׁחִיטָה, וִיהֵא הַצֶּמֶר הַמְדֻבְלָל נִבְלָל עִם שְׁאָר הַגִּזָּה וְאֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה כְמֻפְרָשׁ מִמֶּנָּה, מֻתָּר כִּשְׁאָר הַגִּזָּה:


את שנראה עם הגיזה – when they slaughter it and shear it after the ritual slaughter, that the wool that clumps/dangles is mixed with the rest of the shearing and does not appear as separated from it, it is permitted like the rest of the shearing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:6)


**וְשֶׁאֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה עִם הַגִּזָּה.** שֶׁיָּצָא חוּץ יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי וְנִכָּר לַכֹּל (שמדבלל) [שֶׁמֻּבְדָּל] מִן הַגִּזָּה:


ושאינו נראה עם הגיזה – that it went outside too much and is recognized that it is [separated] from the shearing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 3:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/3:4:7)


**אָסוּר.** כְּמִי שֶׁנָּשַׁר לְגַמְרֵי קֹדֶם שְׁחִיטָה. וּסְתָמָא כְרַבָּנָן דִּפְלִיגֵי עֲלֵיהּ דַּעֲקַבְיָא דְהִלְכָתָא כְוָתַיְהוּ:


אסור – it is like it fell off/dropped completely prior to the ritual slaughter. And the anonymous teaching is according to the Rabbis who dispute that of [Rabbi] Akavya [ben Mehallalel], and the Halakha is according to them.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:1)


**עַד כַּמָּה.** לְהִטַּפֵּל בִּבְכוֹר. לְהִתְעַסֵּק בְּגִדּוּלוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּתְּנֵהוּ לַכֹּהֵן:


עד כמה. להטפל בבכור – to be engaged with its (i.e., the firstling’s) raising prior to giving it to the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:2)


**בְּדַקָּה עַד שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם וּבְגַסָּה עַד חֲמִשִּׁים.** בַּגְּמָרָא יָלְפִינַן לַהּ מִדִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) מְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ לֹא תְאַחֵר בְּכוֹר בָּנֶיךָ תִּתֶּן לִי, וּסְמִיךְ לֵיהּ כֵּן תַּעֲשֶׂה לְשֹׁרְךָ לְצֹאנֶךָ, וְרָמִינַן שֹׁרְךָ הַמֻּקְדָּם בַּמִּקְרָא לִמְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ שֶׁהוּא מֻקְדָּם, וְצֹאנֶךָ הַמְאֻחָר לִבְכוֹר בָּנֶיךָ שֶׁהוּא מְאֻחָר, וּמְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ שֶׁהֵם הַבִּכּוּרִים קְרֵבִים לַחֲמִשִּׁים יוֹם, שֶׁהַתְּבוּאָה מְבֻשֶּׁלֶת בְּפֶסַח וְאֵין מְבִיאִין בִּכּוּרִים עַד עֲצֶרֶת דִּכְתִיב (שם כג) וְחַג הַקָּצִיר בִּכּוּרֵי מַעֲשֶׂיךָ, וּכְמוֹ שֶׁאַתָּה עוֹשֶׂה לִמְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ שֶׁאֵינְךָ מְבִיאָם אֶלָּא לְסוֹף חֲמִשִּׁים יוֹם, כֵּן תַּעֲשֶׂה לְשׁוֹרְךָ שֶׁלֹּא תְבִיאֶנּוּ עַד חֲמִשִּׁים יוֹם, וּכְדֶרֶךְ שֶׁאַתָּה עוֹשֶׂה לִבְכוֹר בָּנֶיךָ דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ (במדבר יח) וּפְדוּיָו מִבֶּן חֹדֶשׁ תִּפְדֶּה, כֵּן תַּעֲשֶׂה לְצֹאנְךָ שֶׁלֹּא תְּבִיאֶנּוּ עַד שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם:


בדקה עד שלשים יום ובגסה עד חמשים – In the Gemara (Tractate Bekhorot 26b), we derive it, from what is written (Exodus 22:28): “You shall not put off the skimming of the first yield of your vats. You shall give Me the first-born among your sons,” and adjacent to it (Exodus 22:29), “You shall do the same with your cattle and your flocks,” and they show an incongruity/objection, “your bull” which is mentioned earlier in Scripture to “the skimming of the first yield of your vats” which is early, and “your flocks” which is later than the “the first-born among your sons” but “the skimming of the first yield of your vats” which are the first-fruits are offered at fifty days, that the grain is ripened on Passover and we don’t bring the first-fruits until Atzeret/Shavuot, as it is written (Exodus 23:16): “and the Feast of the Harvest, of the first fruits of your work, [of what you sow in the field], and just as you do for the “skimming of the first yield of your vats, which you do not bring until the end of fifty days, so you do for your bulls that you do not bring it until fifty days, and in the manner that you act regarding the first-born of your sons, as it is written concerning it (Numbers 18:16): “Take as their redemption price from the age of one month up,” similarly you should do for your flock that you should not bring it until thirty days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:3)


**בְּדַקָּה שְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים.** לְפִי שֶׁטִּפּוּלָהּ מְרֻבֶּה, שֶׁשִּׁנֶּיהָ דַקּוֹת וְאֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לֶאֱכֹל עֵשֶׂב, וְאִם לֹא תִהְיֶה עִם אִמָּהּ תָּמוּת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


בדקה שלשה חדשים – because its care-taking (i.e., of small cattle) is more troublesome (see Talmud Bekhorot 26b and Talmud Bava Metzia 69a), for its teeth are thin and it is not able to eat grass. And if it won’t be with its mother it will die. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:4)


**לֹא יִתְּנוֹ לוֹ.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל רַמְיָא לְטַפֵּל בּוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים יוֹם וְאָמַר לוֹ כֹהֵן בְּתוֹךְ זְמַן זֶה תְּנֵהוּ לִי וְאֶרְעֶנּוּ, דּוֹמֶה כְמִי שֶׁשּׂוֹכְרוֹ, שֶׁהוּא מַצִּילוֹ מִן הַטֹּרַח עַל מְנָת שֶׁיִּתְּנֶנּוּ לוֹ וְלֹא לְכֹהֵן אַחֵר, וְדוֹמֶה לְכֹהֵן הַמְסַיֵּעַ בְּבֵית הַגְּרָנוֹת לָדוּשׁ וְלִזְרוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ לוֹ הַתְּרוּמוֹת, וְתַנְיָא כֹהֲנִים וּלְוִיִּם הַמְסַיְּעִים בְּבֵית הַגְּרָנוֹת אֵין נוֹתְנִין לָהֶם תְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת. אֲבָל אִם הָיָה בַּעַל מוּם וְאָמַר לוֹ תֵּן לִי שֶׁאֹכְלֶנּוּ, הוֹאִיל וַחֲזִי לֵיהּ לֹא הָוֵי כְּכֹהֵן הַמְסַיֵּעַ בְּבֵית הַגְּרָנוֹת:


לא יתנו לו – for since its care-taking is cast upon an Israelite for fifty days, and the Kohen said to him “Within this time [period], give it to me and I will pasture/tend it.” It is similar to someone who rents it that he saves him from the trouble on the condition that he (i.e., the owner) will give it to him and not to another Kohen, and this is similar to a Kohen who helps/assists in the granary/threshing floor to thresh and to winnow in order that they will give him the heave-offerings/Terumot, and it is taught [in a Baraitha] that Kohanim and Levites who assist in the granary/threshing floor, they don’t give them have-offerings and tithes. But if [the animal] had a blemish and he said to him: “Give it to me and I will consume it, for since it was pointed out to him, e was not like the Kohen who assists in the granary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:1:5)


**בֵּין תָּמִים בֵּין בַּעַל מוּם.** בֵּין בִּזְמַן הַמִּקְדָּשׁ וּבֵין עַכְשָׁיו, כָּל שָׁנָה דִידֵיהּ רַשַּׁאי לְקַיְּמוֹ. וּבְכוֹר בַּעַל מוּם יָלְפִינַן שֶׁהוּא נִתָּן לַכֹּהֵן וְאוֹכְלוֹ בְמוּמוֹ מִדִּכְתִיב (שם) וּבְשָׂרָם יִהְיֶה לָּךְ, לְשׁוֹן רַבִּים, אֶחָד בְּכוֹר תָּם וְאֶחָד בְּכוֹר בַּעַל מוּם:


בין תמים בין בעל מחם – whether at the time of the Temple or whether at the present time, every year it is his, he is permitted to uphold it. And a firstling with a blemish, we derive that it is given to a Kohen, and he consumes it with its blemish for since it is written (Numbers 18:18): “But their meat shall be yours,” is the plural language, whether it is pure firstling or whether it is a firstling with a blemish.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:2:1)


**כָּל שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ.** מִשֶּׁנּוֹלַד:


כל שנים עשר חדש – from when it (i.e., the firstling) was born.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:2:2)


**לְאַחַר שְׁנָתוֹ.** כְּגוֹן בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה דְּצָרִיךְ לְקַיְּמוֹ עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ מוּם:


לאחר שנתו – as for example, at this time, when he needs to keep it/sustain it until a blemish befalls it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:2:3)


**אֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי לְקַיְּמוֹ אֶלָּא עַד שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** מִיּוֹם שֶׁנָּפַל בּוֹ הַמּוּם:


אינו רשאי לקיימו אלא עד שלשים יום – from the day that the blemish befalls it [consuming it thirty days later].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:3:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר.** בְּמוּמִין שֶׁבָּעַיִן מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָסוּר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵן מִשְׁתַּנִּים לְאַחַר מִיתָה, שֶׁמִּפְּנֵי צַעַר מִיתָה הָעַיִן מִשְׁתַּנָּה, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַשְׁתָּא מִתְחֲזֵי מוּם קָבוּעַ אִי הֲוָה חָזֵי לֵיהּ מֵחַיִּים הֲוָה מִתְחֲזֵי עוֹבֵר, וְלֹא שָׁרֵי אֶלָּא בְמוּמִין שֶׁבַּגּוּף שֶׁאֵינָן מִשְׁתַּנִּים. וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר סָבַר גָּזְרִינַן מוּמִין שֶׁבַּגּוּף שֶׁאֵינָן מִשְׁתַּנִּים אַטּוּ מוּמִין שֶׁבָּעַיִן שֶׁהֵן מִשְׁתַּנִּים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


רבי יהודה מתיר – with blemishes that are in the eye, Rabbi Yehuda admits/agrees that it is prohibited, because they change after death since because of the pain of death, the eye changes and even though it appears as a permanent blemish, if it had appeared to it while living it would have appeared as a passing [blemish], and he would not permit anything other than blemishes that are on the body that do not change. But Rabbi Meir holds that we make a decree that blemishes that are on the body that do not change on account of blemishes that are in the eye that do change. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:1)


**מֻמְחֶה.** הוּא שֶׁנָּטַל רְשׁוּת מִן הַנָּשִׂיא אוֹ מִבֵּית דִּין שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהַתִּיר בְּכוֹרוֹת. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְעָלְמָא מִי שֶׁלָּמַד בַּתּוֹרָה [שֶׁבִּכְתָב וְ]שֶׁבְּעַל פֶּה וְיוֹדֵעַ לִסְבֹּר וּלְהַקִּישׁ וּלְהָבִין דָּבָר מִתּוֹךְ דָּבָר הוּא הַנִּקְרָא מֻמְחֶה, וּכְשֶׁהוּא נִכָּר וְיָדוּעַ וְיָצָא טִבְעוֹ אֵצֶל אַנְשֵׁי דּוֹרוֹ הוּא מֻמְחֶה לָרַבִּים, וְהוּא יָכוֹל לָדוּן יְחִידִי, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא נָקַט רְשׁוּתָא מֵרֹאשׁ גּוֹלָה, לְעִנְיַן הֶתֵּר בְּכוֹרוֹת אֵינוֹ קָרוּי מֻמְחֶה, וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהַתִּיר בְּכוֹרוֹת אֶלָּא אַחַר שֶׁיִּטֹּל רְשׁוּת מִבֵּית דִּין הַסְּמוּכִים בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאֵין רְשׁוּת רֹאשׁ הַגּוֹלָה מוֹעִיל בָּזֶה:


מומחה – he that received permission from the Nasi/President or from the Bet Din/Jewish court of the Land of Israel to permit firstlings. And even though that in a general way, he who studied the [Written] Torah [and the Oral Torah] and knows how to think and to compare by analogy [two laws] and to understand and form a conclusion by analogy, he one who is called a specialist/Mumheh, and when he is recognized and known and when he has acquired fame among the people of his generation, he is a specialist for the many, and he can judge as an individual, even though he did not receive permission from the Exilarch/head of the Diaspora community, [but] in regard to permission to permit firstlings, he is not called a specialist, and he is not able to permit firstlings other than after he receives permission from the Bet Din/Jewish court of those ordained in the Land of Israel, for the domain of the Exilarch does not have an effect in this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:2)


**וִישַׁלֵּם מִבֵּיתוֹ.** וּכְשֶׁהוּא מְשַׁלֵּם לַכֹּהֵן נוֹתֵן רְבִיעַ דָּמָיו אִם הִיא בְהֵמָה דַקָּה, וַחֲצִי דָמָיו אִם הִיא גַסָּה. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי מְשַׁלֵּם מֶחֱצָה, דְמָמוֹן הַמֻּטָּל בְּסָפֵק חוֹלְקִים, דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר אַפְסְדֵיהּ, דְּאִי לֹא הֲוָה שָׁרֵי לֵיהּ הַאי הֲוָה אָתֵי מֻמְחֶה גָּמוּר וְשָׁרֵי לֵיהּ וַהֲוָה אֲכִיל לֵיהּ וְהַשְׁתָּא בָעֵי קְבוּרָה, וְאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר לָאו מִידִי אַפְסְדֵיהּ, דְּדִלְמָא לֹא הֲוָה בֵיהּ מוּמָא, וּמֻמְחֶה גָמוּר לֹא הֲוָה שָׁרֵי לֵיהּ, וְשֶׁמָּא לֹא הֲוָה נָפֵיל בֵּיהּ מוּם קָבוּעַ לְעוֹלָם עַד שֶׁיָּמוּת. וְהַאי דְּלֹא מְשַׁלֵּם פַּלְגָּא לְדַקָּה כְמוֹ לְגַסָּה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיֵּשׁ טֹרַח גָּדוֹל לְגַדֵּל בְּהֵמָה דַקָּה וְזֶה שֶׁהִתִּיר הַבְּכוֹר הִצִּילוֹ לַכֹּהֵן מִן הַטֹּרַח הַגָּדוֹל שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לְהִטַּפֵּל בָּהּ, דְּשֶׁמָּא אִם הֶרְאָהוּ לְאַחֵר לֹא הָיָה מַתִּירוֹ וְהָיָה לוֹ בוֹ טִפּוּל מְרֻבֶּה עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ מוּם, לְהָכִי נָגְעוּ בָהּ דְּלֹא מְשַׁלֵּם אֶלָּא רְבִיעַ. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרְשָׁהּ בְּעִנְיָן אַחֵר. וְזֶה עִקָּר:


וישלם מביתו – and when he pays a Kohen, he gives one-fourth of its cost if it is a small animal, and half of its cost if it is a large animal. And because of this, he pays one-half [of its cost], for the money that is placed in doubt we divide it, but one can say that he loses it, for if he had not permitted this to himself, he would bring a complete expert and permit it for himself and he would consume it, but now it requires burial, and one can say, that he doesn’t lose anything, for perhaps, there wasn’t a blemish and a complete specialist would not permit himself [this], and perhaps a fixed blemish would not fall upon it ever until it dies. And the case where he doesn’t pay one-half for a small animal like that of a large animal, is because there is greater trouble to raise a small animal and he who permitted the firstling for the Kohen saved him from the great trouble to care for a small animal, for perhaps if he had shown it to another, he would not have permitted it and he would have great amount of care taking for it until a blemish would befall it, and for this reason, he would injure it that he would not have to pay other than one-quarter. But Maimonides explained this regarding another matter, and this is essential.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:3)


**מַה שֶּׁעָשָׂה עָשׂוּי וִישַׁלֵּם מִבֵּיתוֹ.** לְמַאן דְּדָאִין דִּינָא דְגַרְמֵי חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם מִבֵּיתוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁגָּרַם הֶפְסֵד לַחֲבֵרוֹ. וּמַאן דְּלֹא דָאִין דִּינָא דְגַרְמֵי מְפָרֵשׁ מַתְנִיתִין דַּוְקָא שֶׁנָּשָׂא וְנָתַן בַּיָּד.


מה שעשה עשוי וישלם מביתו – for the one that where there is a case of indirect damage, he is liable to pay from his house/property for the loss that he caused to his fellow, but the one that does not judge a case of indirect damage explains our Mishnah explicitly that he engaged in business by hand [directly].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:4)


**זִכָּה אֶת הַחַיָּב.** מַשְׁכַּחַת לַהּ כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ מַשְׁכּוֹן לַמַּלְוֶה וְזֶה פָּטַר אֶת הַלֹּוֶה וְנָטַל אֶת הַמַּשְׁכּוֹן בְּיָדוֹ מִיַּד הַמַּלְוֶה וְהֶחֱזִירוֹ לַלֹּוֶה.


וזיכה את החייב – and the case can be found as for example, that he had a deposit with the lender and this [specialist] exempted the borrower [from paying] and he took the deposit in his from the hand of the lender and returned it to the borrower.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:5)


**וְטִמֵּא אֶת הַטָּהוֹר.** נַמִּי מַשְׁכַּחַת לַהּ שֶׁנָּשָׂא וְנָתַן בַּיָּד, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ לְפָנָיו טָהֳרוֹת לִשָּׁאֵל עֲלֵיהֶן וְאָמַר טְמֵאוֹת הֵן וְנָטַל שֶׁרֶץ אֶחָד וְהִגִּיעוֹ בָהֶן כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא בָהֶן עוֹד סָפֵק וְיַעַמְדוּ דְבָרָיו.


וטימא את הטהור – it is found that he did business with it by hand, as for example, that he brought before him pure animals to borrow them and he said that they are ritually impure and he took one reptile and had it come in contact with them, in order that there would be no further doubt and that his words would be validated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:6)


**וְטִהֵר אֶת הַטָּמֵא.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁלָּקַח פֵּרוֹת טְהוֹרוֹת וְעֵרְבָן עִם פֵּרוֹת אֵלּוּ שֶׁטִּהֵר הוּא שֶׁלֹּא כַדִּין וְטִמְּאָן:


וטיהר את הטמא – as for example that he took ritually pure fruit/produce and combined them with those fruit/produce that he purified, and he acted not in accordance with the law and defiled them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:7)


**וְאִם הָיָה מֻמְחֶה לְבֵית דִּין.** שֶׁנָּטַל רְשׁוּת מִבֵּית דִּין:


ואם היה מומחה לבית דין – that he received permission from the Bet Din.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:8)


**פָּטוּר.** דְּלֹא מָצֵי לְמֵימַר לֵיהּ אַמַּאי דָּיְנַת לֵיהּ הוֹאִיל וְלֹא בְקִיאַת בְּדִינֵי:


פטור – for he would not be able to tell him why he made this judgment for he was not expert in the laws.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:9)


**הָאֵם שֶׁלָּהּ.** רֶחֶם שֶׁלָּהּ:


האם שלה – her womb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:10)


**וְהֶאֱכִילָהּ רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן לִכְלָבִים.** שֶׁשָּׁאֲלוּ לוֹ עָלֶיהָ וְאָמַר טְרֵפָה וְהֶאֱכִילָהּ לִכְלָבִים:


והאכילה רבי טרפון לכלבים – for they asked him about it and he said that it was “torn” (i.e., unkosher) and he fed it to the dogs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:11)


**שֶׁלֹּא תֵלֵד.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁפָּרוֹת וַחֲזִירוֹת שֶׁלָּהֶן מְעֻלִּין מְאֹד וּמוֹכְרִים אוֹתָן בְּיֹקֶר, וְרוֹצִים שֶׁלֹּא יֵלְדוּ בְּמַלְכוּת אַחֶרֶת כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ צְרִיכִים לָהֶם, וְחוֹתְכִים הָאֵם שֶׁלָּהּ וְאֵינָהּ מֵתָה, הִלְכָּךְ לָאו טְרֵפָה הִיא:
