שלא תלד – because their cows and pigs were very prominent and they sell them for a high price , and they want that they will not give birth for another kingdom in order that they will be needed, and they cut/sever their mother and she does not die, therefore, she is not “torn”/unkosher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:12)


**הָלְכָה חֲמוֹרְךָ טַרְפוֹן.** הִפְסַדְתָּ חֲמוֹרְךָ, שֶׁאַתָּה צָרִיךְ לְמָכְרוֹ כְּדֵי לְשַׁלֵּם דְּמֵי הַפָּרָה לִבְעָלֶיהָ:


הלכך חמורך טרפון – you have lost your donkey, for you need to sell it in order to pay the cost of the cow to its owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:4:13)


**שֶׁמֻּמְחֶה אַתָּה.** חֲדָא וְעוֹד קָאָמַר לֵיהּ, חֲדָא דְטוֹעֶה בִּדְבַר מִשְׁנָה הוּא, דְּמַתְנִיתִין הִיא בְּאֵלּוּ טְרֵפוֹת נִטְּלָה הָאֵם שֶׁלָּהּ כְּשֵׁרָה, וְקַיְמָא לָן דְּטוֹעֶה בִּדְבַר מִשְׁנָה חוֹזֵר הַדִּין וְדָנִין אוֹתוֹ כָרָאוּי וְאֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם. וְעוֹד, אִי נַמִּי בְּשִׁקּוּל הַדַּעַת טָעִיתָ דְּאִסּוּרְךָ אִסּוּר וְלֹא מָצֵית לְמִהְדַּר וְנִמְצָא שֶׁאַתָּה הִפְסַדְתּוֹ, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי פָּטוּר אַתָּה, לְפִי שֶׁאַתָּה מֻמְחֶה. וְשִׁקּוּל הַדַּעַת, הַיְנוּ כְגוֹן תְּרֵי תַנָּאֵי אוֹ תְּרֵי אֲמוֹרָאֵי דִּפְלִיגֵי וְלֹא אִתְּמַר הִלְכָתָא לֹא כְמַר וְלֹא כְמַר, וְסוּגְיָן דְּעָלְמָא כְּחַד מִנַּיְהוּ, וַאֲזַל הוּא וַעֲבַד כְּאִידָךְ, מַה שֶּׁעָשָׂה עָשׂוּי וּמְשַׁלֵּם מִבֵּיתוֹ:


שמומחה אתה – one and another thing he said to him, one – that he error in this matter of a Mishnah, for our Mishnah in [the third chapter of Tractate Hullin] “These are the torn animals’ [Mishnah 2], you took its mother which was kosher and we hold that he errs in the matter of the Mishnah, and the former argument is to be reinstated and we judge him appropriately and he does not pay. And furthermore, alternatively, and the weighing of opinions [of opposing views] – that is, as for example, two Tannaim or two Amoraim who argue, and the Halakha is not stated either according to this Master or according to that Master and the general practice is like one of them, and he went and acted like the other one, what is done is done and he pays from his estate.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:1)


**אֵין שׁוֹחֲטִין עַל פִּיו.** דְּדִלְמָא מִשּׁוּם אַגְרָא קָא שָׁרֵי לֵיהּ:


אין שוחטין על פיו – for perhaps because of reward he permits it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:2)


**כְּאִילָא.** שֵׁם חָכָם הַדָּר בְּיַבְנֶה. וְחָסִיד הָיָה וְלֹא נֶחֱשַׁד בְּכָךְ. וּבֵין אָמַר לְהוּ תָמִים וּבֵין אָמַר לְהוּ בַּעַל מוּם הָיָה נוֹטֵל כָּל שְׂכָרוֹ:


כאילא – the name of a Sage that lived in Yavneh, and he was a pious individual and was not suspected of this, and whether he said to them that it (i.e., the firstling) was pure or whether he said to them that it had a blemish, he would take all of his salary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:3)


**אַרְבַּע אִסָּרוֹת בִּבְהֵמָה דַקָּה וּבְגַסָּה שִׁשָּׁה.** גַּסָּה נְפִישׁ טִרְחָא, לְהַשְׁלִיכָהּ לָאָרֶץ וְלִכְפּוֹתָהּ וְלִבְדֹּק מוּמָהּ, הִלְכָּךְ שְׂכָרָהּ מְרֻבֶּה:


ארבע איסרות בבהמה דקה ובגסה ששה – a large [animal] increases the trouble involved, to cast it to the ground and to turn it upside down and to check its blemishes. Therefore, its payment is greater.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:5:4)


**בֵּין תָּמִים בֵּין בַּעַל מוּם.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּכִי אָמַר לֵיהּ תָּם הוּא לֹא אַהֲנֵי לֵיהּ וְלֹא מִידִי, נוֹטֵל שְׂכָרוֹ, דְּאִי לֹא שָׁקֵיל אַגְרָא אָתוּ לְמִחְשְׁדֵיהּ כִּי אָמַר בַּעַל מוּם הוּא. וּמִכָּאן דָּן הָרַמְבַּ"ם עַל הַשּׁוֹחֲטִים לָרַבִּים, שֶׁרָאוּי לָהֶם אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִטְּלוּ שָׂכָר כְּלָל אוֹ שֶׁיִּטְּלוּ שָׂכָר עַל הַנִּמְצָאוֹת נְבֵלָה אוֹ טְרֵפָה כְּמוֹ עַל הַנִּמְצָאוֹת כְּשֵׁרָה, דּוּמְיָא דְרוֹאֵי מוּמֵי הַבְּכוֹר:


בין תמים בין בעל מום – and even though that when he tells him that it is ritually pure, he does not benefit from him at all, and he takes his salary, for if he did not take his payment/reward , they wold come to suspect him that he said that it (i.e., the animal) has a blemish, and from here, Maimonides judges on those who slaughter [animals] for the public, for it is appropriate for them either that they should not take a salary at all, or that they should take a salary on the finding of [animals] that died of themselves or were torn, like those that are found in the field, similar to those who see the blemishes in firstlings.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:1)


**הַנּוֹטֵל שָׂכָר לָדוּן דִּינָיו בְּטֵלִים.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים ד) רְאֵה לִמַּדְתִּי אֶתְכֶם חֻקִּים וּמִשְׁפָּטִים כַּאֲשֶׁר צִוַּנִי ה', מָה אֲנִי בְּחִנָּם אַף אַתֶּם בְּחִנָּם. וּבְרַבָּנֵי אַשְׁכְּנַז רָאִיתִי שַׁעֲרוּרִיָּה בְּדָבָר זֶה, שֶׁלֹּא יֵבוֹשׁ הָרַב הַנִּסְמָךְ רֹאשׁ יְשִׁיבָה לִטֹּל עֲשָׂרָה זְהוּבִים כְּדֵי לִהְיוֹת חֲצִי שָׁעָה עַל כְּתִיבַת וּנְתִינַת גֵּט אֶחָד, וְהָעֵדִים הַחוֹתְמִים עַל הַגֵּט שְׁנֵי זְהוּבִים אוֹ זָהוּב לְכָל הַפָּחוֹת לְכָל אֶחָד, וְאֵין זֶה הָרַב בְּעֵינַי אֶלָּא גַזְלָן וְאַנָּס, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁאֵין נוֹתְנִים בְּעִירוֹ גֵּט שֶׁלֹּא בִרְשׁוּתוֹ, וְנוֹתֵן הַגֵּט בְּעַל כָּרְחוֹ צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ כָּל חֶפְצוֹ. וְחוֹשֵׁשׁ אֲנִי לְגֵט זֶה שֶׁהוּא פָּסוּל, דְּהָא תְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין הַנּוֹטֵל שָׂכָר לָדוּן דִּינָיו בְּטֵלִין. לְהָעִיד עֵדוּתוֹ בְּטֵלָה:


הנוטל שכר לדון דיניו בטלים – as it is written (Deuteronomy 4:5): “See, I have imparted to you laws and rules as the LORD my God has commanded me, [for you to abide by in the land that you are about to enter and occupy.],” just as I [do it] gratuitously, so you [do them] gratuitously. But among the Rabbis of Ashkenaz, I saw scandalous behavior in this matter , that an ordained Rabbi, the head of a Yeshiva, is not embarrassed to take ten gold coins in order to be [present] for half-an hour for the writing and delivery of one Get/Jewish bill of divorce, and the witnesses that affix their names on the Get [receive] two gold coins or one gold coin for each of them at the very least, and this is not a Rabbi in my eyes, but rather a thief and a violent man who attained it by force, because he knows that we don’t give a Get in his city without his permission, and he who gives the Get against his will needs to give him all that he desires. |But I am worried regarding [this] Get/Jewish bill of divorce that it is invalid, for it is taught in our Mishnah, that a person who takes a reward to judge, his judgments are invalid; to testify, his testimony is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:2)


**לְהַזּוֹת.** מִמֵּי חַטָּאת עַל טְמֵא מֵת:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:3)


**לְקַדֵּשׁ.** לְעָרֵב אֵפֶר חַטָּאת בְּמַיִם חַיִּים אֶל כֶּלִי:


לקדש – to mix the dust of the sin-offering in fresh water in a vessel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:4)


**אֵפֶר מִקְלֶה.** אֵפֶר כִּירָה קְרוּיָה אֵפֶר מִקְלֶה. כְּלוֹמַר אֵפֶר בְּעָלְמָא שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ קְדֻשָּׁה:


אפר מקלה – the ashes of a portable stove on feet is called calcined ashes/hearth ash (i.e., a symbol of mourning, supplication), that is to say, mere ashes that have no sanctity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:5)


**אֲבָל אִם הָיָה.** זֶה רוֹאֶה הַבְּכוֹרוֹת, אוֹ זֶה הַדַּיָּן אוֹ הָעֵד אוֹ הַמְקַדֵּשׁ, כֹּהֵן, וְהֶעֱבִירוֹ הַמּוֹלִיכוֹ עִמּוֹ בִּמְקוֹם טֻמְאָה וְטִמְּאָהוּ מִתְּרוּמָתוֹ, וּמַפְסִידוֹ שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִקְנוֹת חֻלִּין וְלֶאֱכֹל וּדְמֵי חֻלִּין יְקָרִים מִדְּמֵי תְרוּמָה, שֶׁהַחֻלִּין רְאוּיִין לַכֹּל וּתְרוּמָה אֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה אֶלָּא לְכֹהֲנִים טְהוֹרִים:


אבל אם היה – this person who sees/examines the firstlings, or this one who is a judge, or the witness, or the one sanctifies is a Kohen, and the person who goes with him pardons him in the place of defilement and defiled him from consuming his heave offering, and makes him lose out that he needs to acquire non-sacred produce and to eat, and the value of non-consecrated are more expensive than the value of the heave offering, for non-sanctified produce is appropriate for all, but heave offering is not appropriate other than for pure Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:6)


**מַאֲכִילוֹ.** זֶה הַמּוֹלִיכוֹ, וּמַשְׁקֵהוּ וְסָכוֹ:


מאכילו – this one who brings him gives him to drink and anoints him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:6:7)


**וְנוֹתֵן לוֹ שְׂכָרוֹ כְפוֹעֵל.** אִם הָיָה רָגִיל בִּמְלָאכָה כְבֵדָה וְקָשָׁה וּמַרְוִיחַ בָּהּ הַרְבֵּה, אוֹמְדִים כַּמָּה אָדָם כָּזֶה רוֹצֶה לִטֹּל פָּחוֹת מִמַּה שֶּׁהָיָה מַרְוִיחַ בִּמְלָאכָה כְבֵדָה וּלְהִתְעַסֵּק בִּמְלָאכָה זוֹ שֶׁהִיא קַלָּה, וְכָךְ נוֹתֵן לוֹ. וְכֵן מֻתָּר לְכָל דַּיָּן לִטֹּל שְׂכַר בַּטָּלָה כְּשֶׁהַבַּטָּלָה נִכֶּרֶת וּמְפֻרְסֶמֶת, וְלוֹקֵחַ מִשְּׁנֵי בַעֲלֵי הַדִּין בְּשָׁוֶה. וְיוֹתֵר מִזֶּה אָסוּר:


ונותן לו שכרו כפועל – if he was customarily [engaged] in heavy and difficult labor, and he earns a great deal, we estimate how much a person like this wishes to take that is less than one he would earn with heavy labor and to be engaged in this work which is easier, and such we give him,. For it is permitted fo each judge to take a salary for his idleness, for the idleness is known and recognized/familiar, and he takes from the two litigants equally, but more than this is forbidden.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:1)


**הֶחָשׁוּד עַל הַבְּכוֹרוֹת.** כֹּהֵן הֶחָשׁוּד לְהַטִּיל מוּם בַּבְּכוֹר:


החשוד על הבכורות – a Kohen who is suspected of placing/attaching a blemish on a firstling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:2)


**בְּשַׂר צְבָאִים.** שֶׁאָדֹם הוּא וּמַחֲלִיף בִּבְשַׂר עֵגֶל, וְזִמְנִין דִּמְזַבֵּן בְּכוֹר עֵגֶל תָּמִים וְאוֹמֵר שֶׁבְּשַׂר צְבִי הוּא, דְּאֵין לָחוּשׁ לִבְכוֹרָה:


בשר צבאים – for it (i.e., deer/gazelle meat) is red and he switches it with the meat of a calf, and sometimes, when he sells the firstling of a pure calf and he said that it is the meat of a deer/gazelle, where we aren’t troubled by a firstling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:3)


**וְלֹא עוֹרוֹת שֶׁאֵינָן עֲבוּדִים.** אֲבָל עֲבוּדִים זָבְנִינַן מִנֵּיהּ, דְּאִי אִיתָא דִּבְכוֹר הֲוָה לֹא הֲוָה טָרַח בֵּיהּ, סָבַר שָׁמְעֵי בֵי רַבָּנָן וּמַפְסְדֵי לֵיהּ מִנַּאי:


ולא עורות שאינן עבודים – but of tanned [hides] we buy from him, for if it is a firstling, he would not trouble himself with it, for he holds that the house of the Rabbis will hear of it and will cause him to lose [money] from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:4)


**לוֹקְחִים מִמֶּנּוּ עוֹרוֹת שֶׁל נְקֵבָה.** דְּמֵידַע יְדִיעֵי. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָסַר, סָבַר דִּלְמָא חָתֵיךְ לְזַכְרוּתֵיהּ וַעֲבִיד בֵּיהּ כְּמִין נַקְבוּת, וְכִי מְשַׁיְּלֵי לֵיהּ מַה חִתּוּךְ זֶה שֶׁבִּמְקוֹם נַקְבוּת, אוֹמֵר עַכְבָּרִים אֲכָלוּהוּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


לוקחים ממנו עורות של נקבה - - for they certainly will know it But the first Tanna/teacher who prohibits it, holds that perhaps that they sever its male genitals and treat it like the females, and when they asked him what is this incision in the place of the female genitals, he would state that the mice consumed it. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:5)


**מְלֻבָּן וְצוֹאִי.** פֵּרְשׁוּ בַגְּמָרָא דִמְלֻבָּן מִצּוֹאִי קָאָמַר, כְּלוֹמַר מְרֻחָץ מִצּוֹאָתוֹ. וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן אִי דִבְכוֹר הוּא לֹא מַפְסִיד טִרְחֵיהּ וְלֹא מְלַבֵּן לֵיהּ דְּסָבַר שָׁמְעֵי רַבָּנָן וּמַפְסְדֵי לֵיהּ מִנַּאי, דְּכֵיוָן דְּטִרְחֵיהּ זוּטָא הוּא טָרַח וּמְלַבֵּן לֵיהּ וְלֹא קָפֵיד עֲלֵיהּ:


מלובן וצואי – they explained in the Gemara (Talmud Bekhorot 29b) that it states: wool that is cleansed of its dirt, that is to say, that he washes from its filthy matter . But we don’t say that if it if is a firstling, he does not lose his troubles/efforts and it doesn’t glisten for he holds that the Rabbis heard and causes him to lose from it, for since he troubles a small amount but is not exacting with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:6)


**אֲבָל לוֹקְחִין מִמֶּנּוּ טָווּי וּבְגָדִים.** דְּאִי דִבְכוֹר הוּא כֻלֵּי הַאי לֹא טָרַח בְּהוּ, דְּקָפֵיד אַטִּרְחֵיהּ דִּלְמָא מַפְסְדֵי לֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ:


אבל לוחקין ממנו טווי ובגדים – for if it is firstling, he would not go to the trouble all that much with them, for he stringent with his trouble for perhaps he will suffer loss from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:7:7)


**טָווּי וּבְגָדִים.** לָאו בְּגָדִים מַמָּשׁ קָאָמַר. דְּהַשְׁתָּא טָווּי גְּרֵידָא לוֹקְחִין מִמֶּנּוּ, בְּגָדִים אֲרוּגִים מִבַּעְיָא. אֶלָּא בְגָדִים הַיְנוּ לְבָדִים עֲשׂוּיִים מִצֶּמֶר שֶׁאֵינָן טְווּיִין:


טווי ובגדים – he is not saying actual clothing. For now that is merely spun, they will purchase from him, but he needed woven clothing, but rather, clothing, that is linen made from wool which is not woven.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:8:1)


**הֶחָשׁוּד עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית.** לִזְרֹעַ אוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת סְחוֹרָה בִּסְפִיחֵי שְׁבִיעִית:


החשוד על השביעית – to sow or to do business with the after-growths of the seventh year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:8:2)


**סָרָק.** מְתֻקָּן בְּמַסְרֵק. דְּכֵיוָן דְּטִרְחֵיהּ זוּטָא לֹא קָפֵיד:


סרק – that is prepared with a comb, for since his trouble is little/small, he is not stringent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:8:3)


**אָרִיג.** לָאו אָרִיג מַמָּשׁ. דְּהַשְׁתָּא טָווּי שָׁרֵי אָרִיג מִבַּעְיָא, הֲרֵי בָא לִכְלַל טָווּי קֹדֶם לָכֵן. אֶלָּא אָרִיג הַיְנוּ כְעֵין שַׁרְשְׁרוֹת שֶׁעוֹשִׂין מִפִּשְׁתָּן שֶׁלֹּא נִטְוָה מֵעוֹלָם:


אריג – not actually woven, for now that which is spun is permitted, we require woven. For this comes within the category of spun before it. But rather, woven, that is by the eye, twisted chains that they make from flax that were never spun.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:9:1)


**אֲפִלּוּ מַיִם וּמֶלַח.** מִשּׁוּם קְנָס:


אפילו מים ומלח – and because of a fine [they don’t purchase water or salt from him].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:9:2)


**כֹּל שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ זִקַּת תְּרוּמוֹת.** כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁתְּרוּמָה נוֹהֶגֶת בּוֹ. וְכָל, לַאֲתוֹיֵי קִרְבֵי דָגִים שֶׁמְּעָרְבִין בָּהֶן שֶׁמֶן זַיִת, שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ זִיקַת תְּרוּמָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


כל שיש בו זיקת תרומה – every thing that the heave offering is used for. And “all” includes the insides of fish that they combine with them olive oil that has the obligation to pay heave offering. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:10:1)


**אֵינוֹ חָשׁוּד עַל הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת.** וְלוֹקְחִים מִמֶּנּוּ תְבוּאָה בִּשְׁאָר שְׁנֵי הַשָּׁבוּעַ, וְאֵין מְעַשְּׂרִין מֵהֶן וַדַּאי אֶלָּא דְמַאי. וְטַעֲמָא דְחָשׁוּד עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית אֵין חָשׁוּד עַל הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת וְחָשׁוּד עַל הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת אֵין חָשׁוּד עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית, לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּכָל אֶחָד מֵהֶן חֹמֶר שֶׁאֵינוֹ בַּחֲבֵרוֹ, שְׁבִיעִית אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לְהֵאָכֵל לִפְנִים מֵחוֹמַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, וּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי אֵינוֹ נֶאֱכָל אֶלָּא לִפְנִים מִן הַחוֹמָה, הִלְכָּךְ אִיכָּא דַחֲמִיר לֵיהּ עֹנֶשׁ מַעֲשֵׂר מֵעֹנֶשׁ שְׁבִיעִית. וּמַעֲשֵׂר אִית לֵיהּ פִּדְיוֹן, וּשְׁבִיעִית כֵּיוָן שֶׁנֶּאֶסְרָה אֵין לָהּ פִּדְיוֹן, הִלְכָּךְ אִיכָּא דַחֲמִיר עֲלֵיהּ שְׁבִיעִית מִמַּעֲשֵׂר:


אינו חשוד על המעשרות – and they purchase from him grain in the rest of the years of the Seven Year cycle, but they don’t tithe from them as definitively [tithed] but as a doubtfully tithed. And the reason is that he who is suspected [of violating] the Sabbatical Year is not suspected on account of tithes, and one who is suspected on account of tithes is not suspected on account of the Seventh Year [produce], because there is is in each of hen material that is not part of its partner; Seventh Year produce does not have be consumed inside the walls of Jerusalem, but Second Tithe is not consumed other than within/inside the wall [of Jerusalem]. Therefore, there is a more stringent punishment regarding [Second] Tithe than from the punishment of [not consuming] the Sabbatical Year produce. For the [Second] Tithe has redemption, but since the Seventh Year [produce] is forbidden, it does not have redemption, therefore, the Seventh Year is more stringent than from the [Second] Tithe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:10:2)


**הֶחָשׁוּד עַל זֶה וְעַל זֶה.** כֵּיוָן דְּחָשׁוּד אַדְּאוֹרַיְתָא כָּל שֶׁכֵּן דְּחָשׁוּד עַל הַטָּהֳרוֹת, דְּמִדְּרַבָּנָן הוּא שֶׁיְּהֵא אָדָם אוֹכֵל חֻלִּין בְּטָהֳרָה:


החשוד על זה ועל זה – since he is suspected [of violating] a Torah prohibition all the more so, that he is suspect [of violating] on the purities, for they are from the Rabbis, that a person eats his unconsecrated produce in ritual purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/4:10:3)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁחָשׁוּד עַל הַטָּהֳרוֹת.** דְּרַבָּנָן, וְאֵינוֹ חָשׁוּד לִשְׁבִיעִית וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת. דְּמַאן דְּחָשׁוּד אַדְּרַבָּנָן לֹא חָשׁוּד אַדְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


ויש שחשוד על הטהרות – that are of the Rabbis, but is not suspected on account of Seventh Year produce and [Second] Tithe. For one that is suspected on a Rabbinic [legislation] is not suspected [of violation] of a Torah prohibition.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:1)


**כָּל פְּסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁין.** קָדָשִׁים שֶׁנָּפַל בָּהֶם מוּם, אִם מוֹכְרִין אוֹתָן בְּיֹקֶר, טוֹבַת הֲנָאָה לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ הוּא. הִלְכָּךְ נִמְכָּרִים בָּאִטְלִיז, דְּהַיְנוּ שׁוּק שֶׁמּוֹכְרִים בּוֹ שְׁאָר בְּשַׂר חֻלִּין וְשָׁם נִמְכָּר בְּיֹקֶר:


כל פסולי המוקדשין (all consecrated animals that are disqualified) – Holy Things that had a blemish befall them, if we sell them at a high price, it is a benefit of discretion to Temple property. Therefore, they are sold in the bazaar/shop, that is the marketplace where they sell in the rest of the unconsecrated meat and there it is sold at a high price.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:2)


**וְנִשְׁקָלִין בְּלִטְרָא.** לְהִמָּכֵר כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהַקַּצָּבִים מוֹכְרִים בְּשַׂר חֻלִּין. דְּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁיְּכוֹלִים לִמְכֹּר בְּיֹקֶר מוֹסִיפִים בַּדָּמִים כְּשֶׁפּוֹדִים אוֹתָם מִן הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ:


ונשקלין בליטרא – to be sold in the manner that the butchers sell unconsecrated meat, for since they are able to sell it at a high price, they add to its worth when they redeem them from Temple property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:3)


**חוּץ מִן הַבְּכוֹר וּמִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר.** שֶׁאִם נִמְכָּרִים בְּיֹקֶר הֲנָאָתָן לַבְּעָלִים. בְּכוֹר הֲנָאָתוֹ לַכֹּהֵן, דִּבְשַׂר בְּכוֹר נֶאֱכָל לְכָל אָדָם וְהַכֹּהֵן מוֹכְרוֹ וְנוֹטֵל דָּמָיו, וְלַכֹּהֵן קָרֵי בְּעָלִים שֶׁל בְּכוֹר. וּמִשּׁוּם הֲנָאַת הֶדְיוֹט לֹא מְזַלְזְלִינַן בַּקָּדָשִׁים לִנְהֹג בָּהֶן מִנְהַג חֻלִּין לְמָכְרָן בָּאִטְלִיז, אֶלָּא בְּבֵיתוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא יִקְפְּצוּ עֲלֵיהֶם בְּנֵי אָדָם כָּל כָּךְ. וְאֵין נִשְׁקָלִים בְּלִטְרָא אֶלָּא בְאֹמֶד, וְאִם יַפְסִיד לֵית לָן בָּהּ:


חוץ מן הבכור ומן המעשר – that if they are sold at a high price their benefit is to owners. The firstling, its benefit is to the Kohen, for the meat of a firstling is consumed by everyone, but the Kohen sells it and takes its monetary worth, for the Kohen is called the owners of the firstling. But because of the benefit of the regular individual/commoner, we do not treat lightly Holy Things to treat them with the custom of unconsecrated things to sell them in a bazaar/shop, but rather in his house, and even though people will not cut the meat so much. But they are not measured with a litra but rather by estimation, and if he loses [a bit], it does not matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:1:4)


**וְשׁוֹקְלִין מָנָה כְנֶגֶד מָנָה בַּבְּכוֹר.** שֶׁאִם יֵשׁ לוֹ חֲתִיכַת בְּשַׂר חֻלִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁקְלָה בְּלִטְרָא יָכוֹל לִשְׁקֹל בְּשַׂר בְּכוֹר כְּנֶגְדָּהּ. אֲבָל מַעֲשֵׂר אֵין שׁוֹקְלִים מָנָה כְּנֶגֶד מָנָה, דְּמֵחֲזֵי כְאִלּוּ מוֹכְרוֹ, וּבֶהֱמַת מַעֲשֵׂר אָסוּר לְמָכְרָהּ כְּלָל מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא כְתִיב בָּהּ לֹא יִפָּדֶה כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁכָּתוּב בַּבְּכוֹר אֶלָּא לֹא יִגָּאֵל, וְשָׁנִינוּ בְּסִפְרִי בְּכוֹר שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ לֹא יִפָּדֶה, הוּא נִמְכָּר, מַעֲשֵׂר שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ לֹא יִגָּאֵל אֵינוֹ נִמְכָּר לֹא חַי וְלֹא שָׁחוּט וְלֹא תָמִים וְלֹא בַעַל מוּם:


ושוקלין מנה כנגד מנה – that if he has a piece of unconsecrated meat that is weighted in the litra, he can weigh the meat of a firstling against it. But tithes we don’t weigh a Maneh against a Maneh, for at it appears as if he sells it, and the tithe of cattle it is prohibited to sell it at all because it is not written regarding it (see Numbers 18:17): “[But the firstlings of cattle, sheep, or goats] may not be redeemed,” in the manner that it is written regarding a firstling but rather (Leviticus 27:33): “[If he does make substitution for it, then it and its substitute shall both be holy:] it cannot be redeemed/לא יגאל,” and we taught in Sifrei [B’midbar] that regarding a firstling where it states “it cannot be ransomed,” tithes, where it states concerning it, “it shall not be redeemed”, is no sold, neither living nor slaughtered, and not pure and not with a blemish.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:1)


**לֹא יִמָּנֶה יִשְׂרָאֵל עִם הַכֹּהֵן.** לֶאֱכֹל עִמּוֹ בַּחֲבוּרָה מִבְּשַׂר בְּכוֹר בַּעַל מוּם. שֶׁאֵין נִמְנִין עַל הַבְּכוֹר אֶלָּא חֲבוּרָה שֶׁכֻּלָּהּ כֹּהֲנִים, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) וּבְשָׂרָם יִהְיֶה לָּךָ כַּחֲזֵה הַתְּנוּפָה וּכְשׁוֹק הַיָּמִין, מָה חָזֶה וְשׁוֹק כֹּהֲנִים אִין יִשְׂרָאֵל לֹא, אַף בְּכוֹר בֵּין תָּמִים בֵּין בַּעַל מוּם כֹּהֲנִים אִין יִשְׂרָאֵל לֹא:


לא ימנה ישראל עם הכהן – to eat with him in an association from the meat of a firstling with a blemish, for they not numbered regarding the firstling other than in an association that is entirely Kohanim [alone], as it is written (Numbers 18:18): “But their meat shall be yours: it shall be yours like the breast of elevation offering and like the right thigh,” for just as the breast and the thigh are for the Kohanim [only], and not for the Israelite, even the firstling, whether pure or with a blemish, are for the Kohanim [only] and not for the Israelite.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:2)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מַתִּירִין וַאֲפִלּוּ נָכְרִי.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים יב) כַּצְּבִי וְכָאַיָּל, מַה צְּבִי וְאַיָּל אֲפִלּוּ נָכְרִי, אַף בְּכוֹר נַמִּי. וּקְרָא דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) וּבְשָׂרָם יִהְיֶה לָּךָ כַּחֲזֵה הַתְּנוּפָה וּכְשׁוֹק הַיָּמִין, בִּבְכוֹר תָּמִים דַּוְקָא מִשְׁתַּעֵי:


ובית הלל מתירין ואפילו נכרי – as it is written (Deuteronomy 12:15): “[The impure and the pure alike may partake of it,] as of the gazelle and the deer,” just as the gazelle and the deer, even a heathen [may partake of it], even to the firstling also. And Scripture [teaches] as it is written (Numbers 18:18): “But their meat shall be yours:…like the breast of elevation offering and like the right thigh,” and it is speaking specifically about the pure firstling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:3)


**שֶׁאֲחָזוֹ דָם.** חֹלִי שֶׁהוּא מִסְתַּכֵּן בּוֹ מֵרִבּוּי הַדָּם:


שאחזו דם – a sick [animal] who is danger from excess blood [letting].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:4)


**אֵין מַקִּיזִין לוֹ דָם.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה בּוֹ מוּם. דְּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁאָדָם בָּהוּל עַל מָמוֹנוֹ, אִי שָׁרֵית לֵיהּ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין עוֹשֶׂה בוֹ מוּם אָתֵי לְמֶעֱבַד בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה בּוֹ מוּם:


אין מקיזין לו דם – and even in a place where he does not make in it a blemish. For since a person is pressed (see Tractate Pesahim 11a) when his money is at stake, if you permitted him in a place where he does not make a blemish, he would come to make it in a place where one makes in it a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:5)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂה בוֹ מוּם.** שֶׁלֹּא יִסְדֹּק רֹאשׁ אָזְנוֹ אוֹ נִיב שְׂפָתָיו בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל לַחֲזֹר וּלְהֵרָפֵא. דְּסָבְרֵי רַבָּנָן כָּל שֶׁכֵּן דְּאִי לֹא שָׁרֵית לֵיהּ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מוּם אָתֵי לְמֶעֱבַד אֲפִלּוּ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ מוּם:


ובלבד שלא יעשה בו מום – that he will not tear apart the top of his ear or the top of its lips in the place where it is not able to return and be healed, the Rabbis hold all the more so that if you did not permit him in a place where there is no blemish, he will come to make it even in a place where it has a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:6)


**לֹא יִשָּׁחֵט עָלָיו.** הוֹאִיל וְהוּא הִטִּילוֹ. עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ מוּם אַחֵר:


לא ישחוט עליו – for since and he placed in it [a blemish] until another blemish befalls it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:2:7)


**יַקִּיז.** אַף בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה בּוֹ מוּם, אִם הוּא צָרִיךְ לְהַקִּיז מֵאוֹתוֹ אֵבָר. וְלֹא יַנִּיחֶנּוּ שֶׁיָּמוּת. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא הִטִּיל הַמּוּם בְּיָדָיו יִשָּׁחֵט עָלָיו. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


יקיז – [he should draw blood] even in the place where he makes a blemish in it, even he needs to let blood from the same limb and not leave it until it dies. But even though he made a blemish with his hands, he should slaughter it. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:1)


**הַצּוֹרֵם.** הַפּוֹגֵם. וּבְכֹהֵן אַיְרֵי, שֶׁפּוֹגֵם אֹזֶן הַבְּכוֹר כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה חֻלִּין בְּיָדוֹ:


הצורם (he who makes a slit) – he mutilates/disqualifies. And we are speaking of a Kohen, who slits the ear of the firstling in order that it will be unconsecrated in his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:2)


**הֲרֵי זֶה לֹא יִשָּׁחֵט עוֹלָמִית.** וַאֲפִלּוּ נָפַל בּוֹ מוּם אַחֵר. מִשּׁוּם קְנָס, לְפִי שֶׁעָבַר וְהִטִּיל מוּם בַּקָּדָשִׁים. שֶׁהַמַּטִּיל מוּם בַּקָּדָשִׁים סוֹפֵג אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים, וַאֲפִלּוּ עָשָׂה מוּם בְּבַעַל מוּם. דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) כָּל מוּם לֹא יִהְיֶה בּוֹ, קָרֵי בֵּיהּ לֹא יְהַיֶּה בּוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יַטִּיל בּוֹ מוּם. וּמִדַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ לְמִכְתַּב מוּם וְכָתַב כָּל מוּם, לְרַבּוֹת אֲפִלּוּ בַּעַל מוּם שֶׁלֹּא יַטִּיל בּוֹ מוּם:


הרי זה לא ישחט עולמית – and even if another blemish befell it, because of a fine, because he transgressed and placed a blemish on Holy Things. For a person who places a blemish on Holy Things is flogged forty times (i.e., minus one), and even if he made a blemish upon a blemish. As it is written (Leviticus 22:21): “there must be no defect in it,” it reads, “will not be in it,” that he will not place in it a defect. For it could have been written, “a defect,” but it is written, "כל מום"/”there must be no defect,” to include even a defect that he did not place upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:3)


**כְּשֶׁיִּוָּלֵד לוֹ מוּם אַחֵר יִשְׁחֹט עָלָיו.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְאוֹתוֹ מוּם עַצְמוֹ, אִם מֵת הַמַּטִּיל אֶת הַמּוּם, בְּנוֹ שׁוֹחֵט אַחֲרָיו עַל אוֹתוֹ הַמּוּם. דִּלְדִידֵיהּ קָנְסוּ רַבָּנָן, לִבְרֵיהּ לֹא קָנְסוּ רַבָּנָן. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


כשיולד לו מום אחר ישחט עליו – and even on the same defect itself. If it person who placed the defect on it died, his son slaughters it after him on that defect, but the Rabbis fined him (i.e., the original person who placed he defect on the animal) but the Rabbis did not fine his son. And such is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:4)


**וּשְׂעָרוֹ מְדֻלְדָּל.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא נִגְזַז מֵעוֹלָם:


ושערו מדולדל – that it was never sheared.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:5)


**קַסְדּוֹר.** מְמֻנֶּה מֵהַמֶּלֶךְ:


קסדור (quaestor) – appointed by the king.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:6)


**מַה טִּיבוֹ שֶׁל זֶה.** שֶׁהִנִּיחוּהוּ לְהַזְקִין כָּל כָּךְ:


מה טיבו של זה- that he left it to be wounded/damaged so much.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:7)


**פִּגְיוֹן.** סַכִּין שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי פִּיּוֹת קָרוּי פִּגְיוֹן:


פגיון – a knife that has two mouths is called a dagger.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:8)


**וְהִתִּירוּהוּ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַנָּכְרִי נִתְכַּוֵּן לְהַטִּיל בּוֹ מוּם, כֵּיוָן דְּשֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעַת יִשְׂרָאֵל עָשָׂה, שֶׁלֹּא נִתְכַּוֵּן לַעֲשׂוֹת נַחַת רוּחַ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


והתירוהו – even though that the heathen intended to place upon it a blemish. Since that it was without the knowledge of an Israelite/Jew that he did it, he didn’t intend to do something beneficial to a Jew.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:9)


**רָאָה שֶׁהִתִּירוּ וְהָלַךְ וְצָרַם בְּאָזְנֵי בְכוֹרוֹת אֲחֵרִים.** כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת נַחַת רוּחַ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. נַעֲשָׂה כְאִלּוּ אָמַר לוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיַּעֲשֵׂהוּ וְאָסוּר:


ראה שהתירוהו והלך וצרם באזני בכורות אחרים – in order to do something beneficial to a Jew, it was done as if the Jew had said to him that he should do it and it is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:10)


**הָיוּ תִינוֹקוֹת מְשַׂחֲקִים.** וּצְרִיכָא לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן קַסְדּוֹר נָכְרִי וְתִינוֹקוֹת. דְּאִי אַשְׁמוֹעִינַן קַסְדּוֹר, הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא בְנָכְרִי הוּא דְשָׁרֵי דְּלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְמִסְרַךְ וְלִלְמֹד לְהַטִּיל מוּם בַּקָּדָשִׁים, דְּסִרְכֵיהּ דְּנָכְרִי לֹא אִכְפַּת לָן שֶׁהֲרֵי נָהוּג הוּא בְאִסּוּרִין, אֲבָל קָטָן דְּאִי שָׁרֵית לֵיהּ אָתֵי לְמִסְרַךְ אֵימָא לֹא. וְאִי אַשְׁמוֹעִינַן תִּינוֹקוֹת הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא תִינוֹק הוּא דְּשָׁרוּ רַבָּנָן, דְּמַאן דְּחָזֵי לֹא אָתֵי לְמֵימַר נַמִּי אִי הֲוָה שָׁדֵי בֵיהּ גָּדוֹל מוּמָא הֲוָה מִשְׁתְּרֵי, דְּקָטָן בְּגָדוֹל לֹא מִחַלַּף, אֲבָל נָכְרִי גָדוֹל דְּאָתֵי לְאִחַלּוֹפֵי בְגָדוֹל יִשְׂרָאֵל אֵימָא לֹא, צְרִיכֵי:


היו תינוקות משחקות – and it is necessary to inform us about a heathen quaestor and young children. For if it had taught [only] the quaestor, I might think that it was a heathen that was permitted for one cannot make a decree for perhaps he comes to hand it over to you and to learn how to make a blemish in Holy Things, for we do not care that it is the habit of heathens for it is their custom in forbidden thigs, but a minor, if he permitted him, he would come to hand it over to you, I would say no. But if we taught only regarding young children, I would think that it was a young child that the Rabbis permitted, for one that is appropriate would not come to state also that an adult who cast upon it a blemish would be permitted, for a minor and an adult are not confused/interchangeable, but an adult heathen who comes to switch with an adult Israelite, I would say no. Both are necessary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:11)


**כֹּל שֶׁהוּא לְדַעְתּוֹ אָסוּר.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי גְרָמָא. כְּגוֹן שֶׁיּוֹלִיךְ הַבְּהֵמָה בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ בַּרְזֶל כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּכָּשֵׁל בּוֹ וְיִפֹּל בָּהּ מוּם:


כל שהוא לדעתו אסור – to include an indirect effect. As for example, that he would cause the animal to walk in a place where there is iron in order that it would trip upon it and a blemish would befall it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:3:12)


**וְשֶׁלֹּא לְדַעְתּוֹ מֻתָּר.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי אִם הָיָה יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵסִיחַ לְפִי תֻּמּוֹ וְאוֹמֵר בִּפְנֵי הַנָּכְרִי בְּכוֹר זֶה אִם נָפַל בּוֹ מוּם הָיִינוּ אוֹכְלִים אוֹתוֹ, וְשָׁמַע הַנָּכְרִי וְהִטִּיל בּוֹ מוּם, מֻתָּר:


ושלא לדעתו מותר – to include that if the Israelite/Jew was making a statement incidentally (i.e., in ignorance of its legal bearing), and states in the presence of the heathen that this firstling if a blemish befell it, that is that we consume it. And the heathen heard and placed upon it a blemish, it is permitted.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:1)


**הָיָה בְכוֹר רוֹדְפוֹ כוּ'‏ הֲרֵי זֶה יִשְׁחֹט עָלָיו.** וְלֹא שָׁנוּ אֶלָּא שֶׁבְּעָטוֹ בִּשְׁעַת רְדִיפָה, אֲבָל שֶׁלֹּא בִּשְׁעַת רְדִיפָה לֹא:


היה בכור רודפו כו' הרי זה ישחט עליו – but the law only applies when he kicked it (i.e., the animal) at the time that of the chase [by the animal of the person], but not when it was not at the time of the chase.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:2)


**הָרְאוּיִין לָבֹא בִידֵי אָדָם.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹמַר אָדָם הִטִּילָם בּוֹ. כְּגוֹן נִסְמֵית עֵינוֹ, נִקְטְעָה יָדוֹ, נִסְדְּקָה אָזְנוֹ:


הראויין לבוא בידי אדם – that one can say that a person make them (i.e., the blemishes) in it (i.e., the animal), such blinding its eye, cutting off his hand, or slitting its ear.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:3)


**רוֹעִים יִשְׂרָאֵל נֶאֱמָנִים.** יִשְׂרָאֵל הָרוֹעֶה בְּהֶמְתּוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן נֶאֱמָן לוֹמַר עַל הַמּוּמִין שֶׁנָּפְלוּ בִּבְהֶמְתּוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן שֶׁמֵאֲלֵיהֶם אֵרְעוּ וְיֹאכְלֶנּוּ בְּעָלָיו הַכֹּהֵן בְּמוּמוֹ. וְלֹא חָשְׁדִינַן לֵיהּ שֶׁמָּא עַל יְדֵי אָדָם בָּא זֶה הַמּוּם בְּכַוָּנָה וְרוֹעֶה זֶה מְשַׁקֵּר וּבָא לְהַתִּירוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּשְׁחָטֶנּוּ רַבּוֹ כֹּהֵן וְיַאֲכִילֶנּוּ מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁהַיִּשְׂרָאֵל מֻתָּר לֶאֱכֹל מִן הַבְּכוֹר בַּעַל מוּם כְּשֶׁהַכֹּהֵן נוֹתֵן לוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ, בְּהָכִי לֹא חָשְׁדִינַן לֵיהּ, דְּלִלְגִימָה מֻעֶטֶת כָּזוֹ לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁיְּשַׁקֵּר הָרוֹעֶה וְיַעֲבֹר עֲבֵרָה בַּעֲבוּר הֲנָאַת לְגִימָה בִּלְבַד:


רועים ישראל נאמנים – a Jew who is shepherd of the cattle belonging to a Kohen is believed to state o the blemishes that befell the Kohen’s cattle that they happened on its own, and the Kohen, its owner can consume it with its blemish. But we do not suspect him that perhaps through a person this blemish came intentionally and that this shepherd is lying and he comes to permit it in order that his master the Kohen will slaughter it and feed him from it, for an Israelite/Jew is permitted to eat from the firstling with a blemish when the Kohen gives him [food] from it, for in this we do not suspect him, for a small amount of quaffing like this, we do not suspect that the shepherd would lie and transgress the sin in order for this small quaffing alone (see Talmud Bekhorot 35a – that they are so dependent upon their employers for their bread and butter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:4)


**רוֹעִים כֹּהֲנִים.** רוֹעִים שֶׁהֵם כֹּהֲנִים: **אֵין נֶאֱמָנִים.** כְּשֶׁרוֹעִים בְּהֶמְתָּן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל. דְּנֶחֱשָׁד הַאי כֹּהֵן רוֹעֶה שֶׁהוּא עַצְמוֹ הִטִּיל בּוֹ מוּם, דְּמֵימַר אָמַר הַאי רוֹעֶה, לֹא שָׁבֵיק רַבִּי לְדִידִי וְיַהֲבֵיהּ לְכֹהֵן אַחֲרִינָא. וְהוּא הַדִּין דְּכֹהֵן רוֹעֶה אֵין נֶאֱמָן לְהָעִיד עַל הַבְּכוֹר שֶׁל כֹּהֵן אַחֵר, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן לְגוֹמְלִים, דְּסָבַר אֲעִידֶנּוּ עַכְשָׁיו וִיהֵא גוֹמֵל לִי כְּשֶׁיִּתֵּן לִי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכוֹר תָּם וְאַטִּיל בּוֹ מוּם וְיָבֹא כֹהֵן זֶה וִיעִידֵנִי שֶׁמֵּאֵלָיו נָפַל בּוֹ מוּם:


רועים כהנים – shepherds who are Kohanim, are not believed when they shepherd the cattle of Israelites/Jews. For this Kohen is suspected that he himself placed a blemish in it, that this shepherd would say, My master did not leave it to me and I will give it to an other Kohen. And the same law applies that the Kohen shepherd is not believed to testify on a firstling of another Kohen, for we are suspicious of doing good to each other, for he held that I will testify for him now and he will do do me a favor when an Israelite gives me a pure firstling, and I will place a blemish in it and this Kohen will testify on my behalf that the blemish befell it on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:5)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר נֶאֱמָן הוּא עַל שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ וְאֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן עַל שֶׁל עַצְמוֹ.** רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא, וְסָבַר דְּלֹא נֶחְשְׁדוּ כֹהֲנִים אֶלָּא עַל בְּכוֹר עַצְמוֹ שֶׁכְּבָר נְתָנוֹ לוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל, אֲבָל עַל שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ בֵּין רַבּוֹ בֵּין אַחֵר לֹא נֶחְשְׁדוּ עָלָיו:


רבן גמליאל אומר נאמן הוא על של חבירו ואינו נאמן של עצמו – Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel disagrees with the first Tanna/teacher, for he holds that the Kohanim were not suspected other than on the firstling itself for was already given to him by an Israelite, but on that of his fried, whether, his Master/teacher or another, they were not suspected concerning them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:4:6)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר כָּל הֶחָשׁוּד עַל דָּבָר לֹא דָנוֹ וְלֹא מְעִידוֹ.** וְאִיכָּא בֵּין רַבִּי מֵאִיר לְתַנָּא קַמָּא, דִּלְרַבִּי מֵאִיר בְּכוֹר כֹּהֵן שֶׁנָּפַל בּוֹ מוּם צָרִיךְ שְׁנַיִם מִן הַשּׁוּק לְהָעִיד עָלָיו, וְעֵד אֶחָד אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁאֵינוֹ כֹהֵן אֵין מוֹעִיל בּוֹ. וּלְתַנָּא קַמָּא דַּוְקָא כֹהֵן אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן לְהָעִיד עַל בְּכוֹרוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן לְגוֹמְלִים, אֲבָל שֶׁאֵינוֹ כֹהֵן אֲפִלּוּ אֶחָד מֵעִיד נֶאֱמָן לְהַתִּירוֹ. וְרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר אֲפִלּוּ בָנָיו וּבְנֵי בֵיתוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן נֶאֱמָנִים, דְּדַוְקָא אִיהוּ גוּפֵיהּ עַל שֶׁל עַצְמוֹ אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן. וְאִשְׁתּוֹ נַמִּי אֵינָהּ נֶאֱמֶנֶת מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא כְגוּפוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


רבי מאיר אומר כל החשוד בדבר לא דנו ולא מעידו – and there is a [difference] between the first Tanna/teacher and Rabbi Meir, for according to Rabbi Meir, a firstling belonging to a Kohen that blemish befell it, two from the marketplace have to testify upon it, but one witness, even one who is not a Kohen, does not help. But according to the first Tanna/teacher, especially a Kohen is not believed to testify on the firstling of a Kohen, for we are concerned for doing good to one another, but one who is not a Kohen, even one who testifies is believed to permit it. But Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel states that even the children and the members of the household of a Kohen are believed, for specifically, only on what is his he is not believed. And his wife is also not believed because she is like is body. And the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:1)


**הֶרְאֵיתִי בְכוֹר זֶה.** לְחָכָם, וְאָמַר לִי שֶׁמּוּם קָבוּעַ הוּא, וְשׁוֹחֲטוֹ, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לוֹ עֵדִים שֶׁלֹּא הִטִּילוֹ בוֹ. שֶׁהַכֹּהֲנִים חֲשׁוּדִים לְהַטִּיל מוּם בַּבְּכוֹר כְּדֵי לְמָכְרוֹ וּלְהַאֲכִילוֹ כְּחֻלִּין, אֲבָל אֵין חֲשׁוּדִין לֶאֱכֹל קָדָשִׁים תְּמִימִים בַּחוּץ וְלוֹמַר עַל מוּם עוֹבֵר שֶׁהוּא קָבוּעַ. וְכֹהֵן שֶׁהוּא מֻמְחֶה נֶאֱמָן לָדוּן עַל מוּם הַבְּכוֹר שֶׁהוּא קָבוּעַ וּלְהַתִּירוֹ. וְכֵן נֶאֱמָן הַכֹּהֵן לוֹמַר בְּכוֹר זֶה נָתַן לִי יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמוּמוֹ, שֶׁהוּא עָשׂוּי לְהִגָּלוֹת אִם נְתָנוֹ לוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמוּמוֹ אִם לָאו, וּמִלְּתָא דַעֲבִידָא לְאִגַּלּוֹיֵי לֹא מְשַׁקְּרֵי בָהּ אֱינָשֵׁי:


הראיתי בכור זה – to a Sage. And he said to me that this is a permanent blemish and he slaughters it, as long as he has witnesses that he [himself] did not make the blemish. For the Kohanim are suspected of placing a blemish in a firstling in order to sell it or to consume it as unconsecrated meat, but they are not suspected of consuming pure Holy Things outside [of the Temple courtyard] or to state about a temporary blemish that it is permanent. And a Kohen who is a specialist/expert is believed to adjudicate about the blemish of a firstling that it is permanent and/or to permit it. And similarly, a Kohen is believed to state that an Israelite/Jew gave me this firstling with a blemish and he is supposed to reveal if the Israelite/Jew gave it to him with a blemish or not, and it is a matter of business to reveal that people are not lying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:2)


**הַכֹּל נֶאֱמָנִים עַל מוּמֵי הַמַּעֲשֵׂר.** הַכֹּהֲנִים חַיָּבִים לְהַפְרִישׁ מַעֲשַׂר בְּהֵמָה כְּיִשְׂרָאֵל. וְהַכֹּל נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר עַל מוּם שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה בְּבֶהֱמַת מַעֲשֵׂר דְּשֶׁלֹּא בְּכַוָּנָה הָיָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הַבְּעָלִים. מִגּוֹ דְאִי בָעֵי שָׁדֵי בֵיהּ מוּמָא בְכֻלֵּיהּ עֶדְרֵיהּ קֹדֶם שֶׁיְּעַשֵּׂר, וְנִמְצָא הַמַּעֲשֵׂר בַּעַל מוּם:


הכל נאמנים על מומי המעשר – Kohanim are obligated to separate the tithe for cattle like an Israelite/Jew. And everyone is believed to stated regarding a blemish that was made on the cattle that was tithed that it was not intentional, and even its owners. For since that if he needs to, he would place of blemish in his entire flock prior to to tithing, and it would be found that the tithe has a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:3)


**שֶׁנִּסְמֵית עֵינוֹ שֶׁנִּקְטְעָה יָדוֹ.** דְּהַיְנוּ מוּם מֻבְהָק:


שנסמית עינו שנקטעה ידו – that is a prominent blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:4)


**בְּנֵי הַכְּנֶסֶת.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁאֵינָן חֲכָמִים. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין יָחִיד מֻמְחֶה, אֲבָל בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ יָחִיד מֻמְחֶה אֵינוֹ נִשְׁחָט אֶלָּא עַל פִּי מֻמְחֶה, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמוּם מֻבְהָק. דּוּמְיָא דַהֲפָרַת נְדָרִים שֶׁבְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ יָחִיד מֻמְחֶה אֵין שְׁלֹשָׁה הֶדְיוֹטוֹת מוֹעִילִים:


בני הכנסת – that is to say, that they are not Sages. And these words [apply] when there is no individual specialist/expert, but in a place where there is an individual expert/specialist, it is not slaughtered other than by the word of the expert, and even with a prominent blemish. This is similar to the renunciation of vows that in a place where there is an individual expert/specialist, three commoners [on a Jewish court] do not help.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:5:5)


**אֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ שָׁם עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה.** אֲפִלּוּ הָיְתָה סַנְהֶדְרִין בַּמָּקוֹם וְאֵין שָׁם אֶחָד שֶׁנָּטַל רְשׁוּת לְהַתִּיר מוּמֵי הַבְּכוֹר, אֵינוֹ נִשְׁחָט עַל פִּיהֶם וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמוּם מֻבְהָק, עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה שָׁם מֻמְחֶה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


אפילו היו שם עשרים ושלשה – even if there was a Sanhedrin in the place, if there isn’t one individual that received permission to permit the blemishes of a firstling, it is not slaughtered by their word, even with a prominent blemish, until there will be a specialist/expert there. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:1)


**הַשּׁוֹחֵט אֶת הַבְּכוֹר.** וּמָכַר מִבְּשָׂרוֹ וְנוֹדַע לָנוּ שֶׁלֹּא הֶרְאָהוּ לְחָכָם:


השוחט את הבכור – and sold from its flesh and it became known to us that he had not shown it to a Sage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:2)


**מַה שֶּׁאָכְלוּ.** הַלּוֹקְחִים אָכְלוּ:


מה שאכלו – those who purchase its food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:3)


**וְיַחֲזִיר לָהֶם אֶת הַדָּמִים.** מִשּׁוּם קְנָס. שֶׁהֶאֱכִילָם בְּשַׂר אִסּוּר:


ויחזיר להם את הדמים – because of a fine. That he fed them forbidden flesh/meat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/5:6:4)


**מְכָרוּהוּ.** הַלּוֹקְחִים לְנָכְרִי, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא גָרַם לָהֶם אִסּוּרָא, יְשַׁלְּמוּ לוֹ דְמֵי טְרֵפָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִיא נִמְכֶּרֶת בְּזוֹל, וְהוּא יַחֲזִיר לָהֶם אֶת הַמּוֹתָר. וְגַבֵּי בְכוֹר לֹא תָנֵי מְכָרוּהוּ לְנָכְרִי, דִּבְכוֹר תָּמִים אָסוּר בַּהֲנָאָה:


מכרוהו – those who purchase it for a heathen, since he did not cause them the prohibition, they will pay him the cost of the torn animal, like it is sold cheaply, and he will return to them the remainder. But regarding a firstling, it is not taught, “they sold it to heathens,” for from a pure firstling is prohibited to derive benefit.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:1)


**עַל אֵלּוּ מוּמִין.** נִפְגְּמָה. אֵין פְּגִימָה בְּלֹא חֶסְרוֹן. אֲבָל סֶדֶק מַשְׁמַע נַמִּי בְּלֹא חֶסְרוֹן. וְשִׁעוּר פְּגִימָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁתַּחְגֹּר בָּהּ צִפֹּרֶן:


על אלו מומין. נפגמה – there is no notch without loss, but a slit [in the ear] implies also without loss, and the measurement of a notch is when one makes a notch deep a=enough for the nail to halt on passing over the edge (see Talmud Bekhorot 37b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:2)


**הַסְּחוּס.** תְּנוּךְ הָאֹזֶן שֶׁקּוֹרִין טנרו"ם בְּלַעַ"ז:


חסחוס (if the ear is split from the cartilage inwards) – the lobe of the ear which is called TANRUS in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא מִן הָעוֹר.** דְּעוֹר הָדַר בָּרִיא וְלֹא הָוֵי מוּמָא. וְהַאי עוֹר הַיְנוּ אַלְיָה רַכָּה שֶׁל אֹזֶן:


אבל לא מן העור- for the distinguished skin is healthy and it is not a blemish and this skin is the soft ear-lap.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:4)


**נִקְּבָה מְלֹא כַרְשִׁינָה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּחֲלַל הַנֶּקֶב שִׁעוּר גַּרְגִּיר שֶׁל כַּרְשִׁינָה, בֵּין שֶׁהַנֶּקֶב אָרֹךְ בֵּין עָגֹל, אִם יֵשׁ בְּכֻלָּן כְּדֵי לְהִצְטָרֵף בַּחֲלָלוֹ כְּשִׁעוּר גַּרְגִּיר שֶׁל כַּרְשִׁינָה, הֲרֵי זֶה מוּם:


נקיבה מלא כרשינה – that there is in the cavity of the incision a measurement of the berry of vetch, whether the incision is long or whether it is round, if there is in all of them to combine in its cavity like the measurement of a berry of a vetch, this is a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:1:5)


**שֶׁתְּהֵא נִפְרָכֶת.** נַעֲשֵׂית פֵּרוּכִים וּפְתִיתִים כְּשֶׁמְּמַשְׁמְשִׁים בָּהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי. וְכָל הָנֵי מוּמִין דַּחֲשִׁיב בְּמַתְנִיתִין שֶׁשּׁוֹחֲטִין עֲלֵיהֶן אֶת הַבְּכוֹר יָלְפִינַן מִקְּרָאֵי, דִּכְתִיב (דברים טו) וְכִי יִהְיֶה בּוֹ מוּם, כְּלָל. פִּסֵּחַ אוֹ עִוֵּר, פְּרָט. כָּל מוּם רַע, חָזַר וְכָלַל. כְּלָל וּפְרָט וּכְלָל, אִי אַתָּה דָן אֶלָּא כְעֵין הַפְּרָט. מָה הַפְּרָט מְפֹרָשׁ מוּמִין שֶׁבְּגָלוּי וְאֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר, אַף כָּל מוּמִין שֶׁבְּגָלוּי וְאֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר:


שתהא נפרכת – it becomes crumbles and broken pieces when they handle it. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi. But these blemishes that are considered in our Mishnah, when they slaughter upon them the firstling, we derive from Biblical verses, as it is written (Deuteronomy 15:21): “But if it has a defect,” is a generalization, “lameness or blindness” is a detail, “any serious defect” – it returned to provide a generalization, you do not judge other than what is similar to the detail. Just as the detail explains open blemishes that do not return [to their healthy state], so also, all open blemishes and they don’t return [to their healthy state] (and the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:1)


**רִיס שֶׁל עַיִן.** עַפְעַף שֶׁל עַיִן:


ריס של עין- the eyelid of the eye.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:2)


**הֲרֵי בְעֵינָיו.** כְּלוֹמַר הֲרֵי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּעֵינָיו:


הרי בעיניו – that is to say, behold that there is in his eyes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:3)


**דַּק.** טיל"א בְּלַעַ"ז:


דק (cataract; veiled or withered spot in the eye) – TILA in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:4)


**חִלָּזוֹן נָחָשׁ.** הַיְנוּ חִלָּזוֹן הַיְנוּ נָחָשׁ, וְקוֹרֵהוּ נָחָשׁ שֶׁעָשׂוּי מְנֻמָּר כְּנָחָשׁ. וְהוּא בָּשָׂר נוֹסָף הַנִּמְשָׁךְ וְחוֹפֶה קְצָת מִן הַשָּׁחֹר שֶׁבָּעַיִן, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי טפר"א, כְּמוֹ שֶׁקּוֹרִין לַצִּפֹּרֶן טַפְרָא, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא חוֹפֶה אֶת הָעַיִן כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהַצִּפֹּרֶן חוֹפָה בְשַׂר הָאֶצְבַּע:


חלזון, נחש – that is an eye-disease, also called נחש (see Talmud Bekhorot 38b), and we call it NAHASH for it is made striped like a snake. And it is additional flesh that is stretched and covers part of the black that is I the eye, and we call it TIFRA in Arabic, like we call the fingernail TIFRA, because it covers the eye in the manner that the fingernail covers the flesh of the finger.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:5)


**וְעֵנָב.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּעֵינוֹ כְגַרְגִּיר שֶׁל עֵנָב:


עינב (growth on the eye) – that there is in his eye like the berry/grain of a growth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:6)


**לָבָן הַפּוֹסֵק בַּסִּירָא.** סִירָא הַיְנוּ שׁוּרָה שֶׁבָּעַיִן סְבִיב הַשָּׁחֹר שֶׁמִּשָּׁם מַרְאִית הָעַיִן בָּאָה. וְאִם חוּט לָבָן יוֹצֵא מִן הַלָּבָן שֶׁבָּעַיִן וּפוֹסֵק אוֹתָהּ שׁוּרָה וְנִכְנָס בַּשָּׁחֹר הָוֵי מוּם:


לבן הפוסק בסירא – SIRA/thorn that is a line that is in the eye around the black that from which the vision of the eye comes. But if a white thread comes out from the white that is in the eye and breaks through a line and enters into the black, this is a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:7)


**שָׁחֹר וְנִכְנָס בַּלָּבָן.** אִם חוּט אֶחָד שָׁחֹר יוֹצֵא מִן הַשָּׁחֹר שֶׁבָּעַיִן וּפוֹסֵק בַּסִּירָא וְנִכְנָס בַּלָּבָן:


שחור ונכנס בלבן – if one black thread comes out from the black that is in the eye and breaks through the thorn, and enters into the white [of the eye].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:2:8)


**אֵינוֹ מוּם שֶׁאֵין מוּמִין בַּלָּבָן.** דְּלָאו עַיִן הוּא אֶלָּא שֻׁמַּן הָעַיִן:


אינו מום שאין מומין בלבן – for it is not the eye, but rather the fat of the eye.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:1)


**חֲוַרְוַד.** כְּמִין טִפִּין לְבָנוֹת בָּעַיִן. תַּרְגּוּם לָבָן, חִיוַר:


חורוד (white spots on the cornea/cataract) – like white drops on the eye. The Aramaic translation of white is חיור/white.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:2)


**וְהַמַּיִם.** הַיּוֹרְדִים אֶל הָעַיִן וּמוֹנְעִים הָרְאוּת:


והמים (rheum)– that descends into the eye and prevents the seeing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:3)


**הַקְּבוּעִים.** אַתַּרְוַיְהוּ קָאֵי, אַחֲוַרְוָד וְהַמַּיִם:


הקבועים – it refers to both, to the white spots on the cornea and the water/rheum.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:4)


**בּוֹדְקִין אוֹתוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה פְעָמִים בְּתוֹךְ שְׁמוֹנִים יוֹם.** בְּכָל עֶשְׂרִים וְשִׁבְעָה יוֹם שֶׁהוּא שְׁלִישׁ הַזְּמַן שֶׁל שְׁמוֹנִים יוֹם בְּקֵרוּב בּוֹדְקִים אוֹתוֹ אִם הָלַךְ הַחֲוַרְוָד. וְאִם הָלַךְ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחָזַר, מוֹנִין לוֹ שְׁמוֹנִים יוֹם מִיּוֹם שֶׁחָזַר. וְאֵין הַחֲוַרְוָד קָבוּעַ עַד שֶׁיַּעֲמֹד שְׁמוֹנִים יוֹם. וְאִי לֹא בְּדָקוּהוּ בְּתוֹךְ שְׁמוֹנִים יוֹם, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּמְצָא הַחֲוַרְוָד שָׁם בְּיוֹם שְׁמוֹנִים לֹא הָוֵי מוּם, דְּשֶׁמָּא בֵּינְתַיִם הָלַךְ וְחָזַר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶּן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס:


בודקין אותו שלשה פעמים בתוך שמונים יום – on every twenty-seven days which is one-third of the time of eighty days, approximately, we examine it if the cataract/white spots on the cornea went away. And if it it went away, even though it returned, we count eighty days from the day that it returned, for it isn’t lasting white spots on the cornea/cataracts until it stands for eight days. But if he did not examine it within the eighty [days], even though the cataracts/white spots on the cornea are found there on the eightieth day, it is not a blemish, lest in the meanwhile, it went away and then returned. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Hanina ben Antigonus.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:5)


**אֵלּוּ הֵן הַמַּיִם הַקְּבוּעִים.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּמַאי יָדְעִינַן אִי קְבוּעִים אוֹ עוֹבְרִים:


אלו הן מהם הקבועים – meaning to say, how do we know if it lasting or transitory?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:6)


**אָכַל לַח וְיָבֵשׁ שֶׁל גְּשָׁמִים.** אִם הֶאֱכִילוּהוּ לִרְפוּאָה תֶּבֶן וְחָצִיר שֶׁקּוֹרִין פיינ"ו בְּלַעַ"ז:


אכל לח ויבש של גשמים – if they fed it straw and leeks for medicinal purposes that we call FINEO in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:7)


**לַח.** הַגָּדֵל בַּאֲדָר וַחֲצִי נִיסָן:


לח – that grows in Adar and half of Nisan.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:8)


**וְיָבֵשׁ.** הַגָּדֵל בֶּאֱלוּל וַחֲצִי תִשְׁרֵי:


ויבש- that grows in Elul and half of Tishrei.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:9)


**שֶׁל גְּשָׁמִים.** הַיְנוּ שֶׁגָּדֵל בִּשְׂדֵה בֵית הַבַּעַל. וּמַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, אָכַל לַח וְיָבֵשׁ שֶׁל גְּשָׁמִים הֲרֵי זֶה מוּם, שֶׁל בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִין אֵינוֹ מוּם. וְדִגְשָׁמִים נַמִּי, אָכַל יָבֵשׁ וְאַחַר כָּךְ אָכַל לַח אֵינוֹ מוּם, עַד שֶׁיֹּאכַל יָבֵשׁ אַחַר הַלַּח:


של גשמים – that is that grew in a field sufficiently watered by rain and requiring no artificial irrigation. But our Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows (see Talmud Bekhorot 38b): If it ate moist (i.e., that grew during Adar and half of Nisan) and dry (i.e., that grew during Elul and half of Tishrei) of a field regarding a field naturally watered by rain, it is a blemish; if it was a field requiring irrigation, it is not a blemish. But if it is a field naturally water by rain, [if] it ate dry (i.e., that grew during Elul and half of Tishrei) and afterwards, it ate moist (i.e., that which grew during Adar and half of Nisan) it is not a blemish until it would eat dry (i.e., that which grew during Elul and half of Tishrei) after that which is moist (i.e., that which grew during Adar and half of Nisan).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:10)


**שֶׁל בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִין.** אֶרֶץ הַצְּרִיכָה לְהַשְׁקוֹת לֹא הָוֵי רְפוּאָה, וְלֹא בָּדְקִינַן בֵּיהּ:


של בית השלחין - land that requires watering it is not for medicinal purposes, and we don’t examine it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:11)


**אָכַל יָבֵשׁ וְאַחַר כָּךְ לַח.** לִרְפוּאָה, אֵין זוֹ רְפוּאָתוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִתְרַפֵּא בְכָךְ אֵינוֹ מוּם:


אכל יבש ואחר כך לח- for medicinal purposes, this is not its cure, and even though he was not cured through it, it is not a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:3:12)


**עַד שֶׁיֹּאכַל יָבֵשׁ אַחַר הַלָּח.** זוֹ הִיא דֶּרֶךְ רְפוּאָתוֹ, תֶּבֶן וְחָצִיר הַגָּדֵל בַּאֲדָר וַחֲצִי נִיסָן מַאֲכִילִין אוֹתוֹ בַּאֲדָר וַחֲצִי נִיסָן, וְתֶבֶן וְחָצִיר הַגָּדֵל בֶּאֱלוּל וַחֲצִי תִשְׁרֵי מַאֲכִילִין אוֹתוֹ בֶּאֱלוּל וַחֲצִי תִשְׁרֵי. דְּלֹא שָׁחְטִינַן לֵיהּ עַד שֶׁיַּעַבְרוּ עָלָיו כָּל יְמֵי הַקַּיִץ וְיִבְדֹּק בִּשְׁנֵיהֶם, וְאִם לֹא נִתְרַפֵּא הָוֵי מוּם. וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיֹּאכַל מֵהֶם לֹא פָּחוֹת מִכַּגְּרוֹגֶרֶת בְּכָל סְעֻדָּה וּסְעֻדָּה כָּל שְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים בִּתְחִלַּת הַסְּעֻדָּה וּלְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁתָה, וְלֹא יִהְיֶה כָּפוּת בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁאוֹכֵל הָעֲשָׂבִים הַלָּלוּ לִרְפוּאָה, וְלֹא יִהְיֶה יְחִידִי אֶלָּא עִם חֲבֵרָיו, בַּשָּׂדֶה וְלֹא בְתוֹךְ הָעִיר. וְאִם שָׁלְמוּ כָּל הַתְּנָאִים הַלָּלוּ וְלֹא נִתְרַפֵּא הָוֵי מוּם וְשׁוֹחֲטִין עָלָיו:


עד שיאכל יבש אחר הלח – this is the manner of his cure, straw and leeks that grow in Adar and half of Nisan, we feed it during Adar and half of Nisan, but straw and leeks that grew in Elul and half of Tishrei, we feed it in Elul and half of Tishrei. For we don’t slaughter it until all the days of the summer have passed and we examine it at both times, and if it was not cured, it is a blemish, and it (i.e., the animal) needs to eat from them no less than a dried fig during each and every meal all three months at the beginning of the meal and after it drank, and it would not be tied at the time that it eats these grasses for medicinal purposes, and there wouldn’t be an individual [animal] but with its fellows, in the field and not within the city. And if it fulfilled all of these conditions and was not cured, it is a blemish and we slaughter it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:1)


**חָטְמוֹ שֶׁנִּקַּב וְכוּ'.** וְהוּא שֶׁנִּקְּבוּ מְחִצּוֹת הַחִיצוֹנִיּוֹת שֶׁל חֹטֶם, שֶׁהַנֶּקֶב נִרְאֶה מִבַּחוּץ. אֲבָל אֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה אֶלָּא מִבִּפְנִים כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּקְּבָהּ מְחִצָּה הַחוֹלֶקֶת אֶת הַחֹטֶם, אֵינוֹ מוּם, שֶׁסֵּתֶר הוּא, וַאֲנַן יָלְפִינַן מִכְּלָל וּפְרָט וּכְלָל דְּמוּמִין שֶׁבַּגָּלוּי בָּעִינַן:


חוטמו שניקב וכו' – and this is where the outer partitions/walls of the nose were perforated, that the perforation is seen from the outside. But [if] it is not seen other than from inside, as for example, when the partition that divides the nose was perforated, it is not a blemish, for it is secret, and we derive from [the principle] of a generalization and a specification, that we require blemishes that are revealed [in the open].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:2)


**שֶׁנִּפְגַּם.** וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ חֶסְרוֹן:


שנפגם – and it has something missing/lacking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:3)


**שֶׁנִּסְדָּק.** וְאֵין בּוֹ חֶסְרוֹן. וְשִׁעוּר פְּגִימָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁתַּחְגֹּר בָּהּ צִפֹּרֶן:


שנסדק – but there is nothing lacking/missing. And the measurement of the notch/incision, in order that the fingernail can encircle it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:4)


**שְׂפָתוֹ.** שׁוּרָה חִיצוֹנָה שֶׁל שָׂפָה. כְּלוֹמַר חֻדָּהּ הַחִיצוֹן:


שפתו – the outer row/line of the lip [of the firstling], meaning to say, the outer point/thin part.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:5)


**חִטָּיו הַחִיצוֹנִיּוֹת.** הַשִּׁנַּיִם שֶׁבְּאֶמְצַע הַפֶּה:


חיטיו החיצונות – the teeth that are in the middle of the mouth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:6)


**שֶׁנִּפְגְּמוּ.** נֶחֶסְרוּ:


שנפגמו – that are missing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:7)


**נִגְמְמוּ.** נִמּוֹקוּ, וְלֹא נִשְׁאַר מֵהֶם אֶלָּא רֹשֶׁם מֻעָט, וְאֵינָן בּוֹלְטוֹת כְּמוֹ שֶׁרְגִילִים לִהְיוֹת:


נגממו (incisors are broken off/levelled with the gums) – squashed beyond recognition -, and there doesn’t remain from them other than a small mark/trace, and they don’t protrude like they are accustomed to be.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:8)


**וְהַפְּנִימִיּוֹת.** שִׁנַּיִם הַגְּדוֹלוֹת שֶׁקּוֹרִין משילא"ש, שֶׁנֶּעֶקְרוּ לְגַמְרֵי. אֲבָל נִפְגְּמוּ וְנִגְמְמוּ לֹא הָוֵי מוּם:


הפנימיות – the large teeth that we call MASHILASH, that were completely uprooted. But if they were damaged or worn down/levelled, it is not a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:4:9)


**אֵין בּוֹדְקִין מִן הַמַּתְאִימוֹת וְלִפְנִים.** שִׁנַּיִם הַגְּדוֹלוֹת שֶׁאַחַת נִרְאֵית כִּשְׁתַּיִם וַעֲשׂוּיוֹת כִּתְאוֹמוֹת, מִשָּׁם וְלִפְנִים אֵינוֹ נֶחְשָׁב מוּם מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא סֵתֶר. וְהֵן עַצְמָן שֶׁנֶּעֶקְרוּ לֹא הָוֵי מוּם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶּן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס:


אין בודקין את המתאימות ולפנים – the large teeth that one appears like two [teeth] and are made like twins. From there and inward it is not considered a blemish because it is in secret. But they themselves, when they were uprooted, are not a blemish. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Hananiah (Hanina in the Mishnah) ben Antigonus.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:1)


**הַזּוֹבָן.** הַנַּרְתֵּק שֶׁהַגִּיד שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה חָבוּי בּוֹ. כְּשֶׁנִּפְגַּם הָוֵי מוּם דְּלֹא הָדַר בָּרִיא, אֲבָל נִטַּל לֹא הָוֵי מוּם, דְּחוֹזֵר לְאֵיתָנוֹ:


זובן (bag which contains a male animal’s membrum) – the case/casket that the sinew of the cattle is hidden/covered in). When it is damaged/mutilated, it is a blemish because it does not return healthy, but if it was removed it is not a blemish, for it returns to its normal condition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:2)


**עֶרְיָה שֶׁל נְקֵבָה.** בֵּית הָעֶרְוָה הַבּוֹלֵט לַחוּץ וְנִרְאֶה עַל שְׁאָר שֶׁטַח הַגּוּף:


עריה של נקבה (genitals of a female animal) – pudenda that protrudes outward and appears on the rest of the area of the body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:3)


**בְּמֻקְדָּשִׁים.** בִּשְׁאָר קָדָשִׁים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן נְקֵבָה, כְּגוֹן שְׁלָמִים, דְּאִלּוּ בַבְּכוֹר לֵיכָּא נְקֵבָה:


במוקדשים – in the rest of Holy Things that have in them female [animals] as for example, peace offerings, for whereas regarding the firstling, there isn’t a female.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:4)


**אֲבָל לֹא מִן הַפֶּרֶק.** דְּנִפְגַּם בֵּין הַפְּרָקִים מַעֲלֶה אֲרוּכָה, כְּשֶׁלֹּא נֶחְתַּךְ כָּל הַזָּנָב:


אבל לא מן הפרק (but not at the joint) – that is damaged between the joints a long ascent, when all of the tail has not been severed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:5)


**אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה רֹאשׁ הַזָּנָב מַפְצִיל עֶצֶם.** הַזָּנָב הַתָּלוּי לְמַטָּה נִקְלַף הָעוֹר וּבָשָׂר וְנִשְׁאַר הָעֶצֶם מְגֻלֶּה, שׁוּב אֵין מַעֲלֶה אֲרוּכָה, הוֹאִיל וּבְרֹאשׁ הַזָּנָב הוּא:


או שהיה ראש הזנב מפציל את עצם – the head of the tail is suspended below, the flesh and skin is peeled and the boney remains uncovered, it still is not a long ascent, since it is the head of the vone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:6)


**מַפְצִיל.** כְּמוֹ אֲשֶׁר פִּצֵּל [בְּרֵאשִׁית ל]. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, שֶׁנֶּחֱלַק הַזָּנָב בְּקָצֵהוּ לִשְׁתֵּי זְנָבוֹת וּבְכָל אַחַת מִשְּׁתֵּיהֶן עֶצֶם:


מפציל – that it was peeled/split off. Another explanation- that the tail was divided at its end to two tails and there is a bone in each of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:5:7)


**וּבֵין חֻלְיָא לְחֻלְיָא מְלֹא אֶצְבָּע.** שֶׁחֻלְיוֹת שֶׁל זָנָב רְחוֹקוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ מְלֹא אֶצְבַּע, דְּהַיְנוּ רֹחַב גֻּדָל:


ובין חוליא לחוליא מלוא אצבע – that the vertebrae/links of the tail are far from each other, the distance of a finger’s breadth, which is the width of a thumb.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:1)


**אֵין לוֹ בֵיצִים אוֹ אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא בֵיצָה אֶחָת.** מַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, אֵין לוֹ שְׁתֵּי בֵיצִים בִּשְׁתֵּי כִיסִין אֶלָּא בְכִיס אֶחָד, אִי נַמִּי שְׁנֵי כִיסִין וּבֵיצָה אַחַת, הֲרֵי זֶה מוּם:


אין לו ביצים או אין לו אלא ביצה אחת – Our Mishnah is deficient and this is how it should be read (see Talmud Bekhorot 40a): It (i.e., the animal) doesn’t have two testicles in two sacks, but rather [only] in one sack; alternatively, two sacks and one testicle, behold this is a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:2)


**רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל אוֹמֵר אִם יֶשׁ לוֹ שְׁנֵי כִיסִים.** אַסֵּיפָא פָלֵיג, דְּקָאָמַר תַּנָּא קַמָּא אִם אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא בֵּיצָה אַחַת וּשְׁנֵי כִיסִין הָוֵי מוּם, לֹא הִיא, דְּכָל שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁנֵי כִיסִין בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי בֵיצִים. אֲבָל אָרֵישָׁא מוֹדֶה דְּכִי אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא כִיס אֶחָד כְּמִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא בֵּיצָה אַחַת דָּמֵי:


ר' ישמעאל אומר אם יש לו שני כיסים – He is disputing the concluding clause, for the first Tanna/teacher states if it doesn’t have anything other than one testicle and two sacks, it is a blemish, but this is not the case, for whenever it has two sacks, it is certain that it has two testicles. But on the first clause, he admits/agrees that where it doesn’t have other than one sack, it is like someone that doesn’t have anything other than one testicle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:3)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר.** בְּיָדוּעַ לֹא אָמְרִינַן, אֶלָּא מוֹשִׁיבוֹ עַל עַכּוּזוֹ גָּרְסִינַן. וְדֻגְמָתוֹ בְּפֶרֶק כֵּיצַד מְעַבְּרִין [עֵרוּבִין נד] עַכּוּזוֹ תְנַן אוֹ אַכּוּזוֹ תְנַן. עַל עַכּוּזוֹ, עַל עַגְבָתוֹ אַחַת מוֹשִׁיבִין אֶת הַבְּכוֹר שֶׁאֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא בֵּיצָה אַחַת בִּשְׁנֵי כִיסִין:


רבי עקיבא אומר- we certainly don’t say this, but rather, we have the reading, “he sets it on its buttocks and squeezes [its testicles]. And its example is found in the chapter [five of Tractate Eruvin 53b]: “How did they augment towns?” – is it (“sets it on its buttocks”) taught עכוזו (with an AYIN) or is it taught אכוזו (with an ALEPH)? [It is taught] עכוזו (i.e., with an AYIN) – they set the firstling on one rump/buttocks (though it could mean the genitals), for he has only one genital in two sacks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:4)


**וּמְמַעֵךְ.** וּמְמַשְׁמֵשׁ בַּכִּיס וּבַכְּסָלִים:


וממעך (dissolve by rubbing/crushing) – and feels the sack and the groin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:5)


**אִם יֵשׁ שָׁם בֵּיצָה אַחַת סוֹפָהּ לָצֵאת.** וְאִם אֵינָהּ יוֹצְאָה הָוֵי מוּם:


אם יש שם ביצה אחת סופה לצאת - but if it does not come out, it is a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:6:6)


**וְהִתִּיר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא.** דַּהֲוֵי מוּם, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא מְצָאָהּ בִּמְקוֹמָהּ כְּשֶׁמִּעֵךְ וּמִשְׁמֵשׁ. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:
