והתיר רבי עקיבא – that it is a blemish, for he did not find it (i.e., the genital in its place when he crushed it/rubbed it and felt it and. And such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:1)


**בַּעַל חָמֵשׁ רַגְלַיִם אוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ.** הָא דְחָשְׁבִינַן לֵיהּ בַּעַל מוּם וְלֹא טְרֵפָה, הָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁיָּתֵר אוֹ חָסֵר בְּיָדָיו דְּהַיְנוּ אוֹתָם שֶׁל צַד הָרֹאשׁ, אֲבָל חָסֵר רֶגֶל אוֹ יָתֵר רֶגֶל מֵרַגְלָיו הָאַחֲרוֹנִים, הַוְיָא טְרֵפָה וַאֲסוּרָה בַאֲכִילָה:


בעל חמש רגלים או שלש – but that we calculate it as a blemish and not as “torn,” these words [apply] when it has more or less in its hands, that is, those of the side of the head, but missing a foot or having an additional foot from its back feet , it is a “torn” animal, and it is prohibited to be consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:2)


**קְלוּטוֹת.** עֲגֻלּוֹת. וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן סְדוּקוֹת הָוֵי מוּם, דְּפַרְסוֹת בְּהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה אֵינָן עֲגֻלּוֹת:


קלוט (an animal with un-cloven hooves – a sign of uncleanness – born of a clean animal, a monster or a hybrid) – round, and even if they are slit/cloven – it is a blemish, for the split hooves of an pure animal are not round.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:3)


**שָׁחוּל.** שֶׁנִּשְׁמְטָה יְרֵכוֹ. תַּרְגּוּם מְשִׁיתִהוּ, שְׁחַלְתֵּיהּ:


שחול (an animal with a dislocated hip) – that its thigh/haunch is detached. The Aramaic of “I drew him [Moses] out [of the water]” (see Exodus 2:10) is שחלתיה /”I drew him out.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:7:4)


**וְהַכָּסוּל.** דֶּרֶךְ הַבְּהֵמָה שֶׁהַיָּרֵךְ מְחֻבֶּרֶת לָאַלְיָה סָמוּךְ לַכְּסָלִים, וְלֹא לְמַעְלָה מִן הַכְּסָלִים, וְזוֹ הַיָּרֵךְ עַל גַּבֵּי הַכֶּסֶל:


כסול (an animal with a deformed hip) – it is the manner of an animal that the thigh/haunch is attached to the fat-tail near the groin, but not above from the grown, and in this one, the thigh/haunch is upon the groin.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:1)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִכָּר.** כְּשֶׁהוּא עוֹמֵד שֶׁאֵין הַשֶּׁבֶר נִרְאֶה, אֲבָל נִכָּר כְּשֶׁהוּא מְהַלֵּךְ שֶׁהוּא צוֹלֵעַ. דְּאִי אֵינוֹ נִכָּר כְּלָל לֹא הָוֵי מוּם:


אף על פי שאינו ניכר – when it (i.e., the animal) stands, the break is not seen, but it is recognized when it walks that it limps, for it is not recognized at all, it is not a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:2)


**מוּמִין אֵלּוּ.** נִשְׁבַּר עֶצֶם יָדוֹ וְעֶצֶם רַגְלוֹ שֶׁהֻזְכְּרוּ לְמַעְלָה:


מומין אלו – bone of the foreleg or the bone of the hindleg that were mentioned above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:3)


**אִילָא.** שֵׁם חָכָם:


אילא – the name of a Sage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:4)


**עָגֹל כְּשֶׁל אָדָם.** דְּלָאו הַיְנוּ אוֹרְחָא שֶׁיְּהֵא גַלְגַּל עֵין הַבְּהֵמָה עָגֹל כְּשֶׁל אָדָם:


עגול כשל אדם – that it is not the manner that the roundness of the eye of an animal is like that of a human.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:5)


**רֹב הַמְדַבֵּר.** הַיְנוּ אוֹתוֹ קְצָת הַלָּשׁוֹן שֶׁאֵינוֹ מֻדְבָּק לְמַלְקוֹחָיו:


רוב המדבר – that is that part of the tongue that is not attached to its jaws.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:8:6)


**וּבֵית דִּין שֶׁל אַחֲרֵיהֶן אָמְרוּ הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מוּמִין.** וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


ובית דין של אחריהן אמרו הרי אלו מומין – and such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:1)


**מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁהַלְּחִי הַתַּחְתּוֹן.** מִשּׁוּם דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל דִּבְפִיו דּוֹמֶה לַחֲזִיר פְּלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עֲלֵיהּ דְּאִילָא, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן הַשְׁתָּא דְּלֹא פְלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עֲלֵיהּ אֶלָּא בִשְׂפָתוֹ הָעֶלְיוֹנָה עוֹדֶפֶת עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה, אֲבָל שְׂפָתוֹ הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה עוֹדֶפֶת עַל הָעֶלְיוֹנָה מוֹדִים רַבָּנָן דַּהֲוֵי מוּם. וּמַעֲשֶׂה נַמִּי שֶׁהַלְּחִי הַתַּחְתּוֹן עוֹדֵף עַל הָעֶלְיוֹן וְאָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים הֲרֵי זֶה מוּם. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁעֶצֶם הַלְּחִי הַתַּחְתּוֹן עוֹדֵף עַל הָעֶלְיוֹן, אָז הָוֵי מוּם אֲפִלּוּ בִּבְהֵמָה. אֲבָל אֵין בָּהּ עֶצֶם אֶלָּא שֶׁשְּׂפָתוֹ הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה אֲרֻכָּה וּמְקַבְּלָהּ אֶת הָעֶלְיוֹנָה, הָוֵי מוּם בָּאָדָם אֲבָל לֹא בַבְּהֵמָה:


מעשה שהלחי התחתון – because it is taught above (see Tractate Bekhorot, Chapter 6, Mishnah 8) that its mouth (i.e., of a firstling animal) is similar to that of a pig, the Rabbis argue with [the opinion] of Illa, it comes to tell us that now the Rabbis do not dispute it other than regarding its upper lip which is stretched over its lower [lip], but [if] its lower lip is stretched over the upper[lip], the Rabbis admit/agree that it is a blemish. And also the story regarding the lower jaw that stretches over the upper [jaw], and the Sages stated that this is a blemish. These words [apply] when the bone of the lower jaw stretches over the [bone of] the upper [jaw], then it is a blemish even in an animal. But if it lacks a bone but rather that its lower lip is longer and receives the upper lip, it is blemish in a human but not in an animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:2)


**אֹזֶן הַגְּדִי שֶׁהָיְתָה כְפוּלָה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי אָזְנַיִם מִצַּד אֶחָד אֹזֶן בְּתוֹךְ אֹזֶן:


אזן הגדי שהיתה כפולה – that it has two ears from one side, an ear within an ear.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:3)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא עֶצֶם אֶחָד.** שֶׁאֵין לָהּ אֶלָּא תְנוּךְ אֶחָד, שֶׁנִּכְפַּל תְּנוּךְ הָעֶלְיוֹן לְתוֹכוֹ וְנִתְחַבֵּר לְמַטָּה, הָוֵי מוּם:


בזמן שהיא עצם אחד – and it has only one earlobe, in which the upper earlobe is doubled inside it and combined below, it is a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:4)


**וְאִם אֵינָהּ עֶצֶם אֶחָד.** שֶׁהַתְּנוּכִים מֻבְדָּלִים לְמַעְלָה:


ואם אינה עצם אחד – the earlobes are differentiated/separated above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:5)


**אֵינוֹ מוּם.** וְטַעֲמָא לֹא אִתְפָּרַשׁ. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם לֹא דָמֵי לְבַעַל חָמֵשׁ רַגְלַיִם, דְּאֹזֶן בְּתוֹךְ אֹזֶן אֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה כָּל כָּךְ:


אינו מום – but the reason is not explained. But nevertheless, it is not similar to an animal (i.e., a firstling) with five feet, for an ear within an ear is not seen all that often.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:6)


**שֶׁהִיא דוֹמָה לְשֶׁל חֲזִיר.** עֲגֻלָּה כִזְנַב הַחֲזִיר, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ דַּקָּה כְמוֹתָהּ:


שהיא דומה לשל חזיר – round like the tail of a pig, and even though it is not thin like it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:9:7)


**שָׁלֹשׁ חֻלְיוֹת.** דַּוְקָא בְּזָנָב שֶׁל טָלֶה הוּא דְּבָעִינַן שָׁלֹשׁ חֻלְיוֹת. אֲבָל זָנָב שֶׁל גְּדִי, חֻלְיָא אַחַת בִּלְבַד מוּם, שְׁתַּיִם אֵינוֹ מוּם. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


שלש חוליות – it is specifically in the tail of a lamb that we require three vertebrae [of the spinal column], but the tail of a kid, one vertebrae alone is a blemish, two are not a blemish. And such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:1)


**יַבֶּלֶת.** פורו"ש בְּלַעַ"ז. וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא בַּלֹּבֶן שֶׁבָּעַיִן, וְיֵשׁ בָּהּ שֵׂעָר, הֲרֵי זֶה מוּם:


יבלת (wart) – POROSH in the foreign language. And even if its is the white of he eye, and it has hair, it is a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:2)


**וְשֶׁנִּפְגַּם עֶצֶם יָדוֹ.** שֶׁנִּכָּר הַמּוּם. וּלְעֵיל אַיְרֵי בְשֶׁנִּשְׁבַּר:


ושנפגם עצם ידו- that the blemish is known. And above, we are speaking about when it is broken.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:3)


**וְשֶׁנִּפְסַק עַצְמוֹ שֶׁבְּפִיו.** שֶׁהַשִּׁנַּיִם קְבוּעוֹת בּוֹ. וּלְמַעְלָה מִן הַחוּטִין קָא מַיְרֵי. דְּאִי חוּטִין גּוּפַיְהוּ, דְּהַיְנוּ הַשִּׁנַּיִם עַצְמָן שֶׁבְּאֶמְצַע הַפֶּה, הָא תְנַן לְעֵיל דַּאֲפִלּוּ נִפְגְּמוּ אוֹ נִגְמְמוּ הָוֵי מוּם:


ושנפסק עצמו שבפיו – that the teeth are set in it. It (i.e., the Mishnah) is speaking of above the sinews. For if it is the sinews themselves, which are the teeth themselves that are in the middle of the mouth, but it is taught above (see Tractate Bekhorot, Chapter 7, Mishnah 4): “if the incisors are damaged or worn down, it is a blemish.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:4)


**עֵינוֹ אַחַת גְּדוֹלָה.** כְּשֶׁל עֵגֶל:


עינו אחת גדולה – like that of a calf.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:5)


**וְאַחַת קְטַנָּה.** כְּשֶׁל אַוָּז:


ואחת קטנה – like that of a goose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:6)


**בְּמַרְאֶה.** שֶׁנִּרְאֶה לַכֹּל שֶׁזּוֹ גְדוֹלָה מִזּוֹ:


במראה – that it appears to all that this one is larger than the other one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:7)


**אֲבָל לֹא בְמִדָּה.** שֶׁאִם אֵינוֹ נִכָּר אֶלָּא בְמִדָּה, אֵינוֹ מוּם:


אבל לא במדה – that it is not recognized other than by measurement, it is not a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:10:8)


**וְלֹא הוֹדוּ לוֹ חֲכָמִים.** לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ולא הודו לא חכמים – with Rabbi Yehuda. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:1)


**לָעַרְקוֹב.** לַקֶּשֶׁר הָעֶלְיוֹן שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם חִבּוּר הַיָּרֵךְ עִם הַשּׁוֹק. וּכְנֶגְדּוֹ בַגָּמָל נִכָּר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ כְנֶגֶד אַרְכּוּבָה עֶלְיוֹנָה זוֹ עֶצֶם בּוֹלֵט לַחוּץ:


ערקוב (the inner part of the knee/inner angle of the joint which unites the thigh and the leg of the animal) – to the upper tie which is the place of the connection of the thigh with the foreleg. But corresponding to it in a camel it is recognized that it has corresponding to this upper leg from under the hip-bone to the ankle (i.e., the knee and the surrounding parts), this bone protrudes outward.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:2)


**כָּל מַרְבִּית הָעֲגָלִים כֵּן.** כָּל תַּרְבּוּת עֲגָלִים וּמִנְהָגָן שֶׁיְּהֵא זְנָבָן מַגִּיעַ לְעַרְקוֹב [הִלְכָּךְ בְּצִיר מֵהָכִי הָוֵי מוּמָא], וּכְשֶׁהֵן גְּדֵלוֹת נִמְתָּחוֹת עַד לְמַטָּה:


כל מרבית העגלים כן (through the whole period of growth of calves it is so) – all of the growth of calves and their custom – is that their tails reach the inner part of the knee [therefore, less than this it is a blemish], and when they are grown up, they (i.e., the tails) extend/stretch below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:3)


**שֶׁבְּאֶמְצַע הַיָּרֵךְ.** הוּא הַקְּפִיץ הָעֶלְיוֹן שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי דִכְנֶגְדּוֹ בַגָּמָל נִכָּר:


שבאמצע הירך – this is the upper joints/hams of the knee that I explained which correspond to that of a known camel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:11:4)


**עַל אֵלּוּ מוּמִין שׁוֹחֲטִין אֶת הַבְּכוֹר.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין עַל אֵלּוּ מוּמִין שׁוֹחֲטִין אֶת הַבְּכוֹר, הָדַר תַּנְיֵהּ הָכָא. מִשּׁוּם דִּתְנַן שְׁלֹשָׁה הוֹסִיף וְכוּ'‏ וְאָמְרוּ לוֹ לֹא שָׁמַעְנוּ אֶת אֵלּוּ, הֲדַר וְכַלְלִינְהוּ לוֹמַר שֶׁאַף עַל אֵלּוּ מוּמִין שׁוֹחֲטִים:


על אלו מומין שוחטין את הבכור – even though it is taught in the beginning of our chapter (see Mishnah 1 of this chapter), “on account of these blemishes they slaughter the firstling,” it returns and teaches it again here, because it is taught three, it adds, etc. and they said to him: we did not hear of these” (see Mishnah 8 of this chapter), he reviewed them and generalized them to state that even on these blemishes, they slaughter [the firstling].



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:1)


**לֹא בַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן רְאוּיִין, שֶׁאֵין מַקְרִיבִין בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ אֶלָּא מִן הַמֻּבְחָר:


לא במקדש – because they are not appropriate, for one does not offer up in the Sanctuary any other than the most choice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:2)


**וְלֹא בַמְּדִינָה.** שֶׁאֵינָן מוּמִין קְבוּעִים לְהַפְקִיעַ קְדֻשָּׁתָן. אֶלָּא מַנִּיחִין אוֹתוֹ עַד שֶׁיִּפֹּל בָּהֶן מוּם אַחֵר קָבוּעַ וְיִהְיוּ נִשְׁחָטִים עָלָיו:


ולא במדינה – that they are not lasting/permanent to release them from their sanctity, but rather we set them aside until another lasting blemish befalls them and they are slaughtered for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:3)


**גָּרָב.** מִין שְׁחִין לַח מִבַּחוּץ וּמִבִּפְנִים. וְגָרָב הָאָמוּר בַּתּוֹרָה דַּהֲוֵי מוּם, הוּא שְׁחִין יָבֵשׁ:


גרב (itch) – a kind of moist boil from without and from within. And the boil mentioned in the Torah is a blemish, for it is a dry boil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:4)


**יַבֶּלֶת.** בְּלֹבֶן הָעַיִן, וְאֵין בָּהּ שֵׂעָר. וּלְעֵיל דַּחֲשִׁיב לַהּ בַּמּוּמִין שֶׁשּׁוֹחֲטִין עָלָיו, מַיְרֵי דְיֵשׁ בָּהּ שֵׂעָר:


יבלת (wart) - on the white of the eye and it has no hair in it. But above (see Mishnah 10 in our chapter) when we considered blemishes that we slaughter [the firstling] we were speaking about when it has hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:5)


**וּבַעַל חֲזָזִית.** תְּרֵי גַוְנֵי חֲזָזִית הֲווּ. חֲזָזִית הַמִּצְרִית הִיא יַלֶּפֶת הַכְּתוּבָה בַתּוֹרָה שֶׁהוּא לַח מִבַּחוּץ וְיָבֵשׁ מִבִּפְנִים, וְהָוֵי מוּמָא דְּלֹא הָדַר בָּרִיא, דְּאָמַר מַר לָמָּה נִקְרָא שְׁמָהּ יַלֶּפֶת שֶׁמְּלַפֶּפֶת וְהוֹלֶכֶת עַד יוֹם הַמִּיתָה. וַחֲזָזִית הַשְּׁנוּיָה כָאן הִיא מִין שְׁחִין שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ רְפוּאָה:


ובעל חזזית – there are two varieties of lichen. The Egyptian lichen is the lichen written of in the Torah which is moist from the outside and dry from the inside, and it is a blemish that does not return to health, and the Master stated (see Talmud Bekhorot 41a) – Why is it called lichen? Because it continues to cling [to the body] until the day of death, but the lichen that is taught here is a kind of boil that has a cure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:6)


**וּמְזֹהָם.** שֶׁיּוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ רֵיחַ רַע:


ומזוהם – that a bad smells goes out from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:7)


**וְשֶׁנֶּעֶבְדָה בוֹ עֲבֵרָה.** שֶׁרָבַע אֶת הָאִשָּׁה אוֹ שֶׁהֵמִית אֶת הָאָדָם עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא עֵד אֶחָד לֹא בָּרְבִיעָה וְלֹא בַנְּגִיחָה:


ושנעבד בו עבירה - (see Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 8, Mishnah 1) that it (the firstling) had sexual relations with a woman or that it put a man to death with the testimony of one witness, for there is only witness - neither with carnal connection with beasts nor with goring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:8)


**אוֹ עַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ.** שֶׁאֵין שָׁם עֵד, אֶלָּא הַבְּעָלִים אָמְרוּ רְאִינוּהוּ שֶׁנִּרְבַּע אוֹ שֶׁהֵמִית. וְאֵינוֹ נִסְקָל עַל פִּיהֶם. וְאִי הֲווּ שְׁנַיִם עֵדִים כְּשֵׁרִים מְעִידִים בַּדָּבָר הָוֵי הַשּׁוֹר בִּסְקִילָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ בַּהֲנָאָה מִיתַּסַר:


או על פי עצמו – that there is no witness there at all, but the owners stated that we saw it (i.e., the firstling) that it had copulated in an unnatural way or that it put someone to death, it (i.e., the animal) is not stoned by their word. For if there were two kosher witnesses testifying in the matter, the bull would be stoned, and it is prohibited to derive benefit from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:9)


**וְטֻמְטוּם וְאַנְדְּרוֹגִינוֹס.** דִּסְפֵק זָכָר הֵם וְקַדְּשֵׁי, סְפֵק נְקֵבָה וְלֹא קַדְּשֵׁי:


וטומטום ואנדרוגינוס – that they are doubtfully male and are Holy, doubtfully female and not holy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:10)


**אֵין לְךָ מוּם גָּדוֹל מִזֶּה.** אַאַנְדְּרוֹגִינוֹס קָאֵי. [דִּמְקוֹם] נַקְבוּת הָוֵי כְמוּם, וְהָוֵי כִבְכוֹר בַּעַל מוּם, וְנִשְׁחָט בַּמְּדִינָה וְאָסוּר בְּגִזָּה וַעֲבוֹדָה:


אין לך מום גדול מזה – it is referring to the beast of double sex, that in the place of females, it is like a blemish, for it is like firstling with a blemish, and it is slaughtered in the provinces, and it is prohibited to use its shearing and its labor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 6:12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/6:12:11)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ בְכוֹר.** דִּבְרִיָּה בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ הוּא. וְנִגְזָז וְנֶעֱבָד. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים אינו בכור – it is a creature of its own kind. And it may be sheared and worked. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:1)


**מוּמִין אֵלּוּ.** הַפּוֹסְלִין בַּבְּכוֹר, פּוֹסְלִים בְּכֹהֵן לַעֲבוֹדָה, בֵּין קְבוּעִים בֵּין עוֹבְרִים כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהֵן עָלָיו:


מומין אלו – that invalidate the firstling, invalidate the Kohen in his Divine service, whether they are permanent or transient all the while that they are upon him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:2)


**הַכִּילוֹן.** שֶׁרֹאשׁוֹ חַד מִלְּמַעְלָה וְרָחָב לְמַטָּה. וְדוֹמֶה לְכִסּוּי שֶׁל חָבִית שֶׁקָּרוּי אַכְלָה, לְפִיכָךְ נִקְרָא כִּילוֹן:


הכילון (one whose head has the shape of a basket/wedge shaped – see Talmud Bekhorot 43b) – that his head is pointed from above and wide below. And it is similar to the covering of a wine jug which is called an אכלה (or אוכלא/basket or אובלא/perforated trough – vessel made of willow trigs) – therefore it is called a כילון.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:3)


**וְהַלַּפְתָּן.** שֶׁרֹאשׁוֹ דּוֹמֶה לְלֶפֶת, שֶׁרְחָבָה מִלְּמַעְלָה וְהֹלֶכֶת וְכָלָה מִלְּמַטָּה:


והלפתן (turnip-shaped) – that his head is similar to a turnip, that it is wide from above and becomes narrower from below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:4)


**מַקָּבָן.** שֶׁדּוֹמֶה רֹאשׁוֹ לְמַקֶּבֶת, שֶׁבּוֹלֵט הַפַּדַּחַת לַחוּץ וְכֵן עָרְפּוֹ מֵאֲחוֹרָיו, וְלִצְדָעָיו שָׁוֶה רֹאשׁוֹ, וְדוֹמֶה לְפַטִּישׁ דְּהַיְנוּ מַקֶּבֶת שֶׁיּוֹצֵא מִלְּפָנָיו וּמֵאַחֲרָיו:


מקבן (one whose head is mallet-shaped – see Tractate Bekhorot 43b) – that his head is similar to a mallet, in that the forehead protrudes outward and similarly, his neck from behind him, but his temples are even with his head, and he is similar to a hammer – that is a mallet that goes forth from before him and from behind him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:5)


**וְרֹאשׁוֹ שָׁקוּט.** גָּרְסִינַן. יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שֶׁרֹאשׁוֹ בּוֹלֵט הַרְבֵּה וְיוֹצֵא לַחוּץ כְּלַפֵּי פָּנָיו. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שֶׁאֵינוֹ בּוֹלֵט כְּלָל לְפָנָיו, כְּעִנְיַן שְׁקִיפָס מֵאֲחוֹרָיו שֶׁאֵין רֹאשׁוֹ בּוֹלֵט מֵאֲחוֹרָיו כְּלָל, וְהוּא מְחֻסָּר, שֶׁדּוֹמֶה כְּמִי שֶׁנֶּחְתַּךְ מִמֶּנּוּ חֲתִיכָה מֵאֲחוֹרָיו. וּלְשׁוֹן שְׁקִיפָס, שְׁקֹל פִּיסָא [סַנְהֶדְרִין סד:], שֶׁנִּטְּלָה מִמֶּנּוּ חֲתִיכָה. וּבַתּוֹסֶפְתָּא מוֹנֶה גַּם כֵּן צַוָּאר שָׁקוּעַ, שֶׁרֹאשׁוֹ מֻטָּל בֵּין כְּתֵפָיו וְדוֹמֶה כְמִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ צַוָּאר:


ושראשו שקוט (whose head is abruptly bent, angular; forehead receding abruptly – see Talmud Bekhorot 43b) – This is our reading. There are those who explain that his head protrudes greatly, and extends outward towards his face. But there are those who explain that it does not protrude at all in front of him, like the matter that it leaps from behind him, that his head does not protrude from behind him at all, and that he is lacking, that it is similar to one that a piece of him is severed from behind him. But the language of שקיפס, is that of a lump of brittle stone that was thrown at it (an idolatrous statue) [see Sanhedrin 64a), that a piece from him was taken. And in the Tosefta it counts also a sunken neck/צואר שקוע, that his head is placed between his shoulders and it is similar to one who has no neck.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:1:6)


**וּבַעֲלֵי הַחֲטוֹטֶרֶת רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַכְשִׁיר.** כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ עֶצֶם בַּחֲטוֹטֶרֶת כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דַהֲוָה מוּם. כִּי פְלִיגֵי בְּשֶׁאֵין בָּהּ עֶצֶם. רַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר חֲתִיכַת בָּשָׂר בְּעָלְמָא הִיא, וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי אָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא כא) כָּל אִישׁ אֲשֶׁר בּוֹ מוּם מִזֶּרַע אַהֲרֹן, הַשָּׁוֶה בְזַרְעוֹ שֶׁל אַהֲרֹן יַעֲבֹד, וְשֶׁאֵינוֹ שָׁוֶה בְזַרְעוֹ שֶׁל אַהֲרֹן לֹא יַעֲבֹד. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ובעלי החטוטרת ר' יהודה מכשיר – that when he has a bone in the hump/hunch, everyone does not dispute that it is a blemish. But they dispute, when it doesn’t have a bone. Rabbi Yehuda holds that it is a mere piece of flesh, but the Rabbis hold, as Scripture States (Leviticus 21:21): “No man among the offspring of Aaron the priest who has a defect [shall be qualified to offer the LORD’s gift],” those who are fit within the seed of Aaron shall serve, but he who is not fit among the seed of Aaron shall not serve. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:1)


**וְאִם יֶשׁ לוֹ הֲרֵי זֶה כָשֵׁר.** וְהוּא שֶׁתִּהְיֶה שִׁטַּת הַשֵּׂעָר הַמַּקֶּפֶת מֵאֹזֶן לְאֹזֶן מֵאֲחוֹרָיו, וְלֹא לְפָנָיו. דִּטְפֵי הָוֵי נוֹי כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ לַאֲחוֹרָיו וְלֹא לְפָנָיו יוֹתֵר מִכְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ סְבִיב כָּל הָרֹאשׁ וּבָאֶמְצַע קֵרֵחַ. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן דְּיֵשׁ לוֹ לְפָנָיו וְלֹא לַאֲחוֹרָיו דְּפָסוּל:


ואם יש לו הרי זה כשר – and he that should have a row of hair that surrounds from ear to ear from behind him, but not from in front of him. For it is of greater beauty when he has it from behind him but not in front of him more than when he has it (i.e., hair) around all of the head and in the middle it is bald. And all the more so, when he has it (i.e., hair) in front of him, but not behind him, that he is disqualified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:2)


**גְּבִינִים.** גַּבּוֹת הָעַיִן:


גבינים – eye-brows.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:3)


**שֶׁגְּבִינָיו שׁוֹכְבִים.** שֶׁשַּׂעֲרוֹת גְּבִינָיו אֲרֻכִּים וּמֻטָּלִים עַל עֵינָיו:


שגביניו שוכבים – that the hair of his eye-brows is long and hanging down over his eyes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:2:4)


**מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁנֵי גַבִּים וּשְׁתֵּי שִׁדְרָאוֹת.** לֹא שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁנֵי גַּבִּים מַמָּשׁ, דְּהַהוּא לֹא חָיֵי. אֶלָּא כְגוֹן שֶׁשִּׁדְרָתוֹ עֲקֻמָּה וּמֵחֲזֵי כְגַבִּין וְשִׁדְרָאוֹת הַרְבֵּה. וְכֻלֵּי עָלְמָא מוֹדוּ דְּכָל הָנֵי מוּמֵי דַּחֲשִׁיבֵי הָנֵי תַּנָּאֵי הָוֵי מוּם, וּמַשְׁמָעוּת דּוֹרְשִׁים בִּלְבַד אִיכָּא בֵּינַיְהוּ, דְּכָל חַד סָבַר שֶׁהַמּוּם שֶׁהוּא מוֹנֶה הוּא גִּבֵּן הָאָמוּר בַּתּוֹרָה:


מי שיש לו שני גבים ושתי שדראות – not that he actually has two backs, for that [person] does not exist. But, as for example, that his backbone is curved and appears like many backs and backbones. And everyone admits that these blemishes that are considered by these Tannaim/teachers are blemishes, and only the implication of their expounding is what is differs between each of them, for each one holds that the blemish that he counts is the גבן /hump-backed (or one having defective eye brows) that is the "גבן" of the Torah (i.e., Leviticus 21:20: “or one who is a hunchback, [or a dwarf, or has a growth in his eye, or who has a boil-scar, or scurvy , or crushed testes].”



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:1)


**הַכּוֹחֵל שְׁתֵּי עֵינָיו כְּאֶחָת.** שֶׁחָטְמוֹ שֶׁבֵּין עֵינָיו שָׁקוּעַ וְאֵינוֹ בּוֹלֵט כְּלָל, עַד שֶׁכְּשֶׁהוּא בָּא לְהַעֲבִיר מִכְחוֹל בְּעֵינָיו יָכוֹל לִמְשֹׁךְ הַמִּכְחוֹל בֵּין עַיִן לְעַיִן וְאֵין הַחֹטֶם מְעַכְּבוֹ:


כוחל שתי עיניו כאחת (he who paints his two eyes in one movement) – that his nose that is between his eyes is sunk and it does not protrude at all, until when he comes to remove the staff used for painting the eye, he is able to stretch the staff used for painting the eye between each eye, but the nose does not prevent it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:2)


**שְׁתֵּי עֵינָיו לְמַעְלָה.** בְּגֹבַהּ הַמֵּצַח שֶׁלֹּא כִשְׁאָר בְּנֵי אָדָם:


שתי עיניו למעלה – at the height of the forehead which is not like the rest of people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:3)


**וּשְׁתֵּי עֵינָיו לְמַטָּה.** מִן הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁנְּהוּגִים לִהְיוֹת:


ושתי עיניו למטה – from the place where they are accustomed to be.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:4)


**עֵינוֹ אַחַת לְמַעְלָה.** מִן הַמָּקוֹם הַנָּהוּג:


עינו אחת למעלה – from the customary place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:5)


**וְעֵינוֹ אַחַת לְמַטָּה.** מִן הַמָּקוֹם הַנָּהוּג:


ואינו אחת למטה – from the customary place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:6)


**רוֹאֶה אֶת הַחֶדֶר וְאֶת הָעֲלִיָּה כְאֶחָת.** שֶׁעֵינָיו עוֹמְדוֹת בִּמְקוֹמָן הָרָאוּי אֲבָל עֵינוֹ אַחַת רוֹאָה לְמַעְלָה וְעֵינוֹ אַחַת לְמַטָּה, חֶדֶר לְמַטָּה וַעֲלִיָּה לְמַעְלָה. וְכָל מִי שֶׁהוּא מְעֻוַּת הָרְאוּת עוֹשֶׂה כֵן, שֶׁמְּדַבֵּר עִם חֲבֵרוֹ וְנִרְאֶה כְאִלּוּ מַבִּיט בִּפְנֵי אָדָם אַחֵר:


רואה את החדר ואת העליה כאחת – that his eyes stand in their appropriate place, but one of his eyes sees above and his other eye sees below, the room below and the attic above, and anyone who sees crooked that he is speaking with his fellow and it appears as if he is looking into the face of another person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:7)


**סָכֵי שֶׁמֶשׁ.** כְּשֶׁרוֹצֶה לְהַבִּיט אֶל הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ מְעַמֵּץ עֵינָיו וְסוֹגֵר עַפְעַפָּיו:


סכי שמש (squinting, cross-eyed) – that when he wants to look into the sun, he closes his eyes and closes his eyelids (see also Talmud Bekhorot 44a, where Rabbi Yose changes it to read – סני שמש/who hates the sun, blinkard).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:8)


**זוּגְדוֹס.** זוּג שְׁנַיִם. דּוֹס בִּלְשׁוֹן רוֹמִי שְׁנַיִם. כְּלוֹמַר, הַזּוּג שֶׁל עֵינַיִם אוֹ שֶׁל גְּבִינֵי הָעַיִן שֶׁדַּרְכָּם לִהְיוֹת שָׁוִין חָלוּק לִשְׁנֵי עִנְיָנִים. כְּגוֹן עֵינוֹ אַחַת שְׁחֹרָה וְאַחַת כְּעֵין הַתְּכֵלֶת. בְּאַחַת מִגַּבּוֹת עֵינָיו שֵׂעָר מְרֻבֶּה וּבְאַחַת מְעַט. וְכֵן בְּכָל שְׁאָר זוּגוֹת שֶׁל אֵבָרִים כָּל זוּגָא דְּלֹא שָׁווּ לַהֲדָדֵי, זַגְדוֹס קָרֵי לֵיהּ:


זגדוס (unmatched; one with an unequal pair of eyes or eye-brows) – a זוג /pair is two; דוס in the Roman language is two. Meaning to say, the pair of eyes or the eyelids of the eyes which are customarily equivalent are divided into two matters. As for example, his one eye is black and the other is a kind of tekelet/blue-green. In one of his eye brows there is a lot of hair and in the other, a little. And similarly, in all of the other pairs of limbs, each pair is not similar to each other. He is called a זגדוס.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:9)


**צִירָן.** שֶׁעֵינָיו זוֹלְגוֹת, דּוֹלְפוֹת וְיוֹרְדוֹת תָּמִיד דִּמְעָה:


צירן (one whose eyes discharge a briny liquid, bleary-eyed) – whose eyes flow with tears, dripping or always running (see Talmud Bekhorot 44a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:10)


**שֶׁנָּשְׁרוּ רִיסֵי עֵינָיו.** שֶׁנָּפַל שֵׂעָר שֶׁבְּעַפְעַפָּיו:


שנשרו ריסי עיניו – that the hair fell from his eye-lashes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:3:11)


**מִפְּנֵי מַרְאִית הָעָיִן.** אֲבָל מוּם גָּמוּר לֹא הָוֵי. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁנִּשְׁאַר בָּעַפְעַפַּיִם רֹשֶׁם שֵׂעָר, אֲבָל אִם לֹא נִשְׁאַר רֹשֶׁם לַשֵּׂעָר כָּל עִקָּר, הֲרֵי זֶה מוּם גָּמוּר. וְכֵן אִי נְפִישֵׁי שְׂעָרֵי בְּרִיסֵי הָעַיִן, הָוֵי מוּם גָּמוּר. וּשְׁלֹשָׁה דִינִים חֲלוּקִים יֵשׁ בְּמוּמֵי אָדָם. הַמּוּמִין הַשָּׁוִין בָּאָדָם וּבַבְּהֵמָה, כֹּהֵן שֶׁעָבַר וְעָבַד בָּהֶן לוֹקֶה וַעֲבוֹדָתוֹ מְחֻלֶּלֶת. וְהַמּוּמִין הַיְתֵרִים בָּאָדָם וְאֵינָן בַּבְּהֵמָה, לוֹקֶה אִם עָבַד וְאֵין עֲבוֹדָתוֹ מְחֻלֶּלֶת. וְהַמּוּמִין שֶׁהֵן מִפְּנֵי מַרְאִית הָעַיִן, אִם עָבַר וְעָבַד אֵינוֹ לוֹקֶה, וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר שֶׁאֵין עֲבוֹדָתוֹ מְחֻלֶּלֶת:


מפני מראית העין – but it is not a complete blemish. And these words [apply] when there remains in his eyelashes a trace of hair, but if there doesn’t remain even the slightest trace of hair, behold it is a complete blemish. And similarly, if there is a lot of hair in the eye-lid with eye-lashes it is a complete blemish. But there are three laws allotted in the blemishes of a human being. There are equivalent blemishes in man and beasts, a Kohen who transgressed and served with them is flogged and his Divine service is profaned. But the additional blemishes in a person that are not found in animals, a he is flogged but if he served, his Divine service is not profaned. But the blemishes which are on account of being repulsive to look at (i.e, appearance’s sake) if he transgressed and served [in the Temple], he is not flogged, and there is no need to state that his Divine service is profaned.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:4:1)


**גּוּפוֹ גָדוֹל מֵאֵבָרָיו.** שִׁעוּר גּוּפוֹ גָּדוֹל מִן הָרָאוּי לְפִי שִׁעוּר שְׁאָר אֵבָרָיו כְּגוֹן יָדָיו וְרַגְלָיו וְשׁוֹקָיו:


גופו גדול מיבריו – the measurement of his body is larger than appropriate according to the measurement of the remainder of his limbs, as for example, his hands, and his feet and his legs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:4:2)


**חָטְמוֹ גָדוֹל.** שִׁעוּר הַחֹטֶם כְּשִׁעוּר אֶצְבַּע קְטַנָּה שֶׁל יָדוֹ. וְאִם הָיָה אָרֹךְ מִכֵּן אוֹ קָצָר מִכֵּן, הֲרֵי זֶה מוּם:


חוטמו גדול – the measurement of his nose is like the measurement of a small finger of his hand. But if it was long than this or shorter than this, it is a blemish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:4:3)


**דּוֹמוֹת לִסְפוֹג.** שֶׁכְּוֻּצוֹת וּסְתוּמוֹת:


דומות לספוג – shrunk and closed up.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:1)


**דַּדָּיו שׁוֹכְבִים.** גְּדוֹלִים עַד שֶׁנִּרְאִים שׁוֹכְבִים כְּדַדֵּי אִשָּׁה:


דדיו שוכבים – large [breasts] until they appear as if they lie like the breasts of a woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:2)


**נִכְפֶּה.** נוֹפֵל מֵחֲמַת חֹלִי:


נכפה – it falls on account of illness (i.e., epilepsy).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:3)


**אֲפִלּוּ אַחַת לְיָמִים.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹפֵל תָּדִיר אֶלָּא פַּעַם אַחַת לִזְמַן מְרֻבֶּה:


אפילו אחת לימים – that it doesn’t regularly fall, but rather one time in a long while.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:4)


**רוּחַ קַצְרִית.** שֶׁרוּחוֹ קְצָרָה מֵחֲמַת [מָרָה] הַשְּׁחֹרָה הַגּוֹבֶרֶת עָלָיו לִפְרָקִים וְנִשְׁאַר בְּלֹא הֶרְגֵּשׁ:


רוח קצרית (a person subjected to asthmatic spells) – that his breath is short on account of black [gall/bile] that overcomes him periodically, and he remains without any sensation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:5)


**הַמְאֻשְׁכָּן.** שֶׁכִּיס שֶׁל בֵּיצִים שֶׁלּוֹ אָרֹךְ עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לָאַרְכּוּבָה. בֵּין שֶׁהוּא נָפוּחַ מֵחֲמַת רוּחַ אוֹ מֵחֲמַת לֵחָה:


המאושכן (one having large testicles – see Talmud Bekhorot 44b) – that the sack of his testicles is long until it reaches the leg from under the hip-bone to the ankle, whether it is swollen on account of wind or on account of moisture.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:6)


**וּבַעַל גֶּבֶר.** בַּעַל אֵבָר. שֶׁנִּתְאָרֵךְ הַגִּיד שֶׁלּוֹ וְנַעֲשָׂה גָּדוֹל עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לְאַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו:


ובעל גבר – membrum genitale (i.e., the penis). That his membrum virile became long and became large until it reached the leg from under the hip-bone to the ankle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:7)


**שֶׁנִּמְרְחוּ אֲשָׁכָיו.** שֶׁנִּמּוֹחוּ בֵּיצָיו:


שנמרחו אשכיו- that his testicles became crushed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:8)


**שֶׁרוּחַ בַּאֲשָׁכָיו.** וְעַל יְדֵי כֵּן בֵּיצָיו נְפוּחִים:


שרוח באשכיו – and through this, his testicles are swollen/blown up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:5:9)


**שֶׁמַּרְאָיו חֲשׁוּכִין.** שֶׁהוּא שָׁחֹר. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ שָׁחֹר כְּכוּשִׁי. וּלְשׁוֹן מְרוֹחַ אָשֶׁךְ, מַרְאֶה חֹשֶׁךְ. וְלָאו בְּבֵיצִים מִשְׁתַּעֵי קְרָא. וּבְעִקַּר הַדִּין לֹא פְלִיגֵי, שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶם מוֹדֶה בְּמוּם שֶׁמּוֹנֶה חֲבֵרוֹ דַּהֲוֵי מוּם. וְלֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא בְּפֵרוּשׁוֹ שֶׁל מִקְרָא, וּמַשְׁמָעוּת דּוֹרְשִׁין אִיכָּא בֵּינַיְהוּ:


שמראיו חשוכין – that it is dark. And even though it is not black like a Cushite. And the language [of the words] "מרוח אשך"/crushed testes (see Leviticus 21:20), its complexion is dark. But Scripture is not referring to the testicles. But in the essence of the law, there is no dispute, for each one of them (i.e., Rabbi Yismael, Rabbi Akiba and Rabbi Hanina ben Antignos) agree that a blemish that his fellow counts is a blemish. But they did not disagree other than in the explanation of Scripture, and the homiletical implications divide them.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:1)


**הַמַּקִּישׁ בְּקַרְסֻלָּיו.** שֶׁאַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו עֲקֻמּוֹת לַחוּץ וְקַרְסֻלָּיו לִפְנִים וְנוֹקְשִׁים זֶה בָּזֶה כְּשֶׁהוּא מְהַלֵּךְ. וּמַקִּישׁ בְּאַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו הָוֵי אִפְּכָא, שֶׁרַגְלָיו עֲקֻמִּים כְּלַפֵּי חוּץ וּמְרֻחָקִים זוֹ מִזּוֹ עַד שֶׁאַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו לְמַעְלָה [הָעֲקֻמּוֹת] כְּלַפֵּי פְּנִים נוֹקְשׁוֹת זוֹ בְּזוֹ:


המקיש בקרסוליו – that his knees are curved outwardly and his ankles inwardly and they strike against each other when he walks. Or he strikes with his knees -this is the opposite, that his legs are curved towards the outside and they are distanced from each other until that his [curved] knees are above towards the inside, striking each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:2)


**בַּעַל הַפִּיקִין וְהָעִקֵּל.** וְקָא פָרִישׁ עִקֵּל בְּרֵישָׁא:


בעל הפיקין והעיקל (swellings and bandy-legged) – and he explains עיקל/bandy-legged in the first clause [of the Mishnah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:3)


**כֹּל שֶׁהוּא מַקִּיף פַּרְסוֹתָיו.** כְּשֶׁיּוֹשֵׁב וּמְקָרֵב פַּרְסוֹת רַגְלָיו זוֹ לָזוֹ:


כל שהוא מקיף פרסותיו – when he sits and brings close the soles of his feet one to the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:4)


**וְאֵין אַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו.** נוֹגְעוֹת זוֹ לָזוֹ. שֶׁעֲקֻמּוֹת כְּלַפֵּי חוּץ. וּבַעַל הַפִּיקִין הוּא שֶׁפִּיקָה יוֹצֵא מִגּוּדָלוֹ, חֲתִיכַת בָּשָׂר עֲגֻלָּה כְּפִיקָה יוֹצְאָה מִגּוּדָל שֶׁל יָדוֹ אוֹ מִגּוּדָל שֶׁל רַגְלוֹ:


ואין ארכובותיו נוגעות זו לזו – that they are curved towards the outside. And someone who has swelling/a lump that the swelling goes out from his great toe, a piece of flesh is round like a swelling/lump goes out from the thumb of his hand or from the great toe of his foot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:5)


**עֲקֵבוֹ יוֹצֵא מֵאֲחוֹרָיו.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁשּׁוֹקוֹ עוֹמֵד בְּאֶמְצַע רַגְלוֹ, שֶׁחֲצִי הָרֶגֶל לְפָנִים וַחֲצִי רֶגֶל לְאָחוֹר:


עקבו יוצא מאחוריו (his heel juts outward) – as for example, that his foreleg stands in the middle of his foot, that half of his foot is towards the inside and half of his foot is towards the outside/back.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:6)


**רְחָבוֹת כְּשֶׁל אַוָּז.** שֶׁקְּלוּשׁוֹת הֵן כְּשֶׁל אַוָּז וְאֵין אָרְכָּן יוֹתֵר עַל רָחְבָּן:


רחבות בשל אווז – they are weekend like that of a goose and their length is not not greater than their width.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:7)


**קְלוּטוֹת.** מְדֻבָּקוֹת זוֹ בָּזוֹ:


קלוטות – attached to each other (i.e., webbed)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:8)


**עַד הַפֶּרֶק.** הָאֶמְצָעִי שֶׁל אֶצְבָּעוֹת:


עד הפרק – the middle [joint] of the toes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:9)


**לְמַטָּה מִן הַפֶּרֶק.** דְּהַיְנוּ לְצַד הַצִּפֹּרֶן שֶׁמְּחֻבָּרִים כֻּלָּן:


למטה מן הפרק – that is to say, to the side of the nail that is entirely attached.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:10)


**וַחֲתָכָהּ.** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְחֻלָּקִים, כָּשֵׁר:


וחתכן- that they will be separated/divided, it is kosher/appropriate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:11)


**הָיְתָה בוֹ יְתֵרָה.** אֶצְבַּע יָתֶרֶת:


היתה בו יתירה – an extra toe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:12)


**אִם יֶשׁ בָּהּ עֶצֶם פָּסוּל.** דְּהוֹאִיל וַחֲתָכָהּ הֲרֵי הוּא מְחֻסָּר אֵבָר שֶׁנִּמְצָא בוֹ:


אם יש בה עצם פסול – for since he severed it, it is lacing a limb that is found in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:13)


**שֵׁשׁ וָשֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע.** כְּלוֹמַר אֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ אֶצְבְּעוֹת יָדָיו וְאֶצְבְּעוֹת רַגְלָיו שָׁוִין, שֶׁבְּכָל יָד וּבְכָל רֶגֶל יֵשׁ שֵׁשׁ וָשֵׁשׁ, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי חֲכָמִים פּוֹסְלִים. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם הָיָה בְיָדוֹ אַחַת חָמֵשׁ וּבְיָדוֹ אַחַת שֵׁשׁ, דַּהֲוֵי מוּם טְפֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


שש ושש עשרים וארבע – meaning to say, even if the fingers of his hand and the toes of his foot were equivalent, that on each hand and foot there are six [digits] apiece, even so, the Sages disqualify it. And all the more so if there were on one of its hands five [digits] and in the other hand six [digits], that is an even greater blemish. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:14)


**הַשּׁוֹלֵט בִּשְׁתֵּי יָדָיו רַבִּי פּוֹסֵל.** דְּסָבַר כְּחִישׁוּתָא אִתְיְלִידָא בֵיהּ בְּיָמִין:


השולט בשתי ידיו רבי פוסל – for he holds the weakness that he was born with was in his right hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:15)


**וַחֲכָמִים מַכְשִׁירִין.** סָבְרֵי בְּרִיאוּתָא הִיא דְּאִתְיְלִידָא לֵיהּ בִּשְׂמֹאל. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מכשירין – they hold that he has (unusual) strength which happened to rest in the left hand (see Talmud Bekhorot 45b). And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:16)


**הַכּוּשִׁי.** שָׁחֹר:


הכושי – the black [person].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:17)


**הַגִּיחוֹר.** אָדֹם כְּאַרְגָּמָן:


הגיחור (red-spotted in the face) – red like a purple garment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:18)


**הַלַּבְקָן.** לָבָן בְּיוֹתֵר:


הלבקן – extremely white (i.e., albino)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:19)


**וְהַקִּפֵּחַ.** אָרֹךְ וָדַק, שֶׁאֵינוֹ עָב לְפִי אָרְכּוֹ. וְהוּא מְכֹעָר וּמְגֻנֶּה:


והקפה – long and thin, that is not thick according to its length, and he is ugly and reprehensible.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:20)


**וְהַשִּׁכּוֹר.** וְלֹא מִיַּיִן, הוּא דַּחֲשִׁיב כְּבַעַל מוּם מִן הַמּוּמִים הַיְתֵרִים בָּאָדָם, שֶׁאִם עָבַד לוֹקֶה וְאֵין עֲבוֹדָתוֹ מְחֻלֶּלֶת. אֲבָל הַשִּׁכּוֹר מִיַּיִן וְשֵׁכָר, אִם עָבַד חִלֵּל. דִּבְפָרָשַׁת שְׁתוּיֵי יַיִן כְּתִיב (ויקרא י) וּלְהַבְדִּיל בֵּין הַקֹּדֶשׁ וּבֵין הַחֹל. וְשִׁכּוֹר דְּמַתְנִיתִין אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא שֶׁשָּׁתָה חָלָב הַרְבֵּה, אוֹ רֹב דְּבַשׁ, אוֹ אָכַל דְּבֵלָה קַעִילִית, וְנִתְבַּלְבְּלָה דַעְתּוֹ. שֶׁאֵלּוּ חֲשׁוּבִים כְּבַעֲלֵי מוּמִים וְאֵין מְחַלְּלִים עֲבוֹדָה:


והשכור – but not from wine, it is considered like a blemish from the greatest blemishes in a person, for if he performed Divine Service, he is flogged, and his Divine Service is not desecrated. But the drunkard from wine and alcohol, if he served, he has profaned/caused the loss of priestly status. For in the portion of those who drink wine it is written (Leviticus 10:10): “for you must distinguish between the sacred and the profane,” and someone who is drunk of our Mishnah is not one other than having drunk a great deal of wine or much honey, or that ate cakes of twisted pressed figs, and his mind became confused. These are considered like having blemishes and they do not profane the Divine Service.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:21)


**וּבַעֲלֵי נְגָעִים טְהוֹרִים.** כְּגוֹן בֹּהַק וְכֻלּוֹ הָפַךְ לָבָן. דְּאִי מִנְּגָעִים טְמֵאִים לֹא צָרִיךְ לְמֵימַר, דַּאֲפִלּוּ אַכְּנִיסַת עֲזָרָה חַיָּב כָּרֵת:


ובעלי נגעים טהורים – as for example (Leviticus 13:39): “it is a tetter broken out on the skin” or (Leviticus 13:13): “for he has turned all white.” For if it was from impure Negaim/plagues (or suspected leprosy), it is not necessary to state it, for even with his entrance into the temple courtyard, he is liable for extirpation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:6:22)


**דִּלְדּוּלִין.** כְּמִין חֲתִיכוֹת בָּשָׂר יוֹצְאוֹת תְּלוּיוֹת בָּהֶן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב:


דלדוליו (persons or animals afflicted with large warts) – like pieces of flesh that come out and suspend in them. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:1)


**אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ.** אָב וּבְנוֹ כֹהֵן עוֹבְדִים עֲבוֹדָה בְּיוֹם אֶחָד וּבִזְמַן אֶחָד. וְאֵין אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ בַּבְּהֵמָה קְרֵבִין בְּיוֹם אֶחָד:


אותו ואת בנו – a father and son who are Kohanim perform Temple service on the same day and at the same time. But the progenitor and his offspring of animals are not offered up on the same day (see Tractate Hullin, Chapter 5, Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:2)


**וּטְרֵפָה.** שֶׁנּוֹלַד בָּהּ אֶחָד מִסִּימָנֵי טְרֵפוֹת הַמְּנוּיִין בְּפֶרֶק אֵלּוּ טְרֵפוֹת, אֵינָהּ קְרֵבָה עַל גַּבֵּי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


וטריפה – that was born with one of the signs of a (fatal) organic disease -which are delineated in the chapter [three – Tractate Hullin] “These are the Treifah carcasses” (see Mishnah 1 – regarding cattle, and Mishnah 3 among fowl), which is not offered on the Altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:3)


**וְיוֹצֵא דֹפֶן.** שֶׁקָּרְעוּ אֶת אִמּוֹ וְהוֹצִיאוּהוּ חַי. בַּבְּהֵמָה, פְּסוּלָה לְהַקְרָבָה, דְּכִי יֻלַּד כְּתִיב, פְּרָט לְיוֹצֵא דֹפֶן. וּבְכֹהֵן הַמַּקְרִיב לֹא מִפְּסִיל בְּהָכִי:


ויוצא דופן – that they tore open its mother and removed it while living. With an animal, it is disqualified from being offered, for it is written (Leviticus 22:26): “[When an ox or a sheep or a goat] is born, [it shall stay seven days with its mother, and from the eighth day on it shall be acceptable as an offering by fire to the LORD],” excluding that which is born from the side (i.e., Caesarean birth). But with a Kohen who offers [having been born by Caesarean section], he is not disqualified though this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:4)


**הַנּוֹשֵׂא נָשִׁים בַּעֲבֵרָה.** כֹּהֵן שֶׁנָּשָׂא גְּרוּשָׁה וַחֲלָלָה זוֹנָה, נוֹדֵר וְעוֹבֵד. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא גֵרֵשׁ מֻתָּר לַעֲבֹד אַחַר שֶׁנָּדַר שֶׁלֹּא יֵהָנֶה הוּא מִמֶּנָּה וְהִיא מִמֶּנּוּ עַד שֶׁיְּגָרֵשׁ. וְהוּא שֶׁיִּדֹּר עַל דַּעַת רַבִּים שֶׁאֵין לוֹ הַתָּרָה אֶלָּא לִדְבַר מִצְוָה. וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ שֶׁמָּא יֵלֵךְ אֵצֶל חָכָם וְיֹאמַר דִּלְצֹרֶךְ דְּבַר מִצְוָה הוּא שׁוֹאֵל שֶׁיַּתִּירוּ לוֹ אֶת נִדְרוֹ, דְּכֵיוָן דְּקַיְמָא לָן שֶׁהַבָּא לְהַתִּיר נִדְרוֹ צָרִיךְ לְפָרֵשׁ עַל מַה נָּדַר, וַדַּאי כִּי שָׁמַע חָכָם דְּמִשּׁוּם שֶׁהִיא גְרוּשָׁה אוֹ זוֹנָה דַּאֲסוּרָה לוֹ נָדַר לֹא שָׁרֵי לֵיהּ נִדְרוֹ:


הנושא נשים בעבירה – a Kohen who married a divorcee or the female’s issue of a priest’s illegitimate connection/ a priest’s wife illegitimately married to him or a prostitute unfit to marry a priest, he takes a vow and serves [in the Temple]. Even though he has not yet divorced [her], he is permitted to serve after he vowed that he will not benefit from her and she will not benefit from him until he divorces [her]. And he shall take the vow with the knowledge of the community which has no releasing [of the vow] other than for the performance of a Mitzvah. And we should not suspect lest he will go to a Sage and state that for the need of a particular Mitzvah, he asks of him that he should release him from his vow, for since we hold that a person who goes to release his vow must explain upon what he has taken a vow, certainly when the Sage heard that it was because she is a divorcee or a prostitute, that she is forbidden to him through a vow, he will not permit his vow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/7:7:5)


**עַד שֶׁיְּקַבֵּל עָלָיו שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא מִטַּמֵּא לְמֵתִים.** הָכָא סַגִּי לֵיהּ בְּקַבָּלָה, וְלֹא מַדְּרִינַן לֵיהּ כְּמוֹ גַבֵּי נוֹשֵׂא נָשִׁים בַּעֲבֵרָה. מִשּׁוּם דְּהָתָם יִצְרוֹ תוֹקְפוֹ, הָכָא גַבֵּי טֻמְאָה אֵין יִצְרוֹ תוֹקְפוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ בְּקַבָּלָה גְרֵידָא סַגִּי לֵיהּ:


עד שיקבל עליו שלא יהא מיטמא למתים - here it is enough for him with acceptance, for we don’t force him to take a vow [of deriving benefit] like concerning the sinful marrying of [forbidden] women, because there, his [evil] inclination overpowers him, [whereas] here, regarding [corpse] defilement, his [evil] inclination does not overpower him. Therefore, with mere acceptance, it is sufficient for him.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:1)


**יֵשׁ בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה.** לִטֹּל פִּי שְׁנַיִם:


יש בכור לנחלה – to take a double portion (see Deuteronomy 21:17).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ בְכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן.** לָתֵת לַכֹּהֵן פִּדְיוֹנוֹ חֲמֵשֶׁת סְלָעִים:


ואינו כבו לכהן – to give to a Kohen his redemption price of five Selas (see Numbers 18:16).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:3)


**הַבָּא אַחַר הַנְּפָלִים.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיוּ תְאוֹמִים, אֶחָד מֵהֶם נֵפֶל שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ לוֹ חֳדָשָׁיו וְאֶחָד כָּלוּ לוֹ חֳדָשָׁיו, וְהַנֵּפֶל שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ לוֹ חֳדָשָׁיו יָצָא רֹאשׁוֹ חַי וְהֶחֱזִירוֹ, וּקְדָמוֹ אָחִיו וְיָצָא. זֶה הָאַחֲרוֹן בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה, דְּרִאשׁוֹן לֹא הִפְסִידוֹ, וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּיצִיאַת הָרִאשׁוֹן חֲשִׁיבָא לֵידָה מְעַלְּיָא. וְטַעֲמָא, מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב רֵאשִׁית אֹנוֹ, מִי שֶׁלֵּב אָבִיו דָּוֶה עָלָיו אִם מֵת, יָצָא נֵפֶל שֶׁהוֹצִיא רֹאשׁוֹ רִאשׁוֹן דְּלָאו בַּר קְיָמָא [הוּא] דְּאֵין לִבּוֹ דָּוֶה עָלָיו. אֲבָל לְעִנְיַן פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם פָּטוּר הַאי וָלָד [אַחֲרוֹן], דִּבְפֶטֶר רֶחֶם תָּלָה רַחֲמָנָא, וַהֲרֵי הָרִאשׁוֹן פָּטַר אֶת הָרֶחֶם:


הבא אחר הנפלים – as for example, that they were twins. One of whom was premature that his months [of fetal development] were not finished for him, and [the other one], whose months [of fetal development] were completed for him, but the premature one whose months [of fetal development] were not finished for him, his head came out alive and he retracted himself, and his [twin] brother preceded him and went out. This latter [of the two] is the first-born for inheritance, for the first one [whose head came out alive] did not cause him to suffer loss, and even though the exodus of the first [fetus] is considered to be a valid birth. And the reason, because it is written (Deuteronomy 21:17): “since he is the first fruit of his vigor,” that [child over whose death] the heart of the father is grieved (see Tractate Niddah 23b) if he (i.e., the child) dies, this excludes the premature [child] whose head came out first, who is not a viable child, [he] is one whom the heart of the father is not grieved for. But in regard to the opening of the womb (i.e., being the first born/first birth), this [latter] child, for the All-Merciful assigned, but the first [child whose head had exited the womb], was the firstborn of the womb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:4)


**וּבֶן תִּשְׁעָה מֵת שֶׁיָּצָא רֹאשׁוֹ.** וְהֶחֱזִירוֹ, וְיָצָא אָחִיו, הָוֵי בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה. דְּהָא רִאשׁוֹן לָא חֲשִׁיב, דְּאֵין לִבּוֹ דָּוֶה עָלָיו, וּלְעִנְיַן כֹּהֵן לָאו פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם הוּא. אֲבָל יָצָא רֹאשׁ בֶּן תִּשְׁעָה חַי וְאַחַר כָּךְ הֶחֱזִירוֹ וָמֵת, בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה נַמִּי לֹא הָוֵי זֶה הַבָּא אַחֲרָיו:


ובן תשעה מת שיצא ראשו – and went back inside [the womb], and his brother came out [fully first], is the first-born for inheritance. But he is not considered the first, for his father is not grieved for him, but regarding the Kohen [for the redemption of the first-born], he is not the first-born of the womb. Ut if his head came out first alive at the age of nine [months] and afterwards, he was restored [to the womb] and [subsequently] died, he that comes after him also is not the first-born for inheritance.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:5)


**וְכֵן הַמַּפֶּלֶת בְּהֵמָה כוּ'.** לְמִפְטַר מִנַּחֲלָה לָא חֲשִׁיבֵי, דְּאֵין הַלֵּב דָּוֶה עֲלֵיהֶם, אֲבָל פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם מִיהָא הָוֵי:


וכן המפלת בהמה כו' – it is not considered to be exempted from inheritance, for the heart of the father is not grieved for them. But it is the first-born of the womb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בוֹ מִצּוּרַת אָדָם.** לָא חֲשִׁיב פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם, וְהַבָּא אַחֲרָיו הָוֵי בְּכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים עד שיהא בו מצורת אדם – it is not considered the firstborn after the womb, and the one who comes after him is a first born for a Kohen (i.e., requiring redemption by his parents). And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:7)


**סַנְדָּל.** חֲתִיכַת בָּשָׂר עֲשׂוּיָה כִּדְמוּת סַנְדָּל, וְאֵין לָהּ צוּרַת אֵבָרִים, וּרְגִילָה לָבֹא עִם וָלָד. וּלְשׁוֹן סַנְדָּל יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שָׂנְאוּי וָדַל:


סנדל (a flat, fish-shaped abortion -see Tractate Niddah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 4) – a piece of flesh made in the image of a sandal/flat fish, and it lacks the form of limbs, and regularly comes together with the child. But the language of סנדל has explanations/commentaries that are hated and poor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:8)


**אוֹ שִׁלְיָא.** דְּאֵין שִׁלְיָא בְּלֹא וָלָד, אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּמּוֹחַ, וּמִיהוּ פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם הָוֵי:


או שליא – that there is no placenta without a child but that it is softened. But however, it is the opening of the womb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:9)


**וְשָׁפִיר מְרֻקָּם.** חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל בָּשָׂר שֶׁמְּרֻקָּם בָּהּ צוּרַת הָעֻבָּר. וְעַל שֵׁם שֶׁעֲשׂוּיָה כִשְׁפוֹפֶרֶת שֶׁל בֵּיצָה קְרוּיָה שָׁפִיר:


ושפיר מרוקם (fully-formed fetus)– a piece of flesh that is has the articulated shape of the form of a fetus. And because it is made like an egg-shell, it is called a sack of a fetus.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:10)


**וְהַיּוֹצֵא מְחֻתָּךְ.** שֶׁנֶּחְתַּךְ אֵבָר אֵבָר וּבְדֶרֶךְ זֶה יָצָא כֻלּוֹ. אֲבָל רֹאשׁ מְחֻתָּךְ, לֹא פָּטַר אִם קָדַם אָחִיו וְיָצָא קֹדֶם יְצִיאַת רֹב אֶבְרֵי הַמְחֻתָּךְ, וְהָוֵי נַמִּי בְּכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן:


והיוצא מחותך – that it is severed limb by limb and in this manner it all came out. But if the head is severed, it was not the first of the womb if his brother preceded and came out before the coming out of most of the limbs of the severed one, and it would be also be a first born for the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:11)


**שֶׁכְּבָר יָלְדָה.** דִּלְעִנְיַן נַחֲלָה דְאָב כְּתִיב רֵאשִׁית אֹנוֹ, וּלְעִנְיַן כֹּהֵן תְּלָא רַחֲמָנָא בְּפֶטֶר רֶחֶם:


שכבר ילדה - that in regard to inheritance of the father, it is written (Deuteronomy 21:17): “the first fruit of his vigor”, but regarding the Kohen, the All-Merciful depends upon the opening of the womb (see Exodus 13:2 and 12).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:12)


**עוֹדָהּ שִׁפְחָה.** אֲפִלּוּ עוֹדָהּ שִׁפְחָה בְּלֵידָה רִאשׁוֹנָה וְנִשְׁתַּחְרְרָה עַכְשָׁיו, אוֹ נָכְרִית וְנִתְגַּיְּרָה וּמִשֶּׁבָּאת לְיַד יִשְׂרָאֵל יָלְדָה, נַמִּי הָוֵי בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא הָיוּ לָזֶה בָּנִים:


עודה שפחה – even if she is still a heathen maid-servant at her first birth and was freed now. Or [if] she was a heathen and converted but when she came into the hand of an Israelite, she gave birth, also [the child] is a first born for inheritance, for he had not [yet] had children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:13)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי אוֹמֵר בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה וְלַכֹּהֵן.** דְּבָנִים הַנּוֹלָדִים בְּנָכְרִיּוּתָהּ לֹא פָּטְרֵי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי:


רבי יוסי הגלילי אומר בכור לנחלה ולכהן – for the sons that are born while she was a heathen do not open the womb. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi HaGelili.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:14)


**נִתְגַּיְּרָה מְעֻבֶּרֶת.** וּבַעְלָהּ עִמָּהּ וְיָלְדָה. הֲרֵי אוֹתוֹ וָלָד בְּכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן, דְּפֶטֶר רֶחֶם הוּא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל. וְלֹא לַנַּחֲלָה, דְּכֵיוָן דְּהוֹרָתוֹ שֶׁלֹּא בִּקְדֻשָּׁה לָאו בַּר נַחֲלָה הוּא, דְּזֶרַע עוֹבֵד כּוֹכָבִים רַחֲמָנָא אַפְקְרֵיהּ, דִּכְתִיב (יחזקאל כג) וְזִרְמַת סוּסִים זִרְמָתָם:


נתגיירה מעוברת – and her husband is with her and she gave birth. Behold that child is a firstborn for a Kohen, for he is the firstborn of the womb for an Israelite, but not for inheritance, for since his conception is not in holiness (because she was not yet a Jewish woman at that time), he is not eligible for inheritance, for he is the seed of an idolater, the All-Merciful declared him free/ownerless, as it is written (Ezekiel 23:20): “[she lusted for concubinage with them, whose members were like those of asses] and whose organs like those of stallions.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:15)


**יָלְדָה הִיא וְכֹהֶנֶת.** בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל מְבַכֶּרֶת שֶׁיָּלְדָה עִם כֹּהֶנֶת מְבַכֶּרֶת וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אֵי זֶה וָלָד שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


וילדה היא וכהנת – a Jewish woman who had never given birth and daughter of a Kohen who had given birth, and it is not know which of them (i.e., the babies born) was the child of an Israelite woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:16)


**אוֹ הִיא וּלְוִיָּה.** דִּלְוִיָּה נַמִּי בְנָהּ פָּטוּר מֵחָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים. כְּדַאֲמַרַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא:


או היא ולויה – for a Levite woman, her son is also exempt from the five Selaim, as we stated in the first chapter (see actually, Tractate Bekhorot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:17)


**אוֹ הִיא וְאִשָּׁה שֶׁכְּבָר יָלְדָה.** וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אֵיזֶה בְנָהּ:


או היא ואשה שכבר ילדה – and it is not known which is her son (as the babies had been mixed up).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:18)


**בְּכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן.** וּבַעְלָהּ שֶׁל מְבַכֶּרֶת חַיָּב חָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים לַכֹּהֵן, דְּהָא בֵּן זָכָר יֵשׁ לוֹ כָל הֵיכָא דְאִיתֵיהּ:


כבור לכהן – and the husband of the woman giving birth for the first time is liable for the five Selaim to the Kohen [for redemption of their first-born son], for the male child has [responsibility] all that he has.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:19)


**וְלֹא לַנַּחֲלָה.** דְּהָא לֹא יָדַע בְּרֵיהּ דְּמַאן הוּא:


ולא לנחלה – for he did not know the son of whom it is.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:20)


**וְכֵן מִי שֶׁלֹּא שָׁהֲתָה כוּ'.** הוּא בְּכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן, וְהוּא יִפְדֶּה אֶת עַצְמוֹ. וְלֹא לַנַּחֲלָה, דְּהָא לֹא יָדַע מַאן יָרֵית. וַאֲפִלּוּ כְפָשׁוּט לֹא יָרֵית, דְּהַאי מַדְחֵי לֵיהּ לְגַבֵּי הַאי וְהַאי מַדְחֵי לֵיהּ לְגַבֵּי הַאי. וּמַתְנִיתִין דְּקָתָנֵי וְלֹא לַנַּחֲלָה, עַל הַבָּא אַחֲרָיו קָאָמַר, שֶׁהַבָּא אַחֲרָיו אֵינוֹ בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה, דְּשֶׁמָּא זֶה הַסָּפֵק בֵּן הָאַחֲרוֹן הוּא:


וכן מי שלא שהתה כו' – he is the first-born for a Kohen. And he will redeem himself. But not for inheritance for he does not know who conceived him. And even like an heir entitled to a single share he was not conceived, for this one (i.e., one child) supersedes him regarding this (i.e., entitlement to being a first-born) and that one (i.e., a different child) supersedes him regarding that (i.e., level of inheritance). But our Mishnah which teaches that he is not [the first-born] for inheritance purposes, it states regarding who comes after him, for the one who comes after him is not the first born for inheritance, for perhaps there is doubt if he is the son after him (see Talmud Bekhorot 47b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:21)


**מָלֵא גְנִינִים.** מָלֵא גְּוָנִים הַרְבֵּה. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, תּוֹלָעִים, שֶׁהַשָּׁפִיר עָשׂוּי כֻּלּוֹ חֲתִיכוֹת קְטַנּוֹת דַּקּוֹת דּוֹמוֹת לְתוֹלָעִים:


מלא גנינים ([an abortion consisting of] a bag full of a many-colored substance – see also Tractate Niddah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 3 and Niddah 24b) – many colors. Another explanation – worms. That the fetus is made completely of many small, thin pieces similar to worms.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:22)


**דָּגִים וַחֲגָבִים.** לָאו וָלָד נִינְהוּ, דְּלֹא נֶאֶמְרָה בָּהֶן יְצִירָה כָּאָדָם:


דגים וחגבים – they are not offspring, for it was not stated about them a creation like a human.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:1:23)


**יוֹם אַרְבָּעִים.** מִשֶּׁנִּתְעַבְּרָה, מַיָּא בְּעָלְמָא נִינְהוּ, וְאֵין כָּאן וָלָד עַד לְמָחֳרַת יוֹם אַרְבָּעִים. דְּאַרְבָּעִים יוֹם הָוֵי יְצִירַת הַוָּלָד:


יום ארבעים – from when she became pregnant, it is merely water, and there isn’t an embryo until the day after the fortieth day, for on the fortieth day is the creation of the embryo.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:1)


**יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן וְהַבָּא אַחֲרָיו.** דֶּרֶךְ רֶחֶם. כְּגוֹן שֶׁקָּרְעוּ אִשָּׁה שֶׁתְּאוֹמִים בְּבִטְנָהּ, וּלְאַחַר שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּ הָרִאשׁוֹן דֶּרֶךְ דֹּפֶן יָצָא הַשֵּׁנִי דֶּרֶךְ רֶחֶם. אֲבָל לְהוֹצִיא וָלָד מִן הָאִשָּׁה דֶּרֶךְ דֹּפֶן וְשֶׁתִּתְרַפֵּא הָאִשָּׁה וְתַחֲזֹר וְתִתְעַבֵּר וְתֵלֵד, כָּתַב רַמְבַּ"ם דְּאִי אֶפְשָׁר:


יוצא דופן והבא אחריו – [the second one] through the womb. As for example, that they tore the woman when twins were in her stomach, and after that they removed the first of them through the wall (i.e., by means of Caesarean section, the second left [the womb] through the womb. But to remove an offspring from the woman through the wall [i.e., via Caesarean section] and when the woman would heal, she would once again become pregnant and give birth [naturally]. Maimonides wrote that this is impossible.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:2)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם אֵינָן בְּכוֹר.** רִאשׁוֹן לֹא הָוֵי בְּכוֹר לַנַּחֲלָה, וְיָלְדוּ לוֹ בָּעִינַן. וְשֵׁנִי נַמִּי לֹא, רֵאשִׁית אֹנוֹ בָּעִינַן. וּבְכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן רִאשׁוֹן לֹא הָוֵי, דְּפֶטֶר רֶחֶם בָּעִינַן. וְשֵׁנִי נַמִּי לֹא הָוֵי בְּכוֹר לַכֹּהֵן, דִּבְכוֹר לָרֶחֶם וְאֵינוֹ בְּכוֹר לַוְּלָדוֹת כְּגוֹן זֶה שֶׁהָיָה וָלָד קֹדֶם לָכֵן, אֵינוֹ בְּכוֹר:


שניהם אינן בכור – the first [child born] is not a firstborn for [a double-portion of the] inheritance, for we require (Deuteronomy 21:15): “[If a man has two wives, one loved and the other unloved,] and both the loved and the unloved have born him sons, [but the first-born is the son of the unloved one].” The second is also not the first-born, for we require (Deuteronomy 21:17): “the first fruit of his vigor.” And the first born for a Kohen – the first is not, for we require (Numbers 18:15): “the first issue of the womb.” And the second [child born] is also not the first born for the Kohen [to be redeemed with five Selaim], for [the child] is the first-born of the womb and not the first born for offspring as for example, this one that was the offspring prior to this is not a firstborn.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר הָרִאשׁוֹן לַנַּחֲלָה.** סָבַר וְיָלְדוּ אַף יוֹצֵא דֹּפֶן בְּמַשְׁמַע:


רבי שמעון אומר הרשון לנחלה – he holds that (Deuteronomy 21:15) “have born [him sons],” that even for that comes out from the wall (i.e., Caesarean section) is implied.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:2:4)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי לְחָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים.** סָבַר בְּכוֹר לָרֶחֶם אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ בְּכוֹר לַוְּלָדוֹת הָוֵי בְּכוֹר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


והשני לחמש סלעים – he holds, [that child] is the firstborn for the womb, even though he is not the first-born for offspring which is a firstborn. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:1)


**וְיָלְדָה שְׁנֵי זְכָרִים נוֹתֵן חָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים.** דְּאֶחָד מֵהֶן בְּכוֹר:


וילדה שני זכרים נותן חמש סלעים – that one of them is a first-born male.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:2)


**הָאָב פָּטוּר.** דְּמָצֵי לְמֵימַר הַבְּכוֹר מֵת. וּכְשֶׁמֵּת הַבֵּן קֹדֶם שְׁלֹשִׁים אֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּפִדְיוֹן, וְעַכְשָׁיו שֶׁהַדָּבָר סָפֵק שֶׁמָּא הַבְּכוֹר מֵת וּפָטוּר, שֶׁמָּא זֶה שֶׁנִּשְׁאַר הוּא הַבְּכוֹר וְחַיָּב, הָוֵי הַכֹּהֵן מוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ, וְהַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה. וְהוּא הַדִּין שֶׁהַבֵּן הַנִּשְׁאָר פָּטוּר מִלִּפְדּוֹת אֶת עַצְמוֹ כְּשֶׁיִּהְיֶה גָּדוֹל:


האב פטור – that he is able to state that the firstborn died. But when the son died prior to [the age of] thirty days [old], he (i.e., the father) is not liable for the redemption [of the first-born son]. But now when the matter is in doubt for perhaps the first-born son died and he (i.e., the father) is exempt, or perhaps, the one who remains is the [actual] first-born son and he (i.e., the father) is liable [for the child’s redemption]. The Kohen removes [the money] from his fellow, for the burden of proof is upon the claimant. And the same rule applies when the remaining son is exempt from redeeming himself when he will be an adult.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:3)


**מֵת הָאָב.** לְאַחַר שְׁלֹשִׁים:


מת האב – after thirty days [of fathering a son].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Bekhorot 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Bekhorot/r/8:3:4)
